### [1] [Compound symmetries and double antisymmetry groups in linear time-invariant photonic systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.02902)
*Yunrui Wang,Shiyu Li,Cheng Guo*

Main category: physics.optics

TL;DR: 本文提出了线性光子系统中复合对称性（compound symmetry）的统一框架，利用双反称群（double antisymmetry group）理论将线性时不变光子系统划分为十二种对称类别，并分别给出了各类别对电磁响应的约束，通过光子晶体平板与回旋球体的数值算例验证了该框架的有效性，为光子系统的对称性分析与工程设计提供了系统基础。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 光子系统中外部（空间）对称性与内部对称性（如洛伦兹互易性、能量守恒、时间反演对称性）及其组合而成的复合对称性（以宇称-时间对称性为代表）对电磁响应具有重要约束作用，然而目前尚缺乏统一框架来描述包含互易性、能量守恒与时间反演的广义复合对称性，其分类与物理意义也未得到系统探索。

Method: 本文针对线性光子体系，定义了复合变换与复合对称性，推导了它们对电磁场与散射矩阵的约束条件；引入双反称群理论对内部、外部及复合对称性进行统一描述，据此将线性时不变光子系统划分为十二种对称类别。

Result: 文章展示了十二种对称类别各自的电磁响应特征约束，并以光子晶体平板为例对其中两个代表性类别给出数值演示，同时将该理论应用于回旋球体的基尔霍夫热辐射定律分析。

Conclusion: 该工作为光子系统中对称性的系统分析与工程操控提供了统一的理论基础。

Abstract: Symmetry is fundamental to photonic systems. External (spatial) symmetries and internal symmetries---Lorentz reciprocity, energy conservation, and time-reversal symmetry---constrain the electromagnetic response. Photonic systems can also possess compound symmetries that combine external and internal transformations, exemplified by parity-time (PT) symmetry. However, a unified framework for general compound symmetries involving reciprocity, energy conservation, and time reversal remains lacking, leaving their classification and physical implications unexplored. In this paper, we present such a framework for linear photonic systems. We define compound transformations and symmetries, and derive their constraints on electromagnetic fields and scattering matrices. We show that internal, external, and compound symmetries are naturally described by the theory of double antisymmetry groups. This theory classifies linear time-invariant photonic systems into twelve symmetry categories, each imposing characteristic constraints on the electromagnetic response. We illustrate two representative categories with numerical examples of photonic crystal slabs and apply the theory to examine Kirchhoff's law of thermal radiation for a gyrotropic sphere. Our work provides a systematic foundation for analyzing and engineering symmetry in photonic systems.

</details>


### [2] [AOM-based ultra-low noise laser intensity control up to the MHz range](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.03547)
*Christian Partes,Jonas Auch,Eduard Heidt,Kirill Karpov,Florian Kiesel,Christian Groß*

Main category: physics.optics

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于声光调制器（acousto-optic modulator, AOM）的新型控制系统，通过结合两个标准反馈回路与一个前馈控制支路，并优化AOM晶体内的光束路径，使高功率激光器的相对强度噪声（relative intensity noise）抑制能力扩展至MHz范围，在数百kHz频率处达到低至-155 dB·Hz⁻¹的超低噪声水平。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高功率激光源在多种物理应用中要求具备低相对强度噪声并支持对光功率水平的动态控制，而传统AOM受限于声波传播速度，控制带宽通常仅有数百kHz，难以满足MHz频率范围内噪声抑制的需求。

Method: 提出一种新颖的AOM控制系统，将两个标准反馈回路与一个前馈（feedforward）控制支路相结合，同时对AOM晶体中的光束路径进行优化，以实现高功率激光强度噪声在MHz频段的显著抑制。

Result: 该系统在数百kHz频率处实现了低至-155 dB·Hz⁻¹的超低相对强度噪声水平，将噪声抑制能力从传统AOM的数百kHz上限扩展至MHz范围。

Conclusion: 该成果对于需要在傅里叶频率高达MHz范围内保持超低强度噪声的应用具有重要意义，例如基于超冷轻原子的光学晶格（optical lattice）实验。

Abstract: High-power laser sources that exhibit low relative intensity noise and allow simultaneous dynamic control of their light level are required for a broad range of applications in various fields of physics. Acousto-optic modulators (AOMs) are widely used for active power stabilization and regulation due to their simple drive electronics requirements and high optical power handling capability in free-space. However, the rather slow propagation speed of the sound wave within the AOM crystal typically limits their control bandwidth to a few hundred kHz. In this work, we present a novel AOM-based control system that is capable of significantly suppressing intensity noise of high-power lasers up to the MHz range. By combining two standard feedback loops with one feedforward control branch and optimizing the beam path in the AOM crystal, ultra-low relative intensity noise levels down to $-155\, \text{dB}\,\text{Hz}^{-1}$ even at several hundred kHz are achieved. Our results are relevant for applications that require ultra-low intensity noise at Fourier frequencies up to the MHz range, such as optical lattice experiments with light ultracold atoms.

</details>


### [3] [Self-Focusing Control for Depth-Precise Wafer Slicing of 4H-SiC in Femtosecond Laser Processing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.03814)
*Dong Hee Kang,Jaeseung Lim,Mishfaqur Rahman,Seongheum Han,Jae-Hak Lee,Seungman Kim,Jihoon Jeong*

Main category: physics.optics

TL;DR: 本文系统研究了飞秒激光（femtosecond laser）在4H-SiC晶圆切片中的克尔自聚焦（Kerr-induced self-focusing）行为，通过实验、半经验解析模型和数值光线追踪模拟相结合，建立了脉冲能量与自聚焦深度之间的加工可行性图谱（processability map），该图谱可直接关联激光参数与分离应力及表面纹理指标，为高质量薄片制备提供了超越试错法的实用深度控制指导。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着芯片架构向三维和异构集成方向演进，4H-SiC先进封装要求中间工艺晶圆减薄至100 μm以下以缩短互连并控制热机械应力。飞秒激光切片虽提供低缺陷的非接触加工方案，但强烈的光学非线性效应使激光参数与切片质量之间的关系不明，亟需系统解析。

Method: 结合三方面方法开展研究：(1)飞秒激光4H-SiC切片实验；(2)半经验解析模型——将Marburger公式扩展至聚焦光束，用归一化辐照度（normalized irradiance）替代功率比；(3)数值光线追踪模拟，捕捉自聚焦点的几何特征并与实验观测验证。

Result: 证明了脉冲能量与加工深度的相互作用主导自聚焦行为，该行为直接对应分离后表面纹理参数和分离应力。在此基础上，在脉冲能量与自聚焦深度空间中、以归一化辐照度为背景定义了加工可行性图谱，并得到实验验证。

Conclusion: 在物理定义阈值内，加工可行性图谱可直接建立激光参数与分离应力及表面纹理指标之间的映射关系，为深度控制提供实用的量化指导，避免了传统试错方法。

Abstract: 4H-SiC has emerged as a third-generation chip material because its superior thermal conductivity and high breakdown field enable the material to achieve high power density and higher switching frequencies in power-electronics applications. As chip architectures evolve toward 3D and heterogeneous integration, the mechanical and thermal design space tightens while yield risks grow. In particular, advanced packages require mid-process wafer thinning to < 100 $μ$m to shorten interconnects and control thermo-mechanical stress. Femtosecond laser slicing for 4H-SiC wafers offers a non-contact processing approach to produce thin layers with low defects, while strong optical nonlinearities obscure the relationship between the laser parameters and the resulting slicing quality. Here, we systematically investigate Kerr-induced self-focusing using a femtosecond laser in 4H-SiC slicing by combining experiments, a semi-empirical analytical model, and numerical ray optics simulations. We demonstrate that the interplay between pulse energy and processing depth governs the self-focusing behavior, which directly correlates with post-separation surface texture parameters and separation stress, thereby linking nonlinear beam propagation to slicing quality. Based on this relationship, we define a processability map in the pulse energy with self-focusing depth space over a normalized irradiance background. Analytically, the model extends the Marburger formula to focused beams by replacing the power ratio with a normalized irradiance. Ray optics simulations capture the geometric features at the self-focusing point and are validated against experimental observations. Within physically defined thresholds, the processability map directly connects laser parameters to separation stress and surface texture metrics, providing practical guidance for depth control beyond trial-and-error.

</details>

### [4] [Topologically Charged Morris-Thorne-type Wormholes and the Energy Conditions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.02652)
*Faizuddin Ahmed,Md Sabir Ali,Adnan Malik*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文通过求解含各向异性流体（anisotropic fluid）能动张量的爱因斯坦场方程，研究拓扑荷（topological charge）影响下的 Morris-Thorne 型可穿越虫洞（traversable wormhole），对七种形状函数（shape function）逐一分析能量条件与各向异性参数，发现能量密度始终为正，但部分能量条件被违反。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 延续此前工作（Eur. Phys. J C 84 (2024) 1037），旨在系统探究拓扑荷在可穿越虫洞几何中对能量条件的影响机制，并判断虫洞结构表现出吸引还是排斥行为。

Method: 以各向异性流体作为能动张量求解爱因斯坦场方程，对七类形状函数（如指数型、幂律型、双曲型等）逐一构造虫洞解，并分别检验零、弱、强和主导四种能量条件，同时计算各向异性参数（anisotropy parameter）以判定几何的吸引或排斥性质。

Result: 分析表明，各向异性流体的能量密度始终为正；但对所考察的形状函数而言，能量条件中只有部分被满足，其余则被违反，说明拓扑荷对能量条件的满足程度具有调控作用。

Conclusion: 拓扑荷可显著影响虫洞解中能量条件的成立情况，各向异性流体能量密度为正，但可穿越虫洞所需的某些能量条件仍不可避免地受到违反。

Abstract: In this paper, we investigate topologically charged Morris-Thorne-type traversable wormholes by solving the Einstein field equations with an anisotropic fluid as the energy-momentum tensor and analysing the resulting solutions. In continuation to the earlier work (Eur. Phys. J C {\bf 84} (2024) 1037), we consider the shape functions such as: (i) $A(r)=r_0\,e^{r_0-r}$; (ii) $A(r)=r_0\,a^r/a^{r_0}$,\quad $0 < a<1$; (iii) $A(r)=r_0\,\left(\frac{\cosh r_0}{\cosh r}\right)^δ$,\quad $δ\geq 1$; (iv) $A(r)=\frac{1}{r}+\ln\!\frac{r}{r_0}$; (v) $A(r)=B\,r^n+(1-B)$; (vi) $A(r)=r_0\,\frac{\mbox{ln} (1+r)}{\mbox{ln} (1+r_0)}$, (vii) $A(r)=r_0+a\,r_0\,\left[\left(\frac{r}{r_0}\right)^β-1\right]$, where $β<1$ and $0 < a\,β<1$. We examine the energy conditions-namely, null, weak, strong, and dominant energy conditions and explore how topological charge influences or controls these conditions. Additionally, we calculate the anisotropy parameter to determine whether the wormhole geometry exhibits attractive or repulsive behavior. Our analysis demonstrates that the energy density of the anisotropic fluid is always positive. However, while some of the energy conditions are partially satisfied, others are violated.

</details>


### [5] [Novel Observable Signals from First-Order Gravitational Phase Transitions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.02736)
*Murat Özinan,Oğuzhan K. Yamak,Fethi M. Ramazanoğlu*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 研究发现自发标量化（spontaneous scalarization）在许多天体物理相关场景中以不连续（一级）相变方式发生，从而产生更强烈的可观测信号，例如中子星吸积导致的引力波呼吸模式，为引力理论提供新的检验途径。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自发标量化是标量-张量理论中的著名现象，表现为对广义相对论的大偏差，但其信号通常难以探测，因为该现象一般被视为连续相变进行研究。作者希望探索是否存在更易探测的标量化信号。

Method: 作者通过理论分析论证标量化在多种天体物理相关场景中以不连续（一级）相变方式发生，并以中子星因吸积发生标量化所产生的引力波呼吸模式为例进行演示，同时讨论了其他可能的场景。

Result: 研究结果表明，标量化在许多天体物理相关场景中表现为一级（不连续）相变，而非传统的连续相变。这种不连续转变会产生"响亮"的信号，为引力理论提供新的检验手段，具体示例为中子星吸积触发标量化时产生的引力波呼吸模式。

Conclusion: 一级标量化相变带来的强烈信号能够克服传统连续标量化信号难以探测的困难，为检验引力理论开辟了新的观测途径。

Abstract: Spontaneous scalarization is a well-known phenomenon in scalar-tensor theories, featuring large deviations from general relativity. However, some of its signatures are hard to detect, because it is generally studied as a continuous phase transition. We show that scalarization occurs in a discontinuous (first-order) manner in many astrophysically relevant scenarios. This leads to loud signals that provide new tests of gravity. We demonstrate this in the case of gravitational wave breathing modes from a neutron star that scalarizes due to accretion, and discuss other possibilities.

</details>


### [6] [Determination of the Angular Momentum of Radiated Gravitons, Scalars, and Dark Photons from Binary Orbits](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.02742)
*Arpan Hait,Subhendra Mohanty*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 该论文利用场论方法（field-theoretic approach）计算致密双星系统通过引力波、标量场和矢量场辐射的能量与角动量。研究发现每个发射的引力子携带约2ħ的角动量（适用于椭圆轨道和双曲轨道），而标量和矢量辐射中每个量子携带约ħ的角动量。同时，辐射能量和角动量可由准开普勒轨道的轨道角频率和偏心率演化确定，但无法对发射粒子所携带的角动量进行规范不变的轨道与自旋分量分解。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 此前已有研究指出椭圆轨道上每个引力子携带约2ħ角动量，但双曲轨道情形尚未得到验证；同时标量和矢量场辐射中每个量子所携带的角动量也缺乏系统分析。

Method: 采用场论方法计算致密双星在引力波、标量场和矢量场中的能量和角动量辐射率，通过比值$\dot{J}/\dot{N}$分析每个发射量子携带的平均角动量，并利用准开普勒轨道的演化方程建立辐射量与轨道参数之间的联系。

Result: 每个引力子携带的角动量为$\dot{J}/\dot{N}\simeq 2\hbar$，该结论不仅适用于椭圆轨道，也适用于双曲轨道；每个标量或矢量辐射量子携带的角动量约为$\hbar$；辐射能量和角动量可由轨道角频率和偏心率演化导出；但发射粒子的角动量无法实现规范不变（gauge-invariant）的轨道与自旋分解。

Conclusion: 该工作将每个引力子携带2ħ角动量的结论推广到双曲轨道，并系统给出了标量和矢量辐射的对应规律，深化了对致密双星多通道辐射中角动量携带机制的理解，同时指出角动量轨道-自旋分解的固有局限性。

Abstract: We compute the energy and angular momentum radiated by compact binaries through gravitational waves, scalar and vector fields, using the field-theoretic approach. We observe that each emitted graviton carries away an angular momentum of $\dot{J}/\dot {N}\simeq 2\hbar$, not only for elliptical orbits (as has already been pointed out by Page [arXiv:2409.00305]) but also for hyperbolic orbits. We extend the analysis to scalar and vector radiation and find that the angular momentum carried by each emitted quantum of radiation is approximately $\hbar$, i.e., $\dot{J}/\dot{N}\simeq \hbar$ for both types of radiation. We demonstrate that the radiated energy and angular momentum of binary systems can be determined from the evolution of the orbital angular frequency and the eccentricity of the quasi-Kepler orbit. However, a gauge-invariant decomposition of the angular momentum carried by the emitted particles into the spin and orbital components is not possible.

</details>


### [7] [On Modeling Anisotropic Quark Stars: The Role of Anisotropy in Radial Oscillation Spectra](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.02761)
*G. Panotopoulos*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文对已知质量和半径的致密天体 Cen X-3（被认为是好的奇异夸克星（strange quark star）候选体）建立了包含负各向异性因子（negative anisotropic factor）的模型，计算了前十个最低径向振荡模（radial oscillation modes）的频率，并以三种不同方式引入各向异性，研究其对振荡谱的影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Cen X-3 是已知质量和半径的致密天体，具有作为奇异夸克星良好候选体的研究价值。以往对其振荡谱的研究可能未考虑各向异性压强（anisotropic pressure），而各向异性可能对致密天体的振动特性产生重要影响，值得系统考察。

Method: 对 Cen X-3 建立包含负各向异性因子的模型，采用三种不同的方式引入各向异性（anisotropy），并计算前十个最低径向振荡模的频率，以比较不同各向异性引入方式对振荡谱（oscillation spectrum）的影响。

Result: 研究得到了 Cen X-3 在前十个最低径向振荡模下的频率谱，并展示了三种不同各向异性引入方式对该振荡谱的具体影响差异。

Conclusion: 各向异性因子对奇异夸克星候选体 Cen X-3 的径向振荡谱具有显著影响，且影响程度依赖于各向异性在模型中的引入方式；这为通过振荡观测约束天体内部结构与各向异性性质提供了理论依据。

Abstract: We model the compact object Cen X-3, which is considered to be a good strange quark star candidate of known mass and radius, incorporating a negative anisotropic factor, and we compute the frequencies of the ten lowest radial oscillation modes. We introduce the anisotropy in three different manners and investigate its impact on the spectra.

</details>


### [8] [Dark Matter Signatures in Black Hole Thermodynamics and Information Recovery](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.02767)
*Yahya Ladghami,Brahim Asfour,Taoufik Ouali*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文研究完美流体暗物质（perfect fluid dark matter）包裹下的Schwarzschild和Reissner-Nordström黑洞的热力学性质与信息恢复问题，发现暗物质提高Hawking温度、加速蒸发并缩短寿命，同时通过岛公式（island formula）恢复Page曲线，且暗物质的存在缩短Page时间，加速信息恢复。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 旨在探究暗物质环境对黑洞热力学性质的影响，并利用岛公式解决黑洞信息丢失悖论（black hole information loss paradox），考察暗物质是否影响信息恢复过程。

Method: 采用岛公式计算Hawking辐射的纠缠熵（entanglement entropy），结合黑洞热力学分析（Hawking温度、相结构、蒸发时间），并推导Page时间的解析表达式，建立热力学与信息恢复之间的对应关系。

Abstract: We investigate the conservative dynamics of relativistic charged particles in a Schwarzschild spacetime threaded by an externally supported dipolar magnetic field. The gravitational sector is treated exactly in isotropic coordinates, while the electromagnetic field is described by the leading asymptotic term of the relativistic exterior dipole solution. This formulation provides an analytically tractable relativistic extension of the classical Størmer problem and allows the circular-orbit and stability structure to be studied explicitly. For the outward-Lorentz branch, characterized by a positive signed magnetic coupling for positive azimuthal motion, we find a non-trivial competition between radial and vertical stability. Weak magnetic coupling shifts the radially marginal orbit inward, whereas at stronger coupling vertical instability becomes the limiting mechanism and the inner boundary of stable circular motion moves outward. The associated vertical epicyclic mode progressively softens and vanishes at the vertical stability boundary. The marginally bound sequence exhibits a distinct strong-coupling behaviour, illustrating that energetic and stability boundaries need not evolve together. The radial and vertical epicyclic frequencies also generate characteristic commensurabilities that may provide natural sites for nonlinear orbital coupling. Because the magnetic field is treated as a test field, these charged-particle effects leave the vacuum Schwarzschild null-geodesic structure unchanged. Magnetic signatures in compact-object observations must therefore arise through the dynamics and radiation of charged matter, plasma propagation effects, or physics beyond the test-field approximation. The framework developed here provides an analytical basis for studying nonlinear dynamics, radiation reaction, and more realistic compact-object magnetospheres.

We consider collapse models describing the formation of charged and regular black holes and compute the renormalized stress-energy tensor of the $|\textit{in}\rangle$ vacuum state. We show that, within this framework, the emergence of an anti-trapped region is a generic consequence of the amplification of negative energy fluxes propagating along the outgoing direction inside the initial trapped region. Our analysis further suggests that the fluxes generated by the subsequent anti-trapped region, now propagating along the ingoing direction, may trigger the formation of a new trapped region. This raises the possibility of a cascade of black-to-white-hole transitions, potentially ending in a horizon-free, bouncing spacetime without invoking additional quantum-gravitational dynamics. Although establishing the complete evolution requires a self-consistent treatment of semiclassical backreaction, our framework identifies which features of the mechanism are universal and which depend on the geometry, laying the groundwork for a systematic investigation of semiclassical black-hole-to-white-hole transitions.

Abstract: $Λ$CDM provides a remarkably successful description of the Universe, yet it suffers from persistent discrepancies, with one of the most prominent being the $σ_8$ tension between early-Universe Cosmic Microwave Background (CMB) measurements and late-time structure growth probes. To address this mismatch, we investigate generalized interacting dark sector models governed by a quintessence field coupled to dark matter via conformal and disformal transformations. By generically engineering a quintessence potential that ensures the combined evolution of dark matter and dark energy exactly mimics $Λ$CDM, our models satisfy all background constraints while signaling modified gravity effects within the linear perturbation sector. Our results indicate that purely conformal couplings efficiently transfer energy from dark matter to the quintessence field, suppressing late-time structure formation and successfully accommodating CMB data within late-time modified structure growth. This purely conformal limit yields a strong statistical preference over the $Λ$CDM baseline according to both the Akaike and Bayesian Information Criteria. Conversely, while non-zero disformal couplings introduce a kinetic friction that dampens the dark sector energy exchange, their expanded parameter space incurs heavy statistical penalties without providing a decisive improvement over the purely conformal scenario.

Abstract: Inclusive jet production is measured with the ATLAS detector at the LHC at $\sqrt{s_{\mathrm{NN}}}=5.02$ TeV in Pb+Pb and $pp$ collisions, using data samples corresponding to integrated luminosities of 1.72 nb$^{-1}$ and 255 pb$^{-1}$, respectively.   <summary>Details</summary>

Method: 在分布（distributions）意义下表述时变麦克斯韦方程，对介电常数（permittivity）和磁导率（permeability）给出显式的迹与正则性假设。在此基础上推导时间不连续和后续空间界面产生的跳变条件，利用这些条件构造波矢的广义斯涅尔定律，并将振幅方程整理为时空板条几何下的有限维线性系统，最后给出斜入射（oblique incidence）的显式算例。

Result: 得到了时间分裂、反射和透射所产生波矢的广义斯涅尔定律，推导出对应的振幅方程并以有限维线性系统形式组织；提供了斜入射情形的显式示例。整套方法不依赖光滑场假设，且允许材料参数在界面之外不必为常数。

Conclusion: 所提出的方法为电磁波穿越时间和空间材料界面的传播问题提供了更一般的处理框架，适用范围不限于常数材料参数或光滑场情形。

Abstract: We study the time-dependent Maxwell system, formulated in the sense of distributions, for electromagnetic waves propagating through media with temporal and spatial material interfaces. Under explicit trace and regularity assumptions on the permittivity and permeability, we derive the jump conditions produced by temporal discontinuities and by subsequent spatial interfaces. These conditions are used to obtain generalized Snell laws for the wave vectors generated by temporal splitting, reflection, and transmission. We also derive the associated amplitude equations and organize them as a finite-dimensional linear system for a space-time slab geometry. Finally, we provide an explicit oblique-incidence example. Our approach does not impose a smooth-field ansatz and allows material parameters that need not be constant away from the interfaces.

</details>

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 平滑动力学的振荡器同步行为已被Kuramoto等连续相位模型很好地描述，但这些模型无法无缝适用于含有离散事件的混合振荡器，导致其同步与锁相机理长期被忽视。

Method: 作者综合运用实验、理论与模拟三种手段：以接触充电电泳振荡器作为实验实现，并提出一个显式计入离散事件的离散时间框架来刻画振荡器的锁相行为，识别了惯性（inertia）与相互作用强度沿振荡周期的衰减在促进锁相中的作用。

Result: 实验表明混合振荡器可在相互排斥的情况下实现同相振荡；所提出的离散时间框架能够合理化该行为，并揭示多种机制均可导致这类系统的相干运动。

Conclusion: 该工作提供了一个适用于更广泛耦合混合振荡器类别的锁相判据，证明了混合振荡器存在多种可导致相干性的机制，弥补了连续相位模型在该场景下的不足。

Abstract: The synchronization and phase-locking behavior of oscillators with smooth dynamics is well captured by continuous phase-models à la Kuramoto. However, these models do not apply seamlessly for hybrid oscillators, where discrete events occur along their otherwise smooth dynamics. Consequently, the synchronization and phase-locking mechanisms of hybrid oscillators remain overlooked. Here, we combine experiments, theory, and simulations, to investigate and rationalize the coherent motion of pairs of hybrid oscillator. Using contact-charge electrophoretic (CCEP) oscillators as an experimental realization, we show that in-phase oscillations can occur, despite oscillators repelling each other. We rationalize this behavior by introducing a discrete-time framework that explicitly accounts for discrete events and elucidates the origin of phase-locking. Our model highlights the roles played by inertia and the decay of interaction strength along the oscillation cycle in promoting phase-locking of CCEP oscillators. More generally, it provides a phase-locking criterion that holds for a broader class of coupled hybrid oscillators, and reveals that various mechanisms can lead to their coherence.

  <summary>Details</summary>

Method: 从主方程（master equation）出发，推导粒子密度和极化场的平均场演化方程，并通过von Mises假设封闭所得层级，得到耦合的水动力描述；随后将理论应用于Active Brownian Bug模型并进行线性稳定性分析。

Conclusion: 该框架为增殖活性物质建立了通用的连续介质理论，揭示了活性与种群动力学相互作用驱动集体运动的物理机制，为没有显式对齐相互作用时涌现成群行为提供了物理解释。

Abstract: We develop a continuum theory for proliferating active matter starting from a microscopic stochastic model of self-propelled particles undergoing birth, death, and nonlocal competition. Beginning from the master equation, we derive mean-field evolution equations for the particle density and polarization fields and close the resulting hierarchy through a von Mises ansatz, providing a coupled hydrodynamic description applicable to a broad class of proliferating active systems. As an application, we study the recently introduced Active Brownian Bug model, in which the form of flocking emerges despite the absence of explicit alignment interactions. Linear stability analysis predicts both Turing and Hopf instabilities, whose analytical thresholds agree with numerical simulations. The continuum model reproduces the principal dynamical regimes of the underlying particle system, including homogeneous states, stationary periodic clusters, and coherently propagating flocking states. These results establish a general continuum framework for proliferating active matter and provide a physical interpretation of collective motion driven by the interplay between activity and population dynamics.

</details>

TL;DR: 本文综述了黏着斑（focal adhesions, FAs）在黏弹性基底上的活性润湿与去润湿行为，并提出一个双时间尺度物理框架来统一解释应力松弛、力传递与黏附重塑之间的耦合关系。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 虽然已知基底应力松弛（stress relaxation）可调控黏附稳定性与细胞迁移，但缺乏一个将黏弹性与力传递及黏附动力学联系起来的预测性物理框架，本文旨在填补这一空白。

Method: 作者综合现有实验与理论研究，构建了一个双时间尺度物理框架：在短时间尺度上，肌动蛋白驱动（actomyosin-driven）的振荡位移通过分子离合器（molecular clutches）传递，产生频率依赖的能量转移，并在弹性储能与黏性耗散平衡处达到最优频率，建立共振类似机制；在长时间尺度上，该力学最优态通过有效表面张力（effective surface tension）耦联到黏附重塑，将FA的生长与解聚解释为活性润湿与去润湿过程。

Result: 模型预测黏附稳定性与稳态尺寸由基底刚度、黏弹性时间尺度以及由Piezo1依赖性钙信号介导的机械敏感反馈共同调控。能量传递在最优频率处最大化，同时决定了有效FA刚度和牵引力振幅。

Conclusion: 该双时间尺度框架为理解细胞在黏弹性环境中的黏附动力学提供系统性的预测工具，将力传递的物理机制与黏附重塑的生物学过程统一起来。

Abstract: Cell adhesion to viscoelastic substrates is mediated by focal adhesions (FAs), which dynamically couple actomyosin contractility to the extracellular matrix. Although substrate stress relaxation is known to regulate adhesion stability and cell migration, a predictive physical framework linking viscoelasticity to force transmission and adhesion dynamics remains lacking. Here we review briefly what is known about the active wetting and de-wetting of FAs on viscoelastic substrates and synthesize existing experimental and theoretical work into a two-timescale physical framework to describe the phenomena reported. At short timescales, oscillatory actomyosin-driven displacements are transmitted through molecular clutches, leading to frequency-dependent energy transfer to the substrate. We show that this transfer is maximized at an optimal frequency set by a balance between elastic energy storage and viscous dissipation, establishing a resonance-like mechanism that selects both the effective FA stiffness and traction force amplitude. At longer timescales, this mechanically optimal state couples to adhesion remodelling through an effective surface tension, enabling FA growth and disassembly to be interpreted as active wetting and de-wetting processes. The model predicts that adhesion stability and steady-state size are controlled by substrate stiffness and viscoelastic timescales, as well as mechanosensitive feedback mediated by Piezo1-dependent calcium signalling.

</details>

Abstract: Motility-induced phase separation occurs in assemblies of self-propelled units when activity is coupled negatively to density. In contrast, the consequences of a positive coupling between density and activity on the collective behaviour of active matter remain unexplored. Here, we show that collective activity can emerge from such a positive coupling among non-motile building blocks. We perform experiments with self-sustained oscillators powered by contact-charge electrophoresis. Although the oscillators are non-motile by design, they spontaneously form an active gas when confined together. The super-elastic nature of collisions constitutes a positive density-activity coupling and underlies the active gas properties. Elucidating the origin of binary collisions allows us to precisely control the structure of the active gas and its eventual crystallization. Beyond considering the overlooked positive coupling between density and activity, our work suggests that rich collective properties can emerge not only from the symmetry of interactions between active building blocks, but also from their adaptable and responsive behaviour.

*Biman Bagchi*

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 螺旋结构在生物与合成聚合物中极为常见，但其形成不仅依赖局域构象偏好，还涉及成核、生长、抗断裂及保持恒定手性等全局过程。现有理论难以将手性保持与螺旋-线圈协同性分开处理，亟需一个能够区分这两类物理机制的形式化框架。

Method: 构建三态Ising型转移矩阵理论，每个链段具有线圈态（coil-like）、右手螺旋态和左手螺旋态三种状态。该表述将普通螺旋-线圈协同性与手性保持分开处理，并通过左右对称性将问题分解为对称与反对称两部分，从而获得两个特征长度公式。

Result: 在强螺旋稀壁极限（strongly helical rare-wall limit）下，手性持续长度满足ξχ≃(1/2)exp[β(K+J)]。由此表明，源于Ramachandran图谱的局域手性偏置（local chiral bias）可在有限螺旋域内被放大。

Conclusion: 该三态转移矩阵理论成功将螺旋形成中"是否形成螺旋"与"保持何种手性"两种物理过程解耦，为理解聚合物螺旋的手性稳定性及其对热涨落的响应提供了新的理论框架。

Abstract: Helices are among the most common ordered structures in biological and synthetic polymers, but their formation involves more than local conformational preference. A finite helix must nucleate, grow, resist breaking, and maintain a selected handedness against thermal fluctuations. We develop a three-state Ising-like transfer-matrix theory in which each segment is coil-like, right-handed helical, or left-handed helical. This formulation separates ordinary helix--coil cooperativity from the persistence of handedness. The right--left symmetric problem decomposes into symmetric and antisymmetric sectors, giving two characteristic lengths: the helical correlation length $ξ_H$ and the chiral persistence length $ξ_χ$. In the strongly helical rare-wall limit, $ξ_χ\simeq (1/2)\exp[β(K+J)]$. A local chiral bias, motivated by the Ramachandran landscape, is then amplified over finite helical domains.

</details>

Abstract: Developing synthetic materials that exhibit life-like behavior, such as internally driven cycles, remains a central challenge in active matter. This bio-inspired, paracrine-like signaling mechanism generates context- and history-dependent many-body interactions that break time-reversal symmetry and drive the system far from equilibrium, leading to the spontaneous emergence of autonomous, internally sustained limit cycles in the composition of particle clusters. Using computer simulations, we map the resulting nonequilibrium phase behavior and identify distinct dynamical regimes controlled by the rates of signal production and degradation, together with the diffusion range of the signaling molecules. Among these, we find a robust oscillatory state in which particle clusters autonomously assemble in a cyclic fashion, driven entirely by internal feedback loops. Our results establish paracrine-signaling colloids as a minimal, physically realizable platform for programmable nonequilibrium materials with life-like functionality and provide a general route toward synthetic active matter with self-regulated collective dynamics.

Abstract: The motility of microswimmers in confined environments is a fundamental problem in active matter physics, with direct implications for microfluidic applications and the understanding of microorganism behavior in complex natural habitats. In circular cavities with diameter below the persistence length L_0 ~350 microns, cells transition from bulk active Brownian exploration to quasi-circular wall-following trajectories. In dumbbell geometries, inter-compartment dwell length reflect purely geometric predictions, evidencing no measurable hydrodynamic contributions even for strong confinements. Together, these results demonstrate that environmental geometry can selectively amplify or suppress motility modes in active biological suspensions, opening avenues for the passive control of microswimmer transport in engineered microfluidic networks.

Abstract: The centre-mode elastic instability, prevalent in rectilinear flows of dilute polymer solutions, allows dynamic states to emerge even in the absence of inertia. Here, we show that this instability can be significantly enhanced when the base flow has a nonuniform spatial-distribution of polymers. Specifically, we consider a polymer-laden stream sandwiched between streams of pure solvent. In such a flow, the polymeric stress not only depends on the conformation tensor (determined here by the Oldroyd-B equation) but also varies proportionally with the polymer concentration field, which satisfies a scalar transport equation. We consider the Stokes limit and first focus on the simple setting of periodic Kolmogorov flow. A linear stability analysis shows that the centre-mode instability is strongly promoted when the base flow has polymers localized near the maximum of the base-velocity profile; concentrating polymers near the maximum shear suppresses the instability. As the polymer loading is increased, the neutral stability curve of the nonuniform system develops a double-lobe form, which results in a sudden decrease in the critical Weissenberg number (product of the elastic relaxation time and the typical strain-rate). An energy analysis attributes this destabilization to elastic feedback forces arising from gradients in the polymer concentration. We end by demonstrating the relevance of these findings to channel flow, where localizing polymers about the centreline is shown to strongly promote the centre-mode instability.

Result: 获得了覆盖四个标度区域、十八个KPZ类可解模型的显式闭合矩阵微分-差分方程；验证了着装过程保持菱形方程使得矩阵可观测量与初始种子数据遵循相同的非线性结构；恢复了作者前期工作中的单点双线性方程作为特例；证明了菱形方程与非阿贝尔Hirota-Miwa系统之间是规范等价的。

Conclusion: 该框架为KPZ普适类精确可解模型提供了统一的代数描述，将验证闭合非线性方程的问题归结为对核数据的有限线性条件检查，并通过与经典可积性理论中的Hirota-Miwa系统的联系确立了框架的理论根基。

Abstract: This work introduces an algebraic framework yielding explicit, closed matrix differential-difference equations for eighteen models in the exactly solvable sector of the KPZ universality class across four scaling regimes. By organizing Fredholm determinant data into an overdetermined linear problem on a directed lattice graph, we derive a compatibility system termed the diamond equations. Elementary seed data extracted from the shift structure of the Fredholm kernel provides simple solutions to this system.
  We then construct a Darboux transformation to compress the infinite-dimensional Fredholm data into a finite-dimensional matrix observable. We show this dressing procedure preserves the diamond equations; consequently the resulting matrix observable obeys the same nonlinear structure as the initial seed data. Verifying a closed nonlinear equation for any specific model thus reduces to checking a handful of linear conditions on its kernel data.
  Under a scalar reduction, the framework produces variable-coefficient Hirota-Miwa equations for Fredholm determinants, recovering the one-point bilinear equations of the author's earlier work as specializations. To supply the necessary seed data, a product graph construction with admissible propagators builds multipoint data in the fully discrete regime, while Euclidean division in a polynomial quotient algebra handles vertex and polymer models. Finally, we demonstrate the diamond equations are a gauge-equivalent reparametrization of the non-abelian Hirota-Miwa system, a central system in classical integrability theory.

</details>

Abstract: We establish a weak mesoscopic local law and quantitative eigenvector-delocalization estimates for the SYK Hamiltonian. For even $q\ll N^{\frac{1}{2}}$, we prove that the normalized Stieltjes transform converges uniformly on bounded energy intervals to that of the standard Gaussian law, down to scales of order $qN^{-\frac{1}{2}}$, up to logarithmic factors. The result holds both on the full Hilbert space and in each fermion-parity sector. We derive mesoscopic eigenvalue counting and spectral form factor estimates, as well as an averaged inverse participation ratio bound. For fixed $q$, we further obtain high-probability $\ell^\infty$-delocalization bounds in any deterministic orthonormal basis for individual bulk eigenvectors.

Abstract: We show that the computational complexity of Reshetikhin-Turaev invariants of simply colored links is preserved when their underlying ribbon fusion categories are related by the zesting construction. Zesting modifies an $A$-graded ribbon fusion category $\mathcal{C}$ with additional algebraic data $ζ$ to produce a new category $\mathcal{C}^ζ$ whose link invariants are known to differ from those of $\mathcal{C}$ by an invariant of $A$-colored links $\mathcal{J}_ζ$ depending only on $ζ$. Conclusion: 金属-水界面而非气-水界面是羟基自由基产生的反应位点；电极材料可预测电生微气泡在鲁米诺溶液中是否产生CL。该发现对认为羟基自由基在气-水界面自发形成的流行观点提出了直接质疑。

Therefore, this work affords electrode-based predictions of whether or not electrogenerated microbubbles would yield CL in luminol solutions and calls into question the notion of spontaneous formation of hydroxyl radicals at gas-water interfaces.

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 该研究旨在搜索正负电子对撞中产生C偶（C-even）共振f₁(1285)的反应。由于f₁(1285)是C偶共振，其不能通过单光子过程直接产生，只能经由双光子（two-photon）相互作用产生，因此该反应对理解共振结构与量子电动力学相互作用具有独特物理意义。

Method: 使用VEPP-2000对撞机上的CMD-3探测器进行实验。在质心能量（center-of-mass energy）1.20—1.36 GeV范围内扫描数据，积分亮度为109.1 pb⁻¹；另外在f₁(1285)共振峰能量1282 MeV处专门采集了51.7 pb⁻¹的专用数据，通过e⁺e⁻→ηπ⁰π⁰衰变道进行信号探测。

Result: 在共振峰能量处从e⁺e⁻→ηπ⁰π⁰反应中发现26.5±7.4个事件，对应4.5σ的证据强度，表明检测到C偶共振在e⁺e⁻→f₁(1285)→ηπ⁰π⁰反应中的产生。产生截面为σ(e⁺e⁻→f₁)=0.074±0.021±0.010 nb，对应分支比为B(f₁(1285)→e⁺e⁻)=(8.3±2.3±1.1)×10⁻⁹，而扫描数据中未观察到信号迹象。

Conclusion: 该实验提供了4.5σ证据，表明f₁(1285)共振可在e⁺e⁻对撞中经双光子相互作用产生，并首次测定了f₁(1285)→e⁺e⁻的分支比，为后续相关实验和理论计算提供了基准数据。

Abstract: A search for the $e^+ e^-\to f_1(1285)$ reaction is performed with the CMD-3 detector at the VEPP-2000 collider. Data from a scan in the center-of-mass energy range Ec.m.=1.20--1.36 GeV with 109.1 pb$^{-1}$ of the integrated luminosity is used. A dedicated 51.7 pb$^{-1}$ have been collected at the $f_1(1285)$ resonance peak energy Ec.m.=1282 MeV, where $26.5 \pm 7.4$ events have been found in the $e^+ e^-\toηπ^0π^0$ reaction. No indication of the signal is observed in the scan data. We interpret the result as 4.5$σ$ evidence of the C-even resonance production in the $e^+ e^-\to f_1(1285)\toηπ^0π^0$ reaction via the two-photon interaction. The production cross section is found to be $σ(e^+ e^-\to f_1) = 0.074 \pm 0.021 \pm 0.010$ nb, and the corresponding branching fraction to the $e^+ e^-$ pair is $B(f_1(1285)\to e^+e^-) = (8.3 \pm 2.3 \pm 1.1)\times 10^{-9}$.

</details>

Abstract: Using proton-proton collision data corresponding to an integrated luminosity of $9 \,\textrm{fb}^{-1}$ collected by the LHCb experiment, searches are performed for $B^+_c$ mesons decaying to a charm and a charmless meson pair. What is added here is the organization over the entire face poset: the Hamiltonian defect on a face is the sum of the pullbacks of the primitives on its codimension-one faces, weighted by the orientation incidence numbers, so that the boundary term of the single-stratum identity is resolved into its connected pieces with signs.

We verify all four codimension-two cancellations explicitly for four-dimensional BF theory on $M=Γ\times[0,1]^2$.

If a raw codimension-two descendant is presymplectic and its reduced Dirac structure is the graph of a Poisson bivector, the shifted cotangent construction gives a canonical strict degree-two corner theory. The passage from a reduced Poisson bivector to a strict corner theory is already recorded in \cite{CFT2026}; what is isolated here is the hypothesis under which it applies, and its relation to the face-incidence structure. The construction provides a rigorous ordinary-corner benchmark for extensions of AKSZ descent to Joyce generalized corners.

For the nested sums we consider nested harmonic sums, generalized harmonic sums, nested sums implied by quadratic forms, cyclotomic harmonic sums, and nested sums containing central binomials. These functions are expected to play a role in perturbative calculations in the case of $q$-deformed commutation relations. For the simpler function spaces closed form solutions are presented. For more involved alphabets we present the algorithmic steps leading to the $q$-extension for the individual cases. We also derive the determining differential and difference equations of these higher transcendental functions. The $q$-extended special functions are quite different form the corresponding $μ$-extended functions.

Given an integer $R\ge1$, consider a function $g:Δ_{nR}^{(n)}\to\mathbb R$ satisfying the complete oriented-simplex relations \[ \sum_{i=1}^n g(β+e_i)=0, \qquad β\inΔ_{nR-1}^{(n)}, \] where $e_i$ is the $i$th standard basis vector. We prove that a nonzero value at the balanced point forces the support-cardinality estimate with optimal growth exponent: if $g(R,\ldots,R)\neq 0$, then $|\operatorname{supp}(g)|\ge c_n R^{\lceil n/2\rceil}$. Abstract: We study a Purcell three-link microswimmer whose joints are \emph{odd-elastic}: the torsional stiffness is non-Hermitian, its antisymmetric part $k_o$ injecting mechanical work so that the internal elastic drift is non-conservative. Our main finding is a sharp separation between geometry and cost --- odd elasticity is invisible to the control geometry of the swimmer and visible only in the energy of a manoeuvre. The mechanism is a single algebraic fact: the drift lies in the span of the two control vector fields, so the system is feedback-equivalent to a driftless one and the odd modulus enters the bracket structure only through the scalar $\det\textbf{K}=k^2+k_o^2$. The odd modulus acts solely on the cost: casting the optimal-steering problem in sub-Finsler (Randers) form, we prove that a prescribed reorientation is strictly cheaper for either sign of $k_o$. Abstract: We present an advancement for solving the Discretized Light-Cone Quantization (DLCQ) Hamiltonian for mass spectra in 2D $φ^4$ theory that incorporates perturbative zero-mode contributions. Abstract: We study the minimal classically scale-invariant extension of the Standard Model, containing a single $\mathbb Z_2$-stabilised real scalar singlet whose mass is not an independent input but is generated dynamically through the quantum effective potential and linked to radiative electroweak symmetry breaking through the Higgs portal. We construct the full two-field one-loop effective potential and introduce a modified on-shell renormalisation scheme that fixes the electroweak vacuum, the Higgs mass, the vanishing Higgs--singlet mixing and the singlet curvature at the physical point, yielding predictions stable under renormalisation-scale variation. Abstract: String compactifications generically produce ${\cal O}(100)$ light axion-like particles, yet low-energy mixing has not previously been exploited as a structuring constraint on their spectrum. We show that the requirement of well-defined QCD-induced mass mixing forces the axion masses into a hierarchically spaced ordering, while stochastic mixing organizes the decay constants into a hierarchically split distribution -- two populations separated by an essentially empty window. Both patterns are insensitive to ultraviolet input and satisfy the relevant Swampland constraints within standard large-volume compactifications. We term this emergent pattern the hierarchical axiverse.

</details>

Conclusion: 所提出的数值-解析混合框架能够有效处理高复杂度两圈振幅，为$t\bar{t}W$过程NNLO QCD修正的完整计算提供了关键输入，并验证了该方法的可靠性与交叉一致性。

Abstract: We present a numerical computation of the two-loop QCD scattering amplitudes for the production of a top-antitop quark pair in association with a $W$ boson ($t\bar{t}W$) at the LHC in the generalised leading-colour approximation, retaining the exact dependence on the top-quark and $W$-boson masses. Rather than pursuing a fully analytic calculation, we employ a hybrid framework that combines numerical evaluation with strong algebraic and analytic control, allowing ultraviolet and infrared singularities as well as large intermediate cancellations to be treated exactly. This is achieved by expressing the finite remainder in terms of a set of special functions with rational coefficients. The special functions are evaluated numerically by solving differential equations through power-series expansions, while the values of the rational coefficients are reconstructed, point by point, from finite-field evaluations. The calculation is performed in the 't Hooft-Veltman scheme and validated against an independent implementation in conventional dimensional regularisation employing a substantially different computational strategy. We finally provide the colour- and polarisation-summed hard functions evaluated on the phase-space grid used in a previous computation of the next-to-next-to-leading-order QCD corrections to the $t\bar{t}W$ cross section.

Conclusion: 算子大小是连接有限N谱与大N涌现引力物理的关键桥梁，为理解全息对偶（holographic duality）及其相变提供了新的微观视角。

These results reveal operator size as the bridge between the finite-$N$ spectrum and the emergent gravitational physics at large $N$.

Under the ordinary block-diagonal vacuum--continuum ansatz, the same conclusion holds for entrywise-positive, modular-invariant Borel-measure pairings satisfying the two vacuum marginals, even if they are initially singular or unbounded. Consequently, within these analytic classes, the Virasoro TQFT does not admit a Narain-like ensemble mechanism based on averaging distinct nontrivial torus pairings from ordinary topological boundaries. Any nontrivial ensemble interpretation must instead involve information invisible at genus one, additional sectors, or genuinely generalized boundary conditions. Technically, the proof identifies the Virasoro $S$ and $T$ transformations with the even Weil representation of the metaplectic group and applies the Cowling--Steger lattice-restriction theorem.   <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在Feynman积分的分部积分（integration-by-parts）归约中，Laporta算法的阶关系决定了主积分（master integrals）的集合。理解这些主积分积分的交集矩阵结构有助于简化计算，但现有研究中交集矩阵的结构比预期更复杂，需要系统性的理论分析。

Method: 采用几何阶关系（geometric order relation）获得的积分构造主积分，精确定义积分及其对偶（duals），在此基础上分析滤波相容基与ε因子化基下交集矩阵的代数结构与退化性质，并设计消除辅助超越函数的算法。

Conclusion: 交集矩阵的简洁结构揭示了主积分间内在的代数关系，使得在ε因子化过程中可以避免引入及处理冗余的辅助超越函数，从而提升Feynman积分计算的效率和实用性。

Abstract: In integration-by-parts reduction of Feynman integrals, the order relation in the Laporta algorithm determines a set of master integrals. In this paper we investigate the intersection matrices of the integrands of the master integrals that are obtained from a geometric order relation. With an appropriate definition of integrands and their duals, we find that the intersection matrices are simpler than expected: For a filtration-compatible basis, the entries of the intersection matrix are Laurent polynomials in the dimensional regularisation parameter $\varepsilon$. For an $\varepsilon$-factorised basis, the entries are instead integers, up to an overall power of $\varepsilon$, if the boundary values for the auxiliary functions of the rotation are chosen appropriately. Motivation: 能量关联器（energy correlator）在近共线极限下的数学结构中包含非平凡符号字母，理解其代数来源对揭示散射振幅的深层数学结构具有重要意义。

Method: 采用丛代数（cluster algebra）的框架，对四点能量关联器近共线展开（near-collinear expansion）前导阶中出现的立方根符号字母进行系统构造。

Result: 成功给出了立方根符号字母的丛代数构造，建立了这些特殊符号字母与丛代数结构之间的明确联系。

Conclusion: 该结果表明丛代数可以为超出传统符号字母范畴的更高阶数学对象提供自然且统一的构造框架。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 此前软光子定理主要在d=4维度被广泛研究，本文旨在将软光子定理推广到更高维时空，探究在d>4维度下红外行为与软项结构的物理特性。

Abstract: We derive the leading logarithmic soft-photon theorem in $d>4$ spacetime dimensions and its classical radiative counterpart. A direct one-loop analysis in massive scalar quantum electrodynamics (QED) yields a factorizing soft term of order $ω^{d-4}\lnω$, although the charged-particle S-matrix is infrared finite. The logarithm is generated by the scale-invariant loop-momentum region $ω\ll|\ell|\llΛ$, where $Λ$ denotes a characteristic hard-particle energy scale. At leading radiative order, the corresponding logarithmic contribution to the classical electromagnetic waveform arises from the long-range acceleration of the asymptotic charged particles. In even $d\geq6$, the straight-line waveform at this radiative order is distributionally supported in retarded time within an interval whose width is set by the characteristic size of the hard-scattering region, whereas the logarithmic acceleration term produces universal early- and late-time radiative tails proportional to $|u|^{-(3d-10)/2}$. In odd $d\geq5$, straight-line motion already gives a late-time tail at the same radiative order proportional to $u^{-(d-4)/2}$. The acceleration correction adds universal late- and early-time terms proportional, respectively, to $\ln u/u^{(3d-10)/2}$ and $|u|^{-(3d-10)/2}$. A comparison of Feynman and retarded boundary conditions separates the classically radiative contribution from the intrinsically quantum part of the logarithmic soft factor.

</details>


### [89] [The Hitchin and Sutcliffe metrics for hyperbolic 2-monopoles](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.03761)
*Thomas Galvin*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文比较了两种已知的双极子（two-monopole）双曲空间度规——Sutcliffe边界度规和Hitchin自对偶爱因斯坦度规，作为双曲单极子动力学模型的候选者。研究发现两者共形不等价且对单极子动力学有显著不同的预测；Sutcliffe度规在极大分离极限下不收敛于点粒子近似，而Hitchin度规通过对称性论证被证明具有与Sen猜想一致的一个L²调和二形式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 双曲空间中的单极子动力学尚无统一描述，存在多个候选度规需要比较评估。Sutcliffe边界度规与Hitchin的自对偶爱因斯坦度规是已知的两个候选模型，但它们的等价性、动力学预测差异以及是否符合现有猜想均未得到明确验证。

Method: 通过比较两种度规的共形性质来检验它们是否等价；将每种度规与Franchetti-Ross点粒子近似进行动力学预测对比；通过计算每种度规的L²调和二形式来验证Sen猜想，并利用对称性论证简化计算。

Result: 两种度规被证明共形不等价，且对单极子动力学作出显著不同的预测。Sutcliffe度规在极大分离极限下不收敛于Franchetti-Ross点粒子近似。通过对称性论证，Hitchin度规被发现具有一个L²调和二形式，与Sen猜想一致。

Conclusion: Hitchin度规符合Sen猜想且在动力学预测上可能更可靠，而Sutcliffe度规在点粒子极限下表现不佳，表明两种度规不能同时作为双曲单极子动力学的有效模型。

Abstract: We compare two currently known examples of hyperbolic SU(2) two-monopole metrics and their candidacy as models of hyperbolic monopole dynamics. The first is Sutcliffe's boundary metric and the other is a well-known self-dual Einstein metric found by Hitchin. We show that these metrics are conformally inequivalent and make significantly different predictions for monopole dynamics. We compare each metric to the Franchetti-Ross point particle approximation of hyperbolic monopole dynamics. We show Sutcliffe's metric does not converge to the point particle approximation in the large separation limit. We also check Sen's conjecture for each metric by computing their $L^2$ harmonic two-forms. By a symmetry argument we are able to show that Hitchin's metric has a single $L^2$ harmonic two-form consistent with the conjecture.

</details>


### [90] [Vacuum Polarization Energy of a Nonzero Radius Cosmic String](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.03864)
*Noah Graham,Herbert Weigel*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文在2+1维时空背景下，计算了标量场在非奇异宇宙弦背景中的真空极化能（vacuum polarization energy, VPE），将其表示为散射数据的重整化求和与积分形式，并展示了局域态密度与全域态密度之间关系在弯曲时空中的拓展。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 计算弯曲时空背景中真空极化能对于理解量子场论中的非微扰效应和宇宙弦这类拓扑缺陷的物理性质具有重要意义，但此前缺乏在非奇异宇宙弦背景下的精确计算方法。

Method: 将真空极化能表达为散射数据的重整化（renormalization）求和与积分形式；对于"圆珠笔"（ballpoint pen）模型，该表达式可写成勒让德函数（Legendre function）与贝塞尔函数（Bessel function）的组合；对于一般弦轮廓则可通过数值方法求解。文章还建立了局域态密度（local density of states, 由格林函数表达）与全域态密度（global density of states, 由Jost函数表达）之间的关系，并将其推广到该弯曲时空背景，从而精确实施微扰重整化条件。

Result: 成功将真空极化能表达为散射数据的重整化求和与积分形式，并在"圆珠笔"模型中给出了解析表达（勒让德与贝塞尔函数），同时证明局域态密度与全域态密度之间的关系可扩展到此弯曲时空背景，支持微扰重整化条件的精确实施。

Conclusion: 该研究为弯曲时空背景下真空极化能的计算提供了统一框架，既适用于可解析求解的特殊弦轮廓模型，也适用于一般弦轮廓的数值计算，拓展了态密度关系在非平直时空中的适用性。

Abstract: We calculate the vacuum polarization energy (VPE) of a scalar field in the background of a nonsingular cosmic string in $2+1$ spacetime dimensions. Our calculation expresses the VPE as a renormalized sum and integral over scattering data, which can be expressed in terms of Legendre and Bessel functions for the "ballpoint pen" model, analogous to a square well in curvature, and which can be obtained numerically for a generic string profile. We show how relationships between the local density of states, expressed in terms of the Green's function, and the global density of states, expressed in terms of the Jost function, extend to this curved spacetime background and allow for precise implementation of perturbative renormalization conditions.

</details>

### [91] [Pressure-induced Superconductivity in Thermoelectric Semiconductor Mg3Sb2](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.03058)
*Cuiying Pei,Yasong Wu,Airan Li,Juefei Wu,Qi Wang,Yifan Zhu,Yi Zhao,Lingling Gao,Changhua Li,Weizheng Cao,Shihao Zhu,Mingxin Zhang,Yulin Chen,Chenguang Fu,Tiejun Zhu,Jiong Yang,Yanpeng Qi*

Main category: cond-mat.supr-con

TL;DR: 本文报道了热电半导体Mg3Sb2在高压下被发现存在压力诱导的超导电性：在8.7 GPa发生金属化转变，随后伴随载流子类型从p型到n型的转换出现超导转变；超导临界温度（Tc）呈穹顶状压力依赖，在12.6 GPa时达到峰值3.3 K。结合理论计算、高压拉曼光谱和X射线衍射（XRD）实验，进一步揭示20 GPa以上存在另一结构相变，产生C2/m-II相，最终建立了Mg3Sb2的高压相图。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 窄带隙热电（thermoelectric, TE）材料的内在电子结构为高压下准粒子耦合效应的研究提供了平台，可探索新兴的电子与声子输运、超导电性及拓扑转变。然而，此前对Mg3Sb2在高压下的电子结构演化及其可能伴随的超导行为研究不足，缺乏系统的压力-相变-电输运关联图景。

Method: 本研究综合运用高压电输运测量、理论计算、高压拉曼光谱（Raman spectroscopy）和X射线衍射（XRD）等多种手段，系统表征了Mg3Sb2在加压过程中的金属化、超导转变、载流子类型变化及结构相变行为，并据此构建高压相图。

Result: Mg3Sb2在8.7 GPa发生金属化，随后出现超导转变并伴随载流子类型从p型到n型的交叉；该现象源于从半导体P-3m1相到金属C2/m-I相的压力诱导结构相变。超导临界温度（Tc）呈穹顶状压力依赖，峰值3.3 K出现在12.6 GPa。高于20 GPa时发现另一结构相变，形成C2/m-II相。

Conclusion: 本研究建立了Mg3Sb2的高压相图，阐明了其压力依赖的电子性质变化机制，为未来高压下热电材料的深入研究提供了有价值的参考与启示。

Abstract: The intrinsic electronic structures of narrow bandgap thermoelectric (TE) materials serve as a platform for the investigation of coupling effects of quasi-particles under high pressure, enabling the exploration of emerging electronic and phonon transport, superconductivity, and topological transition. Here, we report the discovery of pressure-induced superconductivity in the TE semiconductor Mg3Sb2. Upon the increased pressure, the metallization occurs at 8.7 GPa, followed by a superconducting transition concomitant with a carrier-type crossover from p- to n-type. This phenomenon arises from a pressure-induced structural phase transition from the semiconducting P-3m1 to the metallic C2/m-I phase. The superconducting critical temperature (Tc) exhibits a dome-shaped pressure dependence, peaking at 3.3 K at 12.6 GPa. Combined theoretical calculations, high-pressure Raman spectroscopy, and X-ray diffraction (XRD) measurements reveal an additional structural transition above 20 GPa, yielding a distinct C2/m-II phase. Our findings establish the high-pressure phase diagram of Mg3Sb2, elucidate its pressure-dependent electronic properties, and provide valuable insights for future investigations of TE materials under high pressure.

</details>


### [92] [Pressure induced magnetic-field-free superconducting diode effect in NbSe2 flake](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.03072)
*Shihao Zhu,Tian Le,Cuiying Pei,Changhua Li,Yi Liao,Yi Zhao,Lingxiao Zhao,Qi Wang,Juefei Wu,Qilian Zhang,Yueshen Wu,Tonghuan Fu,Xujie Lü,Wenge Yang,Jie Shen,Jun Li,Yulin Chen,Xiao Lin,Wen-Yu He,Yanpeng Qi*

Main category: cond-mat.supr-con

TL;DR: 本文报道了在无异质结构的NbSe2薄片中，通过施加压力实现了无磁场超导二极管效应（SDE），其关键发现是SDE关于外磁场表现出偶函数行为，暗示无需显式打破时间反演对称性（TRS）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 超导二极管效应是一种重要但被普遍认为必须同时打破反演对称性（IS）和时间反演对称性（TRS）才能实现的现象，这通常依赖异质结构工程和外加磁场。作者旨在探索是否可以通过更简洁的方式（如压力）在单一材料中实现无磁场SDE，并挑战现有理论范式。

Method: 作者在无任何异质结构的NbSe2薄片中施加压力，利用二次谐波产生（second harmonic generation）确认了压力单独即可打破反演对称性，并通过测量外加面外磁场下SDE的场依赖行为来确定时间反演对称性是否被打破。

Result: 压力单独作用即可打破IS；在施加面外磁场B时，SDE表现出关于B为偶函数的特征，暗示不存在显式TRS破坏。这一结果首次在无异质结构的单一材料中实现无磁场SDE。

Conclusion: 本工作挑战了SDE必须显式打破TRS的现有理论范式，表明无磁场SDE可以在不显式破坏TRS的情况下实现，并将压力工程确立为诱导非互易超导性和设计无磁场超导器件的强大工具。

Abstract: The superconducting diode effect (SDE) is a fascinating nonreciprocal phenomenon where the critical current is different for opposite current directions. It is widely believed that realizing SDE requires breaking both inversion symmetry (IS) and time-reversal symmetry (TRS), which are usually achieved via heterostructure engineering and applying external magnetic fields. Here, we report a pressure-induced magnetic-field-free SDE in NbSe2 flakes without any heterostructures. We show that pressure alone breaks the IS, as confirmed by the second harmonic generation. Crucially, upon applying an out-of-plane magnetic field (B), the SDE exhibits even-in-B behavior, implying the absence of explicit TRS breaking. This finding challenges the prevailing theoretical paradigm and demonstrates that a magnetic-field-free SDE can emerge without explicitly breaking TRS. Thereby, our work establishes pressure engineering as a powerful tool for inducing nonreciprocal superconductivity and designing versatile, magnetic-field-free superconducting devices.

</details>


### [93] [Two-phonon pairing and superconductivity in $SrTiO_3$](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.03823)
*Antonio Santacesaria,Cristiano Muzzi,Maria Eleonora Temperini,Paolo Barone,Maria N. Gastiasoro,José Lorenzana*

Main category: cond-mat.supr-con

TL;DR: 本文探讨由Ngai提出的双声子配对机制（two-phonon pairing mechanism）能否解释掺杂钛酸锶（STO）中的超导电性，通过第一性原理和经验估算评估双声子形变势，导出了有效电子-电子相互作用，并在弱耦合极限下求解单圈Eliashberg方程，发现该机制在Tc量级上无法被排除，但其预测的Tc随掺杂单调上升与实验不符，因此需要重新审视其适用条件。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 掺杂钛酸锶的超导机制长期存在争议，传统单声子电-声耦合（electron-phonon coupling）难以完全解释其行为，因此探索双声子配对机制是否能够提供替代解释。

Method: 作者采用四种独立的形变势估算方法（两种基于第一性原理计算、两种基于实验数据的经验估计），推导由双声子交换介导的有效电子-电子相互作用，利用软声子强色散特性，在Migdal定理不适用（因双声子相互作用尺度远大于费米能）的情况下推导单圈Eliashberg方程，并在弱耦合极限下求解。

Result: 双声子形变势的四种估算给出同一数量级的极大值；尽管电-声矩阵元显著，相空间因素大幅削弱有效耦合；该机制不能仅凭Tc大小被排除，但其值主要由高能频率物理主导，受软声子行为影响微弱；理论预测Tc随掺杂单调增加，与实验观察不一致。

Conclusion: 双声子机制无法被Tc量级单独排除，但其Tc随掺杂单调上升的预言与实验不符，暗示该机制对掺杂钛酸锶超导性的解释存在根本性局限；作者据此识别了双声子机制在其他材料中有效的条件，并讨论了可能的候选材料。

Abstract: We explore the possibility that the two-phonon pairing mechanism proposed by Ngai can explain superconductivity in doped strontium titanate (STO). The two-phonon deformation potential is evaluated using two distinct theoretical estimates based on first-principles calculations and two empirical estimates based on experimental data, yielding very large values of the same order of magnitude. We derive the effective electron-electron interaction mediated by two-phonon exchange. Crucial to our computations is the strong dispersion of the involved soft phonons. Because the scale of the two-phonon interaction is much larger than the Fermi energy, the Migdal theorem does not apply. We obtain a one-loop Eliashberg equation that can be solved in the weak-coupling limit. We find that despite the significant electron-phonon matrix element, phase space considerations considerably reduce the effective coupling. A two-phonon mechanism can not be excluded based on the $T_c$ magnitude, but its value is dominated by the high-energy-frequency physics and is weakly affected by the soft-phonon behavior. The theory predicts a $T_c$ that monotonously increases with doping, which is not in accordance with the experiment. We identify the conditions for a two-phonon mechanism to be effective in other materials and discuss possible candidates.

</details>


### [94] [Thermodynamic phase transition, pairing symmetry and Fermi surface topology in Ruddlesden-Popper nickelate films](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.03908)
*Yu Miao,Zhiwei Wang,Hongxu Sun,Jianchang Shen,Runqing Luan,Zhipeng Ou,Xinru Yong,Zhenyu Wang,Tao Wu,Haoyu Hu,Junfeng He,Xianhui Chen*

Main category: cond-mat.supr-con

TL;DR: 本文通过角分辨光电子能谱（angle-resolved photoemission spectroscopy, ARPES）研究了一类新型高温超导Ruddlesden-Popper（RP）镍酸盐异质结构La₂PrNi₂O₇/NdAlO₃，在无赝能隙（pseudogap）状态下直接探测到超导序参量（superconducting order parameter）与电子比热跳变，证实了热力学相变，并明确建立了节点缺失（nodeless）的超导对称性证据。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: RP镍酸盐为探索高温超导（high-Tc superconductivity）及其超导机制提供了尚未开发的领域，但此前缺乏镍酸盐中超导相变的热力学直接证据，且超导序参量的对称性尚未明确。

Method: 采用角分辨光电子能谱（ARPES）测量La₂PrNi₂O₇/NdAlO₃异质结构的电子结构，通过观察超导能隙在Tc处打开及相干峰（coherence peaks）的出现，提取超导序参量；同时基于谱数据在微观层面提取电子比热（electronic specific heat）；并开展应变依赖（strain-dependent）测量以分析费米面拓扑与超导性关系。

Result: 实验观察到超导态在无赝能隙状态下形成，超导能隙在Tc处打开并伴随显著相干峰；电子比热跳变在Tc处出现，证明热力学相变发生；超导序参量大小由相干峰定量表征，并在无赝能隙条件下明确确立节点缺失（nodeless）行为；费米面由α、β、γ三个口袋构成，呈多轨道（multi-orbital）性质；应变依赖测量显示γ口袋在所有超导与非超导薄膜中均存在。

Conclusion: 该研究填补了镍酸盐中超导相变缺失的热力学证据，并为超导序参量的对称性提供了直接证明，同时阐明了RP镍酸盐薄膜中费米面拓扑与超导涌现之间的关系。

Abstract: Ruddlesden-Popper (RP) nickelates provide an uncharted territory to explore high-transition-temperature (high-$T_C$) superconductivity and superconducting mechanism. Here, we investigate the electronic structure of a new type of high-$T_C$ superconducting RP nickelate heterostructure $\mathrm{La_2PrNi_2O_7/NdAlO_3}$ by angle-resolved photoemission spectroscopy. A superconducting state is observed without a pseudogap state, enabling a direct measurement of the superconducting order parameter and a microscopic extraction of the electronic specific heat. The observed superconducting gap opens at $T_C$ with prominent coherence peaks, illustrating the emergence of nonzero order parameter upon entering the superconducting state. An electronic specific heat jump appears at $T_C$, further demonstrating a thermodynamic phase transition. The magnitude of the superconducting order parameter is quantified by the observed superconducting coherence peaks, and a nodeless behavior is unambiguously established in the absence of pseudogap. The underlying Fermi surface consists of $α$, $β$ and $γ$ pockets, exhibiting a multi-orbital nature. Strain dependent measurements further reveal the $γ$ pocket in all superconducting and non-superconducting films with different epitaxial strain. Our results establish the missing thermodynamic evidence for superconducting phase transition in nickelates. They also provide direct evidence for the symmetry of the superconducting order parameter and illustrate the relationship between Fermi surface topology and the emergence of superconductivity in RP nickelate films.

</details>

### [95] [Thermodynamically consistent initialization of the Maxwell--Cattaneo---Vernotte heat conduction model: Analytical solutions and engineering applications](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.03495)
*Zalán Sándor,Róbert Kovács*

Main category: physics.app-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究一维麦克斯韦-卡塔内奥-维尔诺特（Maxwell–Cattaneo–Vernotte, MCV）热传导模型的数值初始化问题，系统比较了三种初始时间导数设定策略，证明精确的空间依赖初始导数能够避免非物理振荡并匹配解析解，提出了一种热力学一致的初始化方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代高频热工程应用中，传统时间积分方案和商用有限元求解器处理非傅里叶模型时经常引入严重的数值伪影；同时，非平衡态下初始时间导数的定义在数学上具有挑战性，目前缺少系统性的初始化策略评估。

Method: 采用显式交错有限差分格式（explicit staggered finite-difference scheme），在绝热边界条件下对指数分布初始温度场的瞬态响应进行评估；系统比较了零导数、空间均匀非零导数、精确空间依赖导数三种初始化策略，并以伽辽金法（Galerkin method）解析解作为基准。

Result: 假设零或空间均匀初始导数会引入显著的非物理振荡偏差，且随松弛时间（relaxation time）增大导致热通量预测误差加剧；将精确空间依赖导数映射到交错网格则保持初始态的局部热力学结构，能实现与解析基准匹配的稳健瞬态响应且无显著计算开销。

Conclusion: 建立了一种热力学一致的初始化技术，该方法可进一步推广到非局域（non-local）模型。

Abstract: This paper investigates the numerical initialization of the one-dimensional Maxwell--Cattaneo--Vernotte (MCV) heat conduction model, addressing the critical mathematical challenge of defining the initial time derivative for non-equilibrium states. In modern high-frequency thermal engineering applications, traditional time-integration schemes and commercial finite-element solvers frequently introduce severe numerical artifacts when handling non-Fourier models. This study systematically evaluates three initialization strategies: a zero derivative, a spatially uniform non-zero derivative, and an exact space-dependent derivative. Using an explicit staggered finite-difference scheme, the transient responses to an exponentially distributed initial temperature field under adiabatic boundary conditions are compared against an analytical solution using the Galerkin method. The results demonstrate that assuming a zero or spatially uniform initial derivative introduces significant unphysical oscillatory deviations, leading to heat-flux prediction errors as the relaxation time increases. Conversely, mapping the exact space-dependent derivative onto the staggered grid preserves the local thermodynamic structure of the initial state, yielding robust transient responses that match the analytical benchmark without meaningful computational overhead. These findings establish a thermodynamically consistent initialization technique that can be extended to non-local models as well.

</details>

### [96] [Faraday pattern formation in dipolar superfluid and supersolid quantum gases](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.03553)
*Sumit Semwal,Shawan K. Jha,G. A. Bougas,S. I. Mistakidis,Pankaj Kumar Mishra*

Main category: cond-mat.quant-gas

TL;DR: 本文在扩展Gross-Pitaevskii方程框架下研究三维参数驱动偶极量子气体中的Faraday不稳定性，揭示了从表面集体模式到混合表面-体激发（surface-bulk excitation）的转变，并证明参数驱动在长程相互作用量子流体的斑图形成中起主导作用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解偶极量子气体中参数驱动条件下Faraday不稳定性的产生机制，以及偶极相互作用和超固态转变对斑图形成的影响。

Method: 基于扩展Gross-Pitaevskii方程（extended Gross-Pitaevskii equation）进行三维数值模拟，并通过Mathieu方程（Mathieu equation）解析描述共振频率与参数不稳定窗口。

Result: 在超流体区，短程相互作用的周期调制激发离散多边形状表面模式并呈现次谐波响应；偶极相互作用使共振频率低于非偶极对应值；在超流体-超固态转变附近，柔软的旋子模（roton mode）加速斑图形成并产生混合表面-体激发；在超固态深处，体斑图源于液滴与超流体背景的碰撞而占主导。

Conclusion: 结果表明存在从表面集体模式到混合表面-体激发的交叉（crossover），并展示了参数驱动对长程相互作用量子流体中斑图形成的调控作用。

Abstract: We investigate Faraday instabilities in three-dimensional parametrically driven trapped dipolar quantum gases within the framework of the extended Gross-Pitaevskii equation. In the superfluid regime, periodic modulation of the short-range interactions induces the resonant excitation of discrete polygonal surface modes displaying sub-harmonic response. The resonance frequencies and the parametric windows of instability are accurately captured by an appropriate Mathieu equation, further corroborating our simulations. It is also shown that dipolar interactions in superfluids shift the resonance frequencies to lower values compared to their non-dipolar counterparts. Near the superfluid-to-supersolid transition, intrinsic density undulations associated with the softened roton mode accelerate pattern formation, yielding hybrid surface and bulk excitations. The bulk patterns prevail deeper in the supersolid regime, emerging from droplet collisions with the superfluid background. Our results reveal a crossover from surface collective modes to hybrid surface-bulk excitations and demonstrate how parametric driving dictates pattern formation in long-range interacting quantum fluids.

</details>


### [97] [Quantum Impurities as Probes of Finite-Temperature Fluctuations in Two-Dimensional Bose Gases](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.03665)
*Victor Velasco,Gabriele Spada,Giovanni Midei,Andrea Perali,Luis A. Peña Ardila*

Main category: cond-mat.quant-gas

TL;DR: 本文研究了一个弱相互作用二维玻色气体中的吸引杂质（attractive impurity），采用有限温度多体散射理论与路径积分蒙特卡洛（path-integral Monte Carlo）的混合方法，分析了凝聚区和正常区中杂质准粒子能量（quasiparticle energy）的温度依赖行为，发现声子激活温度以下的能量近乎与温度无关，而声子热占据与凝聚耗散（condensate depletion）的竞争导致极化子（polaron）能量呈现非单调温度依赖。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 二维量子气体中热涨落、有限尺寸效应与两体束缚态形成天然耦合，理解吸引杂质在该环境下的极化子行为对低维量子多体物理具有重要意义。

Method: 采用混合方法，将有限温度多体散射理论与路径积分蒙特卡洛输入相结合，分析凝聚区和正常区中的杂质准粒子能量。

Result: 二维有限尺寸通过引入红外动量尺度稳定了非零凝聚分数，使玻色浴可用Bogoliubov描述；由此产生的红外尺度导致声子激活温度，低于该温度时杂质能量几乎不随温度变化；当声子模式被热占据后，其贡献与凝聚耗散竞争，导致极化子能量出现非单调温度依赖。

Conclusion: 吸引性玻色极化子可能成为低维玻色气体中有限尺寸热涨落、声子缀饰（phonon dressing）和束缚态物理的灵敏探针。

Abstract: Two-dimensional quantum gases provide a distinctive setting in which enhanced thermal fluctuations, finite-size effects, and two-body bound-state formation are intrinsically intertwined. In this work, we study a single attractive impurity immersed in a finite, weakly interacting two-dimensional Bose gas, where finite size stabilizes a nonzero condensate fraction by introducing an infrared momentum scale, thereby enabling a Bogoliubov description of the bath. Using a hybrid approach that combines finite-temperature many-body scattering theory with input from path-integral Monte Carlo, we analyze the impurity quasiparticle energy across the condensate and normal regimes. The infrared scale generates a phonon-activation temperature below which the impurity energy remains nearly temperature independent. Once the resolved phonon modes become thermally populated, their contribution competes with condensate depletion, producing a nonmonotonic temperature dependence of the polaron energy. These results suggest that attractive Bose polarons may serve as sensitive probes of finite-size thermal fluctuations, phonon dressing, and bound-state physics in low-dimensional Bose gases.

</details>

### [98] [Anomalous Thermal Dimension and the Enthalpy Renormalization Group Flow in QCD](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.02649)
*S. D. Campos*

Main category: hep-lat

TL;DR: 本文提出了一个基于Callan-Symanzik型偏微分方程的热力学标度框架来描述QCD相变。通过定义异常热维度h(T)，该框架成功从第一性原理格点QCD数据中捕捉到退禁闭crossover的"标度峰"，并建立量子标度反常与强相互作用物质信息几何曲率之间的新联系。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: QCD相变的精确表征仍是一个基本开放问题，主要因其非微扰本质导致临界温度附近的（近似）标度不变性破缺难以用解析方法处理。

Method: 推导了一个控制QCD焓（enthalpy）标度依赖的线性Callan-Symanzik型偏微分方程，将宏观观测量映射到无量纲辅助场空间，定义异常热维度h(T)来探测迹反常（trace anomaly）和共形热力学态空间几何的形变。

Result: 该框架在Wuppertal-Budapest合作组的格点QCD数据上得到验证，成功捕捉到退禁闭crossover相关的局部"标度峰"（scaling bump），这一特征通常被MIT袋模型（MIT Bag Model）等解析模型遗漏。与已知普适类（universality classes）的比较显示，基于焓的重整化群流对有限夸克质量引起的显式标度破缺高度敏感。

Conclusion: h(T)作为标度变换的热力学响应函数（thermodynamic susceptibility），为量子标度反常与强相互作用物质信息几何曲率之间提供了新的联系。

Abstract: A precise characterization of the QCD phase transition remains a fundamental open problem, primarily due to the intrinsically non-perturbative nature of the dynamics that govern the breakdown of (approximate) scale invariance in the vicinity of the critical temperature $T_c$. In this work, we formulate a thermodynamic scaling framework for the QCD enthalpy by deriving a linear Callan-Symanzik-type partial differential equation that governs its scale dependence. By mapping macroscopic observables onto a dimensionless auxiliary field space, we define an anomalous thermal dimension, $h(T)$, which probes the trace anomaly and the deformation of the conformal thermodynamic state-space geometry. This framework is confirmed against first-principles Lattice QCD data from the Wuppertal-Budapest collaboration, successfully capturing the localized "scaling bump" associated with the deconfinement crossover, a feature typically missed by analytical models such as the MIT Bag Model. Comparison with known universality classes shows that the enthalpy-based renormalization group flow is highly sensitive to explicit scale breaking from finite quark masses. These results indicate that $h(T)$ acts as a thermodynamic susceptibility to scale transformations, offering a new link between quantum scale anomalies and the information-geometric curvature of strongly interacting matter.

</details>

### [99] [A "Neutrino Fog" For Gravitational Waves: The Stochastic Gravitational Wave Background from Supernova Neutrino Memory](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.02747)
*Alex Rojewski,Cecilia Lunardini*

Main category: astro-ph.HE

TL;DR: 本文对核心坍缩超新星（core collapse supernovae）产生的随机引力波背景（stochastic gravitational wave backgrounds, SGWBs）进行建模，基于大量三维、多秒超新星模拟，刻画了该背景的两个主要成分，并发现引力波记忆（gravitational wave memory）成分在未来的空间探测器中具有最佳探测前景。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 来自不可分辨源的引力波（即随机引力波背景，SGWBs）携带关于其不同来源背后物理过程的重要信息，是未来实验搜索的重要目标。对超新星产生的该背景进行精确建模，有助于理解和搜索这些关键信号。

Method: 利用大量当前最先进的（state-of-the-art）三维、多秒超新星模拟数据，对核心坍缩超新星产生的随机引力波背景进行建模，并分别刻画该背景的两个主要成分：其一是由各向异性中微子发射（anisotropic neutrino emission）引起的引力波记忆成分（频率约10^{-2}-1 Hz），其二是来自近核心物质动力学（near-core matter dynamics）的成分（频率约10^{2} Hz及以上）。

Result: 研究发现引力波记忆成分具有最佳探测前景，其特征峰值位于约0.1 Hz，在未来的空间探测器探测范围内。在该峰值频率处，其能量密度可能与慢滚胀（slow roll inflation）以及可能的宇宙学遗迹（cosmological relics）产生的背景相当。

Conclusion: 超新星引力波记忆背景在峰值频率处可能与慢滚胀和宇宙学遗迹背景的能量密度相当，因此可能对这些重要信号的搜索产生影响，需要予以关注。

Abstract: Gravitational waves originating from unresolved sources, or stochastic gravitational wave backgrounds (SGWBs), carry precious information about the physics underlying their diverse sources, and represent an important target for future experimental searches. Here, we model the SGWB due to core collapse supernovae. Using an extensive collection of state-of-the-art, three-dimensional, multi-second supernova simulations, we characterize the two main components of this background: one at $f \sim 10^{-2} - 1$ Hz, due to anisotropic neutrino emission (the gravitational wave memory); the other at $f \gtrsim 10^{2}$ Hz, from the near-core matter dynamics. We find that the memory component offers the best prospects of detection, as its characteristic peak at $f\sim 0.1$ Hz is within the reach of future space-born detectors. At the peak, the energy density might be comparable to that of backgrounds from slow roll inflation and from possible cosmological relics, thus potentially impacting searches of these important signals.

</details>


### [100] [Structural quasi-universality in highly magnetized differentially rotating neutron stars](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.02824)
*Pratul Manna,Surajit Kalita*

Main category: astro-ph.HE

TL;DR: 该论文将中子星（neutron star）的拟普适关系（quasi-universal relation）从均匀转动（uniform rotation）和弱磁化（weak magnetization）假设推广到微分转动（differential rotation）和强磁化（strong magnetization）配置，建立了转动惯量（moment of inertia）与致密度（compactness）之间的广义拟普适关系，并将其应用于磁星（magnetar）光度估计和GW170817双中子星并合残骸（post-merger remnant）转动动能的系统性偏差量化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 中子星宏观性质的普适关系可为探测其内部结构提供有力框架，同时最小化状态方程（equation of state, EoS）相关不确定性。然而，该类关系此前主要针对均匀转动星展开研究，对微分转动和强磁化构型的拓展在很大程度上仍未探索。

Method: 系统地研究了广泛状态方程、转动剖面和磁场强度下的平衡构型（equilibrium configurations），通过数值模拟建立了转动惯量与致密度之间对状态方程不敏感的广义拟普适关系；针对固定角动量序列，进一步推导了归一化转动惯量对致密度依赖性的解析表达式，构建了适用于广泛恒星构型的统一唯象模型（phenomenological model）。

Result: 在均匀转动、微分转动和强磁化构型下，转动惯量与致密度之间均存在对状态方程高度不敏感的拟普适关系。固定角动量序列中，归一化转动惯量对致密度的拟普适依赖性偏差主要来源于磁场强度和微分转动程度，且该偏差可被解析表达。作为天体物理应用，量化了磁星光度估计和GW170817并合残骸转动动能估计中的系统性偏差。

Conclusion: 该研究将拟普适关系推广至超越均匀转动和弱磁化的标准假设，为解释高磁化、微分转动的中子星观测提供了稳健框架，能够对其天体物理性质给出更可靠的约束。

Abstract: Universal relations among macroscopic properties of neutron stars provide a powerful framework to probe their internal structures while minimizing uncertainties associated with the equation of state (EoS). Although such relations have been extensively studied for uniformly rotating stars, their extension to differentially rotating and strongly magnetized configurations remains largely unexplored. We systematically investigate equilibrium configurations for a wide range of EoSs, rotation profiles, and magnetic field strengths. We establish a generalized quasi-universal relation between the moment of inertia and compactness that remains remarkably insensitive to the underlying EoS across uniformly rotating, differentially rotating, and strongly magnetized configurations. For sequences with fixed angular momentum, the normalized moment of inertia exhibits a quasi-universal dependence on compactness, with deviations primarily due to magnetic field strength and degree of differential rotation. We derive analytic expressions for these dependencies, enabling a unified phenomenological model applicable over a wide range of stellar configurations. As astrophysical applications, we quantify the systematic bias in magnetar luminosity estimates and the rotational kinetic energy of post-merger remnant of GW170817. These results extend quasi-universal relations beyond the standard assumptions of uniform rotation and weak magnetization, providing a robust framework for interpreting observations of highly magnetized and differentially rotating neutron stars, and enabling more reliable constraints on their astrophysical properties.

</details>


### [101] [General-relativistic structure of two-component quantum dark fermion stars](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.03593)
*Ilídio Lopes*

Main category: astro-ph.HE

TL;DR: 本文构建了一个广义相对论框架，用于描述由两种简并费米子组成的量子暗物质星（quantum dark fermion stars）：其平衡结构由引力、Yukawa介导的暗第五力以及Bohm量子压强修正共同决定。在粒子质量10^-11~10^-10 eV范围内，此类星体半径3~24 km、致密性0.14~0.34，既可伪装为中子星，也可在最高致密端伪装为质量间隙区的低质量黑洞；潮汐形变率测量可将其与两者区分。该框架为通过多信使观测约束暗扇区微观物理提供了可复现、可证伪的模板。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 暗物质（dark matter）的本质是当代物理学核心未解问题之一，但暗扇区（dark sector）的微观物理参数（如费米子质量、相互作用强度）长期缺乏直接可观测的约束途径。作者旨在建立一种能将暗物质微观性质与引力波、天体测量等宏观观测直接联系起来的形式化框架。

Method: 方法上，作者在Schwarzschild内部保留非线性Klein–Gordon方程的完整协变形式，并采用适用于任意致密度的闭合关系（closure relations），涵盖从超轻基准到相对论标量密度不可忽略的高密度区（约0.16 fm^-3）。仅有两个拉格朗日参数——暗费米子质量与Yukawa通道对引力的无量纲比值——即可确定平衡结构。

Result: 计算显示，在粒子质量10^-11~10^-10 eV范围内，星体呈现3~24 km半径与0.14~0.34致密性（compactness），表现为"双重模仿者"：中等致密性下在质量、半径和吸入频率上与中子星重叠；致密端（κ≈0.34，R_T/R_S≈1.5）穿越光子球，可伪装为质量间隙区低质量黑洞。无量纲潮汐形变率（dimensionless tidal deformability）明显小于中子星值但非零，可与真黑洞区分。这些天体落入LISA、爱因斯坦望远镜（Einstein Telescope）和宇宙探险家（Cosmic Explorer）的灵敏带，且产生Gaia可单独分辨的天体测量微透镜信号。

Conclusion: 该框架为通过多信使观测（引力波、微透镜、X射线等）约束暗扇区性质提供了一个可复现且可证伪的模板；联合测量质量与半径可直接从引力波天文台（gravitational-wave observatory）观测中反推暗扇区微观物理参数。

Abstract: We develop a general-relativistic framework for two-component quantum dark fermion stars: equilibrium configurations of two degenerate fermion species governed by gravity, a Yukawa-mediated dark fifth force and a globally relevant Bohm quantum-pressure correction. The treatment retains the full covariant form of the nonlinear Klein--Gordon equation in the Schwarzschild interior, with closure relations valid at arbitrary compactness, from the ultralight baseline up to densities of order $0.16\,\mathrm{fm}^{-3}$ at which relativistic scalar densities and self-consistent effective fermion masses become unavoidable. Two Lagrangian parameters, the dark-fermion mass and the dimensionless ratio between the Yukawa channel and gravity, together fix the equilibrium structure; a joint measurement of mass and radius therefore constrains the dark-sector microphysics directly from gravitational-wave observables. For particle masses in the band $10^{-11}$--$10^{-10}$ eV, the configurations exhibit radii of $3$--$24\,\mathrm{km}$ and compactness in the range $0.14$--$0.34$, behaving as dual mimickers: at moderate compactness they overlap with neutron stars in mass, radius, and inspiral frequency; at the most compact end ($κ\sim 0.34$, $R_T/R_S\simeq 1.5$) they cross the photon sphere and could masquerade as low-mass black holes in the mass-gap region. Tidal-deformability measurements discriminate against both populations: the dimensionless tidal deformability is measurably smaller than the neutron-star value yet remains non-zero, unlike that of a genuine black hole. These objects populate the sensitivity bands of LISA, the Einstein Telescope, and Cosmic Explorer, producing astrometric microlensing signatures individually resolvable by \textit{Gaia}. The framework thus provides a reproducible and falsifiable template for constraining dark-sector properties through multi-messenger observations.

</details>


<div id='math.NA'></div>

# math.NA [[Back]](#toc)

### [102] [Collimated Sunlight and Air Temperature](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.03781)
*Olivier Pironneau*

Main category: math.NA

TL;DR: 该论文提出了一种非常快速的数值实现方法，用于处理气候模型中辐射模块面临的准直光（collimated light）狄拉克奇点（Dirac singularity）边界条件问题，方法遵循Siewert和Maiorino（1980）的思路。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 地球大气温度源自复杂的物理现象，气候模型必须考虑辐射这一关键模块，而太阳光作为近点源在天空中出现，会在边界条件中产生狄拉克奇点（即准直光），该奇点的数值处理是辐射计算中的难点。

Method: 采用Siewert和Maiorino（1980）提出的方法，设计了一种非常快速的数值实现方案来消除或处理准直光带来的狄拉克奇点。

Result: 论文给出了一种快速数值算法，可有效处理该奇点，从而支持气候模型中辐射传输（radiative transfer）的数值求解。

Conclusion: 通过该数值实现，大气辐射计算中的奇点问题可被高效处理，为气候模型提供更可靠的辐射模块支持。

Abstract: Atmospheric temperature on Earth results from complex phenomena that climate models must account for. One important module is radiation, which has three sources: infrared radiation emitted by the Earth and the air, and visible light from the Sun. The latter arrives from an almost point-like source in the sky, producing a Dirac singularity in the boundary conditions known as collimated light. Following Siewert and Maiorino (1980), we propose a very fast numerical implementation to handle the singularity.

</details>


### [103] [Reduced-order modeling for electromagnetic inverse problems: a layered medium benchmark](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.03996)
*Konstantinos Alexopoulos,Josselin Garnier*

Main category: math.NA

TL;DR: 本文研究分层介质反问题中的降阶建模（reduced-order modeling）方法，利用Goupillaud结构将正问题表述为离散动力系统，并在约化算子（reduced operator）层面定义基于ROM的目标函数。通过在五层介质上进行多种结构扰动下的数值实验，发现基于ROM的反演在无噪声和特定结构扰动情形下提供更优的重构效果，并在所有测试情形中与经典数据拟合（data misfit）方法保持竞争力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在分层介质的反问题中恢复阻抗剖面（impedance profile）是一个经典但具有挑战性的问题；传统基于数据拟合的目标函数在噪声或结构扰动下可能不够稳定，因此需要探索新的反演策略。

Method: 基于Goupillaud结构将正问题表示为离散动力系统，并构建在约化算子层面定义的ROM目标函数；在五层介质上施加多种结构化扰动，通过数值实验将ROM目标与经典数据拟合目标进行系统比较。

Result: ROM-based反演在无噪声（clean）情形和某些结构化扰动（structured perturbation）区间内给出了更优的重构结果，并且在所有测试情形下均与经典方法表现相当或更好。

Conclusion: 基于ROM的反演方法在分层介质阻抗反演中具有良好的稳健性和竞争力，可视为对经典数据拟合方法的有力替代或补充。

Abstract: We study reduced-order modeling for inverse problems in layered media, focusing on the recovery of impedance profiles from time-domain measurements. Using the Goupillaud structure, we formulate the forward problem as a discrete dynamical system and introduce a ROM-based objective defined at the level of reduced operators. Through numerical experiments on a 5-layer medium under various structured perturbations, we compare the ROM-based objective with a classical data misfit. The results show that the ROM-based inversion provides a more favorable reconstruction in the clean setting and in certain structured perturbation regimes, while remaining consistently competitive with the classical approach across all cases considered.

</details>
