### [104] [A Physics-Informed Hybrid Neural Operator for Transient Magnetization Prediction in Power Magnetics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.02965)
*Yachao Zhu,Qiujie Huang,Sinan Li,Yang Li,Gang Lei,Jianguo Zhu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出物理信息混合神经算子（Physics-Informed Hybrid Neural Operator, PI-HNO），一种紧凑的材料特异性神经模型，结合B-H能量一致性正则化，用于面向磁芯损耗的瞬态磁化预测。在MagNetX瞬态数据库上对14种铁氧体材料进行验证，PI-HNO以仅4777个可训练参数实现了平均1.92%、95分位7.60%的B(t)-H(t)能量一致性误差。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高频高功率密度变换器中的磁性元件日益受到非正弦磁通密度波形驱动，涵盖快速转换、小回线（minor-loop）运行、直流偏置和温度变化等工况。在这些条件下，稳态磁芯损耗公式和单值材料曲线无法完整捕捉瞬态磁化响应，亟需新的建模方法。

Method: PI-HNO将局部递归分支与基于Preisach思想的全局分支相结合：局部递归分支负责边界状态表示和率相关响应演化，全局分支提取波形级磁滞上下文。模型以B(t)-H(t)测量历史、预测区间内的B(t)序列和运行条件信息为输入，预测H(t)序列及对应的B-H轨迹重构，并通过B-H能量一致性正则化约束训练。

Result: 在MagNetX瞬态数据库上针对14种铁氧体材料的材料特异性模型评估中，PI-HNO以每模型仅4777个可训练参数实现了序列精度与B(t)-H(t)能量一致性的紧凑权衡，平均能量一致性误差为1.92%，95分位误差为7.60%。消融研究表明局部、全局和能量感知正则化组件对瞬态磁化预测均有独立贡献。

Conclusion: PI-HNO以极少的参数量实现了高精度的瞬态磁化预测，兼顾序列预测准确性与B-H能量一致性，为高功率密度变换器中磁性元件建模提供了紧凑有效的解决方案。

Abstract: Magnetic components in high-frequency, high-power-density converters are increasingly driven by non-sinusoidal flux-density waveforms with fast transitions, minor-loop operation, dc bias, and temperature variation. Under these conditions, steady-state core-loss formulas and single-valued material curves cannot fully capture transient magnetization responses. This work proposes the Physics-Informed Hybrid Neural Operator (PI-HNO), a compact material-specific neural model with B-H energy-consistency regularization for core-loss-oriented transient magnetization prediction. Given the measured B(t)-H(t) history, the input B(t) series over the prediction interval and operating-condition information, PI-HNO predicts the H(t) series and the corresponding reconstructed B-H trajectory. The model integrates a local recurrent branch for boundary-state representation and rate-dependent response evolution with a Preisach-inspired global branch that extracts waveform-level hysteresis context. Evaluation on the MagNetX transient database using material-specific models for 14 ferrite materials demonstrates that PI-HNO achieves a compact trade-off between sequence accuracy and B(t)-H(t) energy consistency, with the mean and 95th percentile B(t)-H(t) energy consistency errors of 1.92% and 7.60%, respectively, using only 4777 trainable parameters per model. Ablation studies further demonstrate that the local, global, and energy-aware regularized components provide distinct contributions to transient magnetization prediction.

</details>

### [105] [Universal crossovers in weakly-monitored quantum critical states](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.02716)
*Abhishek Kumar,Rushikesh A. Patil,Andreas W. W. Ludwig,Romain Vasseur*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 该论文研究了临界与三临界一维量子Ising哈密顿量基态在弱能量测量和弱自旋测量（无后选择）后的测量后系综，通过有限尺寸重整化群交叉分析揭示了由Born规则随机性主导的"测量主导"或"测量改变"不动点，并对不同量子临界模型展示了不同的重整化群流向结构。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解测量如何作为相关扰动（relevant perturbation）作用于未测量的临界基态，并揭示测量诱导随机性如何影响临界系统的普适行为。此前对于多临界Ising基态在弱测量下的重整化群流结构尚缺乏系统理解。

Method: 采用有限尺寸重整化群交叉分析（finite-size RG crossover analyses），利用纠缠有效中心荷（entanglement effective central charge）、有效Affleck-Ludwig边界熵（effective Affleck-Ludwig boundary entropy）以及测量平均关联函数矩的多分形（multifractality）特征来刻画普适性质。

Result: 临界Ising模型存在直接流向投影测量不动点（projective-measurement fixed point）的重整化群流，伴随面积律纠缠；三临界Ising模型则存在弱测量不动点（weak-measurement fixed point），伴随对数纠缠。两种情况下均发现由Born规则随机性支配的不动点。

Conclusion: 研究澄清了弱测量多临界Ising基态的重整化群流结构，表明内在测量诱导随机性能够在测量后系综中生成复杂而丰富的普适长程标度行为，且可通过受控解析RG与数值有限尺寸RG交叉分析获取。

Abstract: We study post-measurement ensembles of ground states of tricritical and critical 1D quantum Ising Hamiltonians subjected, respectively, to weak energy and spin measurements without post-selection. These measurements act as relevant perturbations about the unmeasured critical ground states. Using finite-size renormalization group (RG) crossover analyses, we characterize their universal properties through the entanglement effective central charge, effective Affleck-Ludwig boundary entropy, and signatures of multifractality from moments of measurement-averaged correlation functions. In both cases, we find evidence for "measurement-dominated" or "measurement-altered" fixed points governed by the underlying Born-rule randomness. For critical Ising, we find a direct RG flow to a projective-measurement fixed point with area-law entanglement, whereas for the tricritical Ising model, we find evidence for a weak-measurement fixed point with logarithmic entanglement. These results clarify the RG-flow structure of weakly measured multicritical Ising ground states and show how intrinsic measurement-induced randomness can generate complex and rich universal long-distance scaling behavior in the post-measurement ensembles, accessible to controlled analytical RG and numerical finite-size RG crossover analyses.

</details>


### [106] [Fractal deconfinement and confinement in Sierpinski ice](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.02741)
*James Walkling,Roderich Moessner*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 本文研究了定义在谢尔宾斯基垫片（Sierpinski gasket）——一个配位数为4、豪斯多夫维数（Hausdorff dimension）为 d_f=log₂3 的分层分形结构——上的六顶点模型（six-vertex model）。通过递归计算配分函数和相关函数，作者得到了包含多个不同机制的丰富相图：冰模型表现出熵性电荷禁闭（entropic charge confinement），而四顶点极限则表现出分形退禁闭（fractal deconfinement），连接退禁闭电荷的弦本身是分形维数 d_l≈1.3 的统计分形。最后，作者提出了人工自旋冰（artificial spin ice）的实验装置设想。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 维度对长波行为（long-wavelength behavior）有着关键影响，而谢尔宾斯基垫片的豪斯多夫维数介于整数维度之间，为研究维度效应对此类模型的作用提供了独特平台。作者希望理解在非整数维度下，六顶点模型中的关联性和电荷禁闭行为如何随顶点权重变化而改变。

Method: 作者利用分形的自相似层级结构，递归地计算配分函数和相关函数，从而系统地扫描不同顶点权重参数空间并绘制相图。具体地，比较等权重的冰模型（ice model）与特定四顶点极限下的行为差异，并通过分析连接电荷的弦的几何性质来量化退禁闭现象的统计特性。

Result: 获得了包含多个不同物理机制的丰富相图。冰模型（等权重）表现出熵驱动的电荷禁闭；而特定的四顶点极限则实现了分形退禁闭，其中连接退禁闭电荷的弦自身是一个统计分形，其分形维数为 d_l=log₂(5/2)≈1.3。

Conclusion: 谢尔宾斯基垫片上的六顶点模型呈现维度依赖的丰富物理现象，其禁闭-退禁闭转变行为与整数维度系统显著不同。作者提出将其构建为人工自旋冰装置，以期为谢尔宾斯基冰及其推广形式中的丰富现象学提供实验研究途径。

Abstract: We study the six-vertex model on the Sierpinski gasket, a four-coordinated hierarchical fractal with Hausdorff dimension $d_f=\log_23$. Given the importance of dimensionality for the long-wavelength behavior of such models, we specifically consider correlations and confinement as a function of the vertex weights, with the equal-weight point corresponding to the ice model. We calculate the partition function and correlators recursively to obtain a rich phase diagram hosting many different regimes. While the ice model shows entropic charge confinement, a particular four-vertex limit exhibits fractal deconfinement, with the string joining the deconfined charges itself a statistical fractal with fractal dimension $d_l=\log_2(5/2)\approx 1.3$. Finally, we propose a setup as an artificial spin ice to enable experimental study of the rich phenomenology of Sierpinski ice and its generalisations.

</details>


### [107] [Two-Parameter Ansatz for the Violation of Eigenstate Thermalization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.02744)
*Kohei Ohgane,Lev Vidmar*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 本文提出一个双参数拟设（two-parameter ansatz）来描述本征态热化假说（ETH）违逆的连续过渡，统一并区分了两种机制：淡出遍历性（fading ergodicity）和束缚遍历性（trapped ergodicity）。在自旋-1/2 J₁-J₂链中，无论存在无序还是线性势，观测到的ETH违逆均与束缚遍历性一致。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 本征态热化假说（eigenstate thermalization hypothesis, ETH）是理解孤立多体系统中量子热化和遍历性的主流框架，但目前缺乏一般性理论来描述ETH从常规成立到完全失效之间的连续过渡过程。作者希望建立一个统一框架，以刻画并区分ETH违逆的不同机制。

Method: 作者引入了一个包含两个参数的双参数拟设（two-parameter ansatz）来描述ETH违逆，该拟设统一并区分了两种机制：淡出遍历性（fading ergodicity）对应已知的遍历性破缺路径，而束缚遍历性（trapped ergodicity）描述了一种新场景——ETH在有限系统中被违逆，但在热力学极限（thermodynamic limit）下恢复。作者将该框架应用于自旋-1/2 J₁-J₂链，并分别引入on-site无序和线性势进行检验。

Result: 在带有点内无序（on-site disorder）和线性势（linear potential）的两种自旋-1/2 J₁-J₂链情形中，观察到的ETH违逆均与束缚遍历性（trapped ergodicity）机制一致，而非传统上认为的淡出遍历性（fading ergodicity）机制。

Conclusion: 所提出的双参数框架能够统一描述ETH违逆的连续发生过程，并区分不同的违逆机制。实验验证表明，在无序和线性势两种情形下，系统表现出的ETH违逆均属于束缚遍历性，即ETH违逆是有限尺寸效应，最终在热力学极限下恢复。

Abstract: The eigenstate thermalization hypothesis (ETH) provides the prevailing framework for understanding quantum thermalization and ergodicity in isolated many-body systems. Yet, no general theory describes the continuous onset of ETH violation between the conventional ETH and its complete breakdown. Here, we introduce a two-parameter ansatz for the ETH violation that unifies and distinguishes two mechanisms: fading ergodicity and trapped ergodicity. While fading ergodicity captures the established route to ergodicity breaking, trapped ergodicity describes a distinct scenario in which ETH is violated in finite systems but restored in the thermodynamic limit. We test this framework in the spin-1/2 $J_1$-$J_2$ chains with on-site disorder and linear potential. In both cases, we find that the observed ETH violation is consistent with trapped ergodicity.

</details>


### [108] [Two dimensional inhomogeneous classical systems at criticality](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.02903)
*Jean-Marie Stéphan*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 本文研究二维经典格点模型在缓慢空间非均匀形变下的临界行为，发现此类系统在大尺度上可由弯曲空间中的共形场论（conformal field theory, CFT）描述，并通过伊辛模型（Ising model）和六顶角模型（six vertex model）两个实例识别了相应的空间度量。对于带畴壁边界条件的六顶角模型，作者利用流体力学求解了自由情形，并在存在相互作用时确定了分隔临界涨落区域与有序（冻结）相的北极线（arctic curve）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 作者旨在理解共形场论描述的二维临界格点模型在缓慢非均匀形变下是否仍保持普适的临界行为，以及如何从几何角度（弯曲空间中的CFT）统一刻画这类系统。

Method: 作者选择在格点尺度上缓慢变化、同时保持临界性质的非均匀形变，探究其大尺度行为，并用伊辛模型和六顶角模型（后者带有非均匀的畴壁边界条件）两个具体实例进行验证。自由情形通过流体力学（hydrodynamics）求解，相互作用情形下则利用相关方法确定北极线。

Result: 作者发现此类系统整体上可由弯曲空间中的共形场论描述，并识别出底层空间度量；对伊辛模型和六顶角模型均给出了相应刻画。六顶角模型在自由情形下通过流体力学求解成功，在有相互作用时确定了分隔临界涨落区域与有序（冻结）相的北极线。

Conclusion: 缓慢非均匀形变的二维临界格点模型可由弯曲空间中的共形场论统一描述，即使边界条件本身非均匀（如畴壁条件），仍可通过流体力学等工具获取精确或半精确的解，从而揭示临界区域与冻结相之间的几何边界。

Abstract: We study simple inhomogeneous deformations of known two-dimensional classical lattice models described by conformal field theory (CFT) at large distances. The deformations are chosen to vary slowly at lattice scales, while preserving critical behavior. Globally, we find that such systems are described by a CFT in curved space, and identify the underlying space metric. Our two examples are the Ising model and the six vertex model with domain wall boundary conditions. In the latter boundaries are also inhomogeneous, which complicates the analysis. We nevertheless solve the free case using hydrodynamics. In the presence of interactions we determine the arctic curve which separates a critical fluctuating region from an ordered (frozen) phase.

</details>


### [109] [Exact Resonances Are Not Sufficient for Phonon Energy Diffusion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.03180)
*Wei Lin,Yong Zhang,Hong Zhao*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 本文通过理论分析指出，多声子共振条件（multi-phonon resonance）虽然是声子输运和晶格热化动力学理论的基础，但精确共振匹配、非零相互作用系数和网络连通性并不足以保证持续的能量扩散。对称性强制平衡关系使精确共振碰撞流（collision currents）收敛到非热零通量态（zero-flux states），导致从单个共振集到连通网络均出现动力学停滞（kinetic arrest）。完整能量扩散实由准共振（quasi-resonances）支撑，且热力学极限与弱非线性极限不对易。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 精确共振匹配、非零相互作用系数和网络连通性是否足以保证声子系统的持续能量扩散，是该领域尚未澄清的基本动力学判据问题。

Method: 作者通过对称性分析推导了精确共振碰撞流的平衡关系（balance relations），并从单个共振集扩展到连通网络，同时考察热力学极限与弱非线性极限的交换次序及有限尺寸下的交叉行为。

Result: 精确共振系统在对称性平衡关系约束下收敛至非热零通量态，产生从个体共振集到连通网络的动力学停滞；能量完全扩散依靠准共振维持。在有限尺寸下，热化时间随非线性减弱经高阶交叉（higher-order crossovers）发散，显示两极限不对易。

Conclusion: 精确共振的存在性与网络连通性只是声子能量扩散的动力学前提（kinematic criteria），而非充分条件（sufficient dynamical criteria）；对称性平衡关系决定了精确共振无法驱动持续热化。

Abstract: Multi-phonon resonance conditions underpin kinetic theories of phonon transport and lattice thermalization. We show that exact resonance matching, nonzero interaction coefficients, and network connectivity do not guarantee persistent energy diffusion. Symmetry-enforced balance relations drive exact-resonant collision currents to nonthermal zero-flux states, producing kinetic arrest from individual resonant sets to connected networks. Complete energy spreading is sustained by quasi-resonances. The thermodynamic and weak-nonlinearity limits do not commute: the leading kinetic behavior is recovered in the former, whereas at fixed finite size the thermalization time diverges through higher-order crossovers as the nonlinearity vanishes. Exact-resonance existence and connectivity are therefore kinematic, not sufficient dynamical, criteria for phonon energy diffusion.

</details>


### [110] [Fibonacci number systems and the localization criterion in the many-body Aubry-André model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.03458)
*Balázs Hetényi*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 本文引入了一种称为分数斐波那契数系（fractional Fibonacci number system）的扩展数系，它在用于自然数的斐波那契数系与用于表示实数的无理基数φ数系（base-φ number system）之间进行插值。该数系用于解释多体无相互作用的Aubry-André模型的局域化相图。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 作者旨在发展一种统一的数系框架，既涵盖整数表示又能推广到实数表示，并将其应用于多体无相互作用Aubry-André模型（many-body non-interacting Aubry-André model）的局域化（localization）相图解释，填补传统数系在该物理问题应用上的空白。

Method: 作者构造了分数斐波那契数系，使其在自然数情形退化为标准斐波那契数系，在实数情形退化为基φ数系。对有限系统尺寸，将粒子密度在该数系下展开以获得局域化判据；在热力学极限下，粒子密度改用基φ数系表示以保持判据成立。

Result: 在有限系统尺寸下，局域化判据可通过分数斐波那契数系中粒子密度的表示获得；在热力学极限（thermodynamic limit）下，该判据依然保持有效，但粒子密度改用基φ数系表达。

Conclusion: 分数斐波那契数系为自然数与实数表示提供了统一的插值框架，并成功应用于Aubry-André模型的局域化分析；同时论文对热力学极限的性质进行了讨论。

Abstract: We introduce an extension of the Fibonacci number system, we call the fractional Fibonacci number system, which interpolates between the Fibonacci number system (used for natural numbers) and the irrational base-$φ$ number system, which can be used to represent real numbers. The number system finds its use in interpreting the localization phase diagram of the many-body non-interacting Aubry-André model. For finite system sizes a localization criterion can be obtained if the particle density is written in the fractional Fibonacci number system. In the thermodynamic limit, the criterion remains, but in this case the particle density is expressed in base-$φ$. The nature of the thermodynamic limit is also discussed.

</details>


### [111] [Discrepancy between the H-Function and Entropy: Insights into the rigorously established Boltzmann equation for hard-sphere gases](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.03355)
*Li-Xiang Cen*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 本文通过考察H函数（H-function）与熵（entropy）在演化上的差异，阐明在玻尔兹曼-格拉德极限（Boltzmann-Grad limit）下严格推导的硬球气体玻尔兹曼方程中，H函数不能作为有效的时间箭头（arrow of time）判据。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 旨在澄清玻尔兹曼方程中时间箭头问题的理论基础，揭示H函数作为时间方向判据的有效性边界。

Method: 通过对比分析H函数与熵在演化行为上的差异，考察硬球气体模型在玻尔兹曼-格拉德极限下严格推导的玻尔兹曼方程中H函数的性质。

Result: 发现H函数与熵的演化存在不一致性，H函数在该严格框架下不能充当时间箭头的有效判据。

Conclusion: H函数在硬球气体玻尔兹曼方程的严格推导中失效，表明需要重新审视统计力学（statistical mechanics）中时间箭头的理论判据。

Abstract: By investigating the origin of the evolutionary discrepancy between the H-function and entropy, we elucidate that the H-function fails to serve as a valid arrow-of-time criterion in the rigorously derived Boltzmann equation for the hard-sphere gas model under the Boltzmann-Grad limit.

</details>


### [112] [The intermediate scattering function of an interacting adlayer as a characteristic function: a closed-form theory of Ising lattice-gas surface diffusion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.03398)
*S. Miret-Artés*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 本文利用氦自旋回波（helium spin-echo）测量的中间散射函数（ISF）作为特征函数（CF）的双重角色——其零时刻值是静态结构因子，将伊辛晶格气体（Ising lattice-gas）型横向相互作用引入表面扩散，推导出动量、时间、覆盖度和温度的闭式解，并通过一系列相关性指纹反演相互作用参数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 表面扩散中横向相互作用对动力学的影响此前缺乏简洁统一的处理方式。作者利用ISF同时作为特征函数和结构因子的性质，希望将平衡结构信息与相互作用重整化的跳跃率联系起来，从而用单一关联参数统一描述平衡与非平衡性质。

Method: 将主方程（master equation）投影到密度上得到精确运动方程，其振幅为恒等式、初始速率满足求和规则；通过舍弃记忆核（memory kernel）得到闭式ISF解。一维沟道中精确求解，扩展到表面时引入最近邻关联的对近似（pair approximation），结构因子动量依赖则由集体（随机相位）处理给出。采用动力学蒙特卡洛（KMC）在同一哈密顿量上验证解析结果。

Result: 闭式ISF解析解覆盖动量转移、时间、覆盖度和温度，可生成完整的线性响应层级。KMC验证表明：重整化跳跃率误差小于约1%，初始集体速率误差为1—2%。给出了四个相互关联的非临界指纹：准弹性振幅、线宽、Arrhenius斜率及拟合线宽对拟合时间范围的依赖，这些量可闭式反演出尝试频率、静态势垒和相互作用。de Gennes窄化从类比变为定理。应用于Na/Cu(111)和H/Pt(111)体系并进行了分析讨论。

Conclusion: 该闭式框架为从表面扩散实验数据中系统反演横向相互作用提供了理论工具，并验证了相互作用导致的de Gennes窄化等非平庸效应，为理解表面吸附动力学提供了统一视角。

Abstract: The intermediate scattering function (ISF) measured by helium spin-echo is a characteristic function (CF): the Fourier transform of the distribution of adsorbate displacements, whose value at zero time is the static structure factor. We use this double role to bring lateral interactions of Ising lattice-gas type into surface diffusion. The ISF then follows in closed form in momentum transfer, time, coverage and temperature, and with it the whole linear-response hierarchy. A single correlation parameter fixes both the equilibrium structure and the interaction-renormalized hop rate; it is exact along a one-dimensional channel, and is carried onto the surface as the nearest-neighbour correlation of a pair approximation, the momentum dependence of the structure factor then coming from a collective (random-phase) treatment. The closed form is not postulated. Projecting the master equation onto the density gives an exact equation of motion whose amplitude is an identity and whose initial rate obeys a sum rule, so that the quasi-elastic weight is the layer's own structure factor and de Gennes narrowing becomes a theorem rather than an analogy; the closed form is what remains when the memory kernel of that equation is dropped. Kinetic Monte Carlo (KMC) on the same Hamiltonian reproduces the dressed hop rate to better than about one per cent and the initial collective rate to $1$--$2\%$. Four correlated, non-critical fingerprints of the interaction follow --- in the quasi-elastic amplitude, its linewidth, the Arrhenius slope, and the dependence of the fitted linewidth on the range of times over which the decay is fitted. They invert in closed form for the attempt frequency, the static barrier and the interaction. Applications to Na/Cu(111) and H/Pt(111) are analysed and discussed.

</details>


### [113] [Lower bounds on entropy production from dynamical correlation functions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.03619)
*Paul Raux,Alexander M. Maier,Udo Seifert*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 本文针对部分观测和粗粒化系统中熵产生（entropy production）的推断问题，推导了基于粗粒化状态观测量两时间相关函数（two-time correlation functions）不对称性的两种下界。对非平衡稳态（non-equilibrium steady states），该下界对任意相关时间滞后均成立；对含时过程，则需要在滞后趋近于零的极限下成立。该下界适用于底层存在马尔可夫动力学或耦合过阻尼朗之万方程的系统。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 熵产生是随机热力学（stochastic thermodynamics）中的关键量，但在部分观测和粗粒化系统中其推断十分困难，通常依赖于已被证明的下界。现有方法对此类系统的熵产生估计仍存在局限性，需要更普适且便于实验获取的推断途径。

Method: 作者利用实验可获取的粗粒化状态观测量（coarse-grained state observables）的两时间相关函数的不对称性，推导出两类熵产生下界。第一类适用于非平衡稳态，对任意相关滞后均有效；第二类适用于含时过程，需在滞后趋于零的极限下成立。两种下界均对底层具有马尔可夫动力学或耦合过阻尼朗之万方程（overdamped Langevin equations）的任何系统成立。

Result: 作者对两类动力学（马尔可夫过程与过阻尼朗之万方程）分别展示了所推导下界的有效性，并对下界的优化方式和潜在紧致性（tightness）进行了讨论。

Conclusion: 该工作为部分观测与粗粒化系统的熵产生估计提供了一种基于两时间相关函数不对称性的新下界框架，适用于广泛的动力学类型，为实验推断熵产生提供了可行的途径。

Abstract: Entropy production is a key property in stochastic thermodynamics. For partially observed and coarse-grained systems, its inference is challenging and typically rests on proven lower bounds. We derive two versions of such bounds based on the asymmetry of experimentally accessible two-time correlation functions of coarse-grained state observables. For non-equilibrium steady states, the bound is valid for arbitrary correlation lag. For time-dependent processes, it requires the limit of vanishing lag. These bounds hold true for any system that follows either a Markovian dynamics or a coupled set of overdamped Langevin equations on some underlying, unobservable level of description. We illustrate the bounds for both types of dynamics and discuss their optimization and potential tightness.

</details>


### [114] [Kappa distributions as asymptotic marginals of exponential family ensembles](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.03960)
*Sergio Davis*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 本文基于近期在常微观正则热容的驱动经典系统中超统计（superstatistical）行为涌现的结果，证明在总动能服从逆伽马分布的 N 粒子系统中，当 N 趋于无穷时，单粒子速度必然服从 kappa 分布，并为蒙特卡洛 Metropolis 模拟生成 kappa 速度提供了实用方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 近期工作（Physica A 660 130370, 2025）已在恒定微观正则热容的驱动经典系统中证明了超统计行为的涌现。本文旨在将此结果应用于粒子系统，以揭示 kappa 分布在非广延统计力学（nonextensive statistical mechanics）框架之外的本质，从而深化对 kappa 分布起源的理解。

Method: 作者从超统计行为在驱动经典系统中的涌现结果出发，考虑一个总动能服从逆伽马（inverse gamma）分布的 N 粒子系统，在 N 趋于无穷的极限下推导单粒子速度分布。此外，提出利用蒙特卡洛 Metropolis（Monte Carlo Metropolis）模拟在逆伽马系综中生成 kappa 速度的实用方法。

Result: 证明了当系统总动能服从逆伽马分布时，在 N 趋于无穷的极限下，单粒子速度必然呈现 kappa 分布，并提供了在逆伽马系综中通过蒙特卡洛 Metropolis 模拟生成 kappa 速度的实用方案。

Conclusion: 该结果在非广延统计力学的理论框架之外为 kappa 分布的来源提供了新的理解途径，同时为数值模拟中高效生成 kappa 分布速度提供了实用工具。

Abstract: Recently (Physica A 660 130370, 2025), emergence of superstatistical behavior in driven classical systems has been shown for systems with constant microcanonical heat capacity. As an application of this result, in this work we show that a system of $N$ particles with inverse gamma distribution of total kinetic energies must have kappa-distributed single-particle velocities in the limit $N \rightarrow \infty$. Our results provide insight into the nature of kappa distributions outside the theory of nonextensive statistical mechanics, while also bringing forward a practical method for the generation of kappa velocities via Monte Carlo Metropolis simulation in the inverse gamma ensemble.

</details>

### [115] [1+1d Lattice Dirac Fermions from Non-Onsite Vector and Axial Symmetries](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.02722)
*Tabin Dharanikota,Lukasz Fidkowski*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 本文构建了一个精确可解的哈密顿量格子模型，实现了具有精确微观矢量与轴矢U(1)对称性的1+1维狄拉克费米子（Dirac fermion），其中混合反常由对称性的非局域（not-on-site）作用承载。通过局域保持幺正映射到费米子Villain希尔伯特空间后，哈密顿量可精确求解，并可推广至相互作用费米子Luttinger液体的精确可解实现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在格子模型中实现具有精确连续对称性及其混合反常的费米子系统是凝聚态理论中的核心挑战，尤其需要将量子反常正确编码到微观模型中，同时保持模型可解性以进行深入分析。

Method: 作者构造了一个Z₂分级的张力积希尔伯特空间（包含无限维转子自由度），使得模型具有精确的微观矢量与轴矢U(1)对称性；随后通过局域保持幺正映射（locality-preserving unitary mapping）将其等价地映射到费米子Villain希尔伯特空间，使得哈密顿量变为精确可解形式；此外还计算了自由狄拉克不动点处的不可约相互作用至领头阶。

Result: 该构造成功实现了精确可解的1+1d狄拉克费米子格子模型，其混合反常（mixed anomaly）由对称性的非局域作用承载；该框架还可进一步实现相互作用费米子Luttinger液体（Luttinger liquids）的精确可解版本；在自由狄拉克不动点处，哈密顿量包含不可约相互作用，其领头阶已被计算。

Conclusion: 该工作为在格子上实现具有精确反常结构的费米子系统提供了一种全新的精确可解框架，为研究反常对称性（anomalous symmetries）与强关联费米子行为之间的相互作用建立了新的理论工具。

Abstract: We construct an exactly solvable Hamiltonian lattice model realizing a 1+1d Dirac fermion, with exact microscopic vector and axial vector $U(1)$ symmetries. The mixed anomaly between these is accommodated by the not-on-site action of the symmetries. Our Hilbert space is a $Z_2$-graded tensor product of local $Z_2$-graded Hilbert spaces which include infinite dimensional rotor degrees of freedom. The Hamiltonian becomes manifestly exactly solvable after a locality-preserving unitary mapping to an equivalent fermionic Villain Hilbert space. Our construction also allows an exactly solvable realization of interacting fermionic Luttinger liquids. At the free Dirac fixed point, our Hamiltonian contains irrelevant interactions, which we compute to leading order.

</details>


### [116] [Spin-charge separation in the triangular-lattice Hofstadter-Hubbard model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.02727)
*Yuntian Gu,Hui Yang,Zhehao Dai,Yantao Wu*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 本文利用变分蒙特卡洛（variational Monte Carlo）结合神经量子态（neural quantum states, NQS）和投影纠缠对态（projected entangled pair states, PEPS）两种计算方法，对三角晶格Hofstadter-Hubbard模型中的手性自旋液体（chiral spin liquid）相进行了研究。在真实空间中通过数值自旋泵浦模拟和实时自旋及电荷运动观察到自旋-电荷分离（spin-charge separation），并研究了该模型中推测存在的任意子超导（anyonic superconductivity）相关性质。工作展示了NQS和PEPS作为可以相互交叉验证的强有力工具，用于诊断强关联电子系统中的拓扑序（topological order）和分数化激发（fractionalized excitations）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 摩尔材料（moiré materials）中的近期实验已能实现多种新奇量子相，Hofstadter-Hubbard模型被提出作为承载手性自旋液体的潜在平台。与此同时，二维多体费米子系统的计算方法取得了重大进展，使得在真实二维几何中数值研究这一具有挑战性的模型成为可能。

Method: 采用变分蒙特卡洛与神经量子态（NQS）以及投影纠缠对态（PEPS）两种方法，在三角晶格Hofstadter-Hubbard模型上研究可能的手性自旋液体相。通过数值自旋泵浦模拟以及实时自旋和电荷运动的观测，在实空间中直接探测自旋-电荷分离现象。

Result: 在实空间中直接观察到自旋-电荷分离现象。对于该模型中推测的任意子超导，在小尺寸上发现正的两电子束缚能（two-electron binding energy），但随系统尺寸增大该能量降至数值分辨率以下。

Conclusion: NQS和PEPS被证明是能够相互交叉验证的强有力工具，可用于诊断强关联电子系统中的拓扑序和分数化激发。本研究为Hofstadter-Hubbard模型中手性自旋液体相的存在提供了数值证据，并阐明了任意子超导在该体系中的尺寸依赖行为。

Abstract: Recent experiments in moiré materials have enabled the realization of a variety of exotic quantum phases. In this context, the Hofstadter-Hubbard model has been proposed as a possible setting for hosting chiral spin liquid. Concurrently, significant progress has been recently made in the computational methods for two-dimensional many-body fermion systems, which makes numerically studying this challenging model a real possibility in genuine 2D geometry. Motivated by these advances, we investigate the putative chiral spin liquid phase in the triangular-lattice Hofstadter-Hubbard model using variational Monte Carlo with neural quantum states (NQS) and projected entangled pair states (PEPS). We observe spin-charge separation directly in real space through numerical spin-pumping simulation and real-time spin and charge motion. In addition, in the context of anyonic superconductivity conjectured in this model, we find a positive two-electron binding energy on small systems, but it decreases below our numerical resolution as the system size increases. Our work demonstrates NQS and PEPS as powerful tools, capable of cross-checking each other, for diagnosing topological order and fractionalized excitations in strongly correlated electronic systems.

</details>


### [117] [Particle-Vortex Duality of Hydrodynamics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.02732)
*Cenke Xu,Matthew P. A. Fisher*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 本文利用混合态物质新近识别的对称结构及"强-弱自发对称破缺"（SW-SSB），为二维玻色子/转子体系发展出量子粒子-涡旋对偶（quantum particle-vortex duality），将经典流体力学与磁流体力学（magnetohydrodynamics）联系起来。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 作者旨在利用混合态物质中最近识别的对称性结构，建立二维玻色子体系流体力学描述中的对偶关系，从而在物质流体力学与磁流体力学之间架起桥梁。

Method: 基于"强-弱自发对称破缺"（SW-SSB）框架，构建二维玻色子/转子体系中涌现的模型F和模型A流体力学（hydrodynamics）的量子粒子-涡旋对偶理论；该对偶同时体现电荷对称性U(1)_c、对偶的涌现1-形式对称性U(1)_e^{(1)}以及涡旋守恒对应的U(1)_v对称性。

Result: 展示了二维玻色子经典流体力学既可由电荷对称性U(1)_c描述，也可等价地由对偶（涌现）1-形式对称性U(1)_e^{(1)}及涡旋守恒相关的U(1)_v来描述，确立了三种对称性描述之间的等价性。

Conclusion: 该对偶关系为物质流体力学与磁流体力学之间提供了桥梁，表明二者可由统一的对称性框架加以描述。

Abstract: Equipped with the recently recognized symmetry structure of mixed states of matter and "strong-weak spontaneous symmetry breaking" (SW-SSB), we develop a quantum particle-vortex duality of emergent model-F and model-A hydrodynamics of 2d boson/rotor systems. This duality relation demonstrates that classical hydrodynamics of 2d bosons can be described in terms of the charge symmetry $U(1)_c$, but also equivalently in terms of the dual (emergent) 1-form symmetry $U(1)^{(1)}_e$, as well as $U(1)_v$ associated with the conservation of vortices. This duality provides a bridge between the hydrodynamics of matter and magnetohydrodynamics.

</details>


### [118] [Large scale neural quantum states reveal the interplay between superconductivity and quantum criticality in the Hofstadter-Hubbard model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.02753)
*Christopher Roth,Andrew Millis,Tomohiro Soejima*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 本文利用神经网络量子态（neural quantum states）在多达432个格点的三角格子Hofstadter-Hubbard模型上研究母体绝缘态如何塑造掺杂后的超导电性。研究发现弱耦合整数量子霍尔绝缘体与中等耦合手性自旋液体之间的拓扑相变为连续相变，掺杂后两侧均出现具有d+id配对的长程离域有序的拓扑超导体，且超流刚度在临界点附近显著增强。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解母体绝缘态如何决定掺杂后涌现的超导性是自Anderson共振价键（resonating-valence-bond）理论以来的长期问题。三角格子Hofstadter-Hubbard模型在π/2通量下为研究该问题提供了理想平台，因为它拥有两个不同的母体态，但此前对这一拓扑相变及其掺杂行为的本质缺乏清晰认识。

Method: 采用神经网络量子态在多达432个格点的环面（torus）上进行大规模数值模拟，研究半填充时的相变性质，并通过掺杂系统地分析超导序参量、超流刚度和电荷能隙等关键物理量在相变两侧的行为。

Result: 提供了相变连续的有力证据，表现为2e电荷能隙消失和临界电荷涨落（critical charge fluctuations）。掺杂后发现两侧均存在具有离域长程序（off-diagonal long-range order）的拓扑超导体，与d+id配对一致。配对序参量在相变两侧几乎不变，而超流刚度在临界点附近显著增强。

Conclusion: 超导体的能量尺度并非由具体掺杂哪一种母体态决定，而是由与母体态之间相变的邻近性决定。该研究确立了神经网络量子态作为理解长程电子关联与超导之间微妙相互作用的有力工具。

Abstract: Understanding how a parent insulating state shapes the superconductivity that emerges upon doping is a long-standing problem dating back to Anderson's resonating-valence-bond proposal. The triangular-lattice Hofstadter-Hubbard model with $π/2$ flux per plaquette offers an ideal setting for this question: at half filling it hosts two distinct parent states---an integer quantum Hall insulator at weak coupling and a chiral spin liquid at intermediate coupling---separated by a topological phase transition. Using neural quantum states on tori of up to $432$ sites, we present strong evidence that the transition is continuous, with a vanishing $2e$ charge gap and critical charge fluctuations. Upon doping, we find a topological superconductor with off-diagonal long-range order consistent with $d+id$ pairing on either side of the transition. The two ingredients of superconductivity, pair formation and phase coherence, respond to the parent state in sharply different ways: while the pairing order parameter remains nearly unchanged across the transition, the superfluid stiffness is strongly enhanced near the critical point. The energy scale of the superconductor is therefore set not by which parent state is doped, but by proximity to the transition between them. Our results establish neural quantum states as a powerful tool for understanding the delicate interplay between long-ranged electronic correlations and superconductivity.

</details>


### [119] [Tunable Skyrmions in a Topological Wigner Crystal](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.02832)
*David A. Dahlbom,Daniel Kaplan,Premala Chandra,Cristian D. Batista*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 本文研究了低密度强相互作用系统中电子形成维格纳晶体（Wigner crystal）的拓扑性质。通过在三角晶格上建立赝自旋相互作用模型，作者发现铁磁海森堡交换作用与手性相互作用之间的竞争能够稳定一种零磁场下的斯格明子晶体（skyrmion crystal），其密度可通过手性耦合比γ/J连续调节。在大手性耦合极限下，作者还发现了一种24位点的四斯格明子晶体相，并建立了铁磁拓扑维格纳晶体的完整相图。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 近年来，二维体系中携带贝里曲率（Berry curvature）的电子为维格纳结晶引入了拓扑维度。作者希望通过研究拓扑维格纳晶体中赝自旋相互作用的竞争机制，探索零磁场下可调密度的斯格明子晶体等新奇拓扑物态，并建立系统的相图。

Method: 作者采用三角晶格上赝自旋相互作用的唯象模型，考虑铁磁海森堡交换作用J与手性相互作用γ之间的竞争。手性相互作用源于底层时间反演破缺的维格纳晶体中有限贝里曲率。在连续极限下，作者将手性相互作用视为斯格明子的化学势，并且超越非线性σ模型（nonlinear sigma model）的高阶梯度项用来选择斯格明子密度。作者还分析了这些相的磁振子（magnon）结构。

Result: 研究发现在铁磁交换J与手性相互作用γ的竞争下，三角晶格上可稳定零磁场斯格明子晶体，其密度可通过γ/J比值连续调节。在大手性耦合极限下，发现了携带每个磁原胞四单位拓扑电荷的24位点四斯格明子晶体，该相在J→0时与四子晶格四面体序简并。研究还表征了这些相的磁振子结构，并讨论了可调斯格明子晶体到这些大γ/J态之间的转变。

Conclusion: 本文建立了铁磁拓扑维格纳晶体的相图，揭示了贝里曲率如何通过手性相互作用在零磁场下驱动可调密度斯格明子晶体的形成，以及该相与四斯格明子晶体和四面体序等大耦合极限态之间的转变关系。

Abstract: In low density systems with strong interactions electrons are expected to crystallize into a Wigner solid. Recently, advances in two-dimensional systems where electrons carry Berry curvature have added a topological dimension to Wigner crystallization. Using a model for pseudospin interactions in a topological Wigner crystal, here we show that the competition between ferromagnetic Heisenberg exchange $J$ and the chiral interaction $γ$ on the triangular lattice stabilizes a zero-field skyrmion crystal whose density is continuously tunable through the ratio $γ/J$. The chiral interaction originates from finite Berry curvature in an underlying time-reversal broken Wigner crystal. In the continuum limit, the chiral interaction acts as a chemical potential for skyrmions, while higher-order gradient terms beyond the nonlinear sigma model select the skyrmion density. At large chiral coupling, we uncover a 24-site tetra-skyrmion crystal carrying four units of topological charge per magnetic unit cell, which becomes degenerate with four-sublattice tetrahedral order as $J \to 0$. We characterize the magnon structure for these phases and discuss the transition between the tunable skyrmion crystal to these states at large $γ/J$. Our results establish the phase diagram of ferromagnetic topological Wigner crystals.

</details>


### [120] [Scale-covariant liquid in nonlocal high Tc strange metals](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.03013)
*Jian Xian Sim*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 本文提出一种解释高温超导体中节点尺度协变自能的新机制：由屏蔽不完全的有效排斥势的非局域性所驱动，其指数随掺杂连续变化，无需量子临界性即可产生尺度协变现象。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 近年高温超导体实验发现节点尺度协变自能(scale-covariant self-energy)的指数随掺杂连续变化，这一现象缺乏令人满意的理论解释。作者旨在提出一个不依赖量子临界性(quantum criticality)的替代机制。

Method: 提出被屏蔽不良的有效排斥相互作用 $V_α(r) \sim 1/r^α$ 的非局域性机制，其中掺杂依赖的指数 $1 \le α\le 3$ 在Mott绝缘体和费米液体(Fermi liquid)极限之间自然插值。在此基础上发展流体动力学屏蔽(hydrodynamic screening)现象学，推导尺度协变的准粒子衰减率 $Γ(ω,T) \propto T^γ Φ(ω/T)$，其中 $\gamma = 2 - 1/α$ 适用于非局域区间 $1 < α< 2$。

Result: 理论自然捕捉了最佳掺杂到过掺杂区间，而欠掺杂区在定性上有所不同。该理论中，光谱拟合得到的指数直接反映带电流体的有效空间非局域性，从而将实验可观测指数与系统的非局域度联系起来。

Conclusion: 作者认为非局域性可产生尺度协变现象，说明量子临界性并非唯一或必要的解释途径；该机制为高温超导体中掺杂相关尺度协变行为提供了新的理论框架。

Abstract: Experiments in recent years on high $T_c$ superconductors find a puzzling nodal scale-covariant self-energy with an exponent varying continuously with doping. We propose a mechanism: nonlocality induced by poorly screened effective repulsions $V_α(r) \sim 1/r^α$, where a continuously doping-dependent exponent $1 \le α\le 3$ naturally interpolates between the Mott insulating and Fermi liquid limits. We develop a phenomenology of hydrodynamic screening, finding a scale-covariant quasiparticle decay rate $Γ(ω,T) \propto T^γ Φ(ω/T)$ in energy $ω$ and temperature $T$, with $γ= 2-\frac{1}α$ for nonlocal $ 1 < α< 2$. Our results naturally capture the optimally doped to overdoped regimes, whereas the underdoped regime is qualitatively distinct. In our theory, spectroscopy-fitted exponents directly probe the charged fluid's effective spatial nonlocality. Nonlocality shows that quantum criticality is not necessary to explain scale-covariant phenomena.

</details>


### [121] [Exotic superconductivity in the doped Kitaev quantum spin liquid](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.03024)
*Takahiro Misawa,Kota Ido*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 本文采用多变量变分蒙特卡洛方法研究掺杂 $t$-$J$ 型 Kitaev 模型中的超导行为，发现铁磁 Kitaev 相互作用下存在与铁磁共存的三重态 $p$-波超导，反铁磁相互作用下则出现从自旋依赖三重态 $p$-波到单重态 $d+id$ 的配对对称性转变。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究载流子掺杂的 Kitaev 量子自旋液体中非常规超导的稳定性与配对机制，为在 Kitaev 候选材料中实验搜寻超导提供理论基础。

Method: 对空穴掺杂的 $t$-$J$ 型 Kitaev 模型应用多变量变分蒙特卡洛方法（many-variable variational Monte Carlo），使用可精确表示 Kitaev 量子自旋液体的投影配对积波函数，在各向同性二维团簇上检验超导相的稳定性。

Result: 铁磁 Kitaev 相互作用下，低至中等掺杂区间内稳健的三重态 $p$-波超导与铁磁共存，但在逼近完全极化铁磁态时被抑制；反铁磁相互作用下，超导主导配对对称性从低掺杂的自旋依赖三重态 $p$-波转变为中等掺杂的单重态 $d+id$。固定掺杂时改变铁磁 Kitaev 相互作用强度，三重态超导随铁磁矩增大而增强，在略低于完全极化处达到最强。

Conclusion: 研究为在载流子掺杂的 Kitaev 候选材料中实验搜寻与铁磁共存的非常规（如三重态）超导提供了理论依据。

Abstract: We investigate superconductivity in a doped Kitaev quantum spin liquid by applying the many-variable variational Monte Carlo method to the hole-doped $t$-$J$-type Kitaev model. Using a projected pair-product wave function that can exactly represent the Kitaev quantum spin liquid, we examine the stability of superconducting phases on isotropic two-dimensional clusters. For the ferromagnetic Kitaev interaction, robust triplet $p$-wave superconductivity coexists with ferromagnetism in the low-to-intermediate doping regime but is suppressed as the system approaches the fully polarized ferromagnetic phase. For the antiferromagnetic Kitaev interaction, superconductivity exhibits a change in the dominant pairing symmetry from spin-dependent triplet $p$-wave at low doping to singlet $d+id$ at intermediate doping. By varying the strength of the ferromagnetic Kitaev interaction at fixed doping, we show that the triplet superconductivity increases together with the ferromagnetic moment and becomes strongest slightly below full polarization. Our results provide a theoretical basis for experimental searches for unconventional superconductivity, such as triplet superconductivity coexisting with ferromagnetism, in carrier-doped Kitaev candidate materials.

</details>


### [122] [Contrasting anisotropic electron-phonon-spin coupling in Fe$_{3}$GeTe$_{2}$ and Fe$_{5}$GeTe$_{2}$: A helicity-resolved Raman study](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.03193)
*Smrutiranjan Mekap,Jyoti Saini,Andrzej Ptok,Pawan Kumar Srivastava,Changgu Lee,Subhasis Ghosh,Anushree Roy*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 本文通过波长和温度依赖的螺旋度分辨拉曼光谱（helicity-resolved Raman spectroscopy），对比研究了二维范德华铁磁体Fe₃GeTe₂（F3GT）和Fe₅GeTe₂（F5GT）中光学共振诱导的各向异性电子-声子耦合（electron-phonon coupling）与磁有序的关联，揭示了晶体结构和磁各向异性在塑造各向异性耦合的电子-声子-自旋动力学中的作用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 二维范德华铁磁体Fe₃GeTe₂和Fe₅GeTe₂表现出显著的磁光响应，为研究晶格、电子和磁自由度之间的相互作用提供了有前景的平台。然而，这些体系中光学共振诱导的各向异性电子-声子耦合及其与磁有序之间的关联尚未被系统阐明。

Method: 采用波长和温度依赖的螺旋度分辨拉曼光谱，分别在左旋和右旋圆偏振激发下分辨双重简并的E模，追踪手性模式劈裂（Δf）随温度的演化；同时利用Fano不对称参数1/q表征声子与电子连续谱之间的耦合强度及角向各向异性。

Result: 手性模式劈裂的温度演化并不跟随磁化强度的行为，表明螺旋度依赖的拉曼响应不仅源于磁有序导致的时间反演对称性破缺，还来自自旋-轨道耦合的电子相互作用。在F3GT中，面外磁化间接调控光学共振下的面内各向异性电子-声子耦合，而F5GT表现出静态的各向异性相互作用。Fano参数1/q揭示了声子与电子连续谱之间模式和温度依赖的耦合强度：F3GT中呈现明显角向各向异性，而F5GT中呈各向同性行为，后者归因于其多个Fe位点和增强的层间杂化。

Conclusion: 晶体结构和磁各向异性在塑造这些层状金属铁磁体中各向异性耦合的电子-声子-自旋动力学中发挥关键作用，FeₓGeTe₂体系为在层状金属铁磁体中实现手性光-物质相互作用的微观洞察提供了多功能平台。

Abstract: Two-dimensional van der Waals ferromagnets Fe$_3$GeTe$ _2$ (F3GT) and Fe$_5$GeTe$_2$ (F5GT) exhibit pronounced magneto-optical responses, which open promising platforms for investigating the interplay among lattice, electronic, and magnetic degrees of freedom. Here, we present a comparative study of optical resonance-induced anisotropic electron-phonon coupling and its association with magnetic ordering in these systems using wavelength- and temperature-dependent helicity-resolved Raman spectroscopy. By resolving the doubly degenerate E modes under left- and right-circularly polarized excitations, we demonstrate that the temperature evolution of the chiral mode splitting ($Δf$) does not track the magnetization behavior, indicating that the helicity-dependent Raman response arises not solely from time-reversal symmetry breaking due to magnetic order, but also from spin-orbit-coupled electronic interactions. Notably, in F3GT, the out-of-plane magnetization indirectly governs the in-plane anisotropic electron-phonon coupling under optical resonance, whereas F5GT exhibits static anisotropic interactions. The Fano asymmetry parameter $1/q$ reveals mode- and temperature-dependent coupling strengths between phonons and the electronic continuum, with pronounced angular anisotropy in F3GT but isotropic behavior in F5GT--- a consequence of its multiple Fe sites and enhanced interlayer hybridization in the latter. Our results demonstrate the role of crystal structure and magnetic anisotropy in shaping the anisotropically coupled electron-phonon-spin dynamics in these layered metallic ferromagnets, and highlight Fe$_x$GeTe$_2$ as a versatile platform for microscopic insight into chiral light-matter interactions in layered metallic ferromagnets.

</details>


### [123] [Fluctuations of topological charges in two-dimensional classical Heisenberg model through high-temperature and low-temperature expansions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.03195)
*Shan-Chang Tang*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 本文从二维XY模型中的KT相变（Kosterlitz-Thouless transition）出发，将拓扑缺陷绑缚/解绑的涨落刻画方法推广至二维海森堡模型（2d Heisenberg model），通过高温与低温展开分别计算拓扑电荷（topological charge）的涨落规律，发现高温下涨落正比于区域面积，低温下则服从周长律（perimeter law）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 二维XY模型中的KT相变由拓扑缺陷的绑缚与解绑驱动，这种行为可通过区域内拓扑电荷的涨落来表征。作者希望将该思路推广到二维海森堡模型中，以检验类似的拓扑涨落刻画是否适用。

Method: 将KT相变中基于拓扑电荷涨落的判据推广至二维海森堡模型，分别利用高温展开（high-temperature expansion）和低温展开（low-temperature expansion）计算区域内拓扑电荷的涨落行为。

Result: 在高温下，二维海森堡模型中拓扑电荷的涨落与所考察区域的面积成正比；在低温下，涨落则服从周长律，即与区域的边界周长成正比。

Conclusion: 二维海森堡模型中拓扑电荷涨落的高温面积律与低温周长律差异，为判断该模型中是否存在类似KT的拓扑相变提供了可操作的数值判据。

Abstract: It is well known that KT transition in 2d XY model is driven by the binding and unbinding of topological defects, which can be characterized by the fluctuation of topological charges inside a region. We extend the idea into the 2d Heisenberg model and calculate the fluctuation through high-temperature and low-temperature expansion respectively. It is found that the fluctuation of topological charges is proportional to the area of the region at high temperatures while obeys the perimeter law at low temperatures.

</details>


### [124] [Magnetoresistance in Magnetic Weyl semimetal Mn$_{3}$ZnC](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.03414)
*Sunil Gangwar,C. S. Yadav*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 本文研究了反钙钛矿磁体Mn₃ZnC（一种磁节点线半金属）的磁电阻（magnetoresistance, MR）行为，揭示了其磁电阻与磁化强度之间的关联，以及在铁磁态和亚铁磁态中不同的MR特征，包括低温下的符号反转和铁磁态极低场下的反常正MR。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 磁电阻（magnetoresistance, MR）反映了自旋相关的电子散射过程，而在具有非零贝里曲率（Berry curvature）的拓扑磁性材料中还存在内在散射通道，使MR研究更具物理意义。作者旨在通过研究Mn₃ZnC这一反钙钛矿磁性节点线半金属的MR行为，理解其磁电阻与磁化强度的关系，以及不同磁有序态下的输运特征。

Method: 研究聚焦于对Mn₃ZnC的磁电阻数据的系统分析与解释。材料在约420 K经历铁磁（ferromagnetic, FM）转变，随后在约195 K经历亚铁磁（ferrimagnetic, FIM）转变。作者在FIM态和FM态下测量并对比MR曲线与等温磁化曲线M(H)，分析MR随磁场和温度的演化规律，并通过载流子迁移率的变化解释MR符号反转。

Result: 研究表明，Mn₃ZnC的低场MR与磁化强度呈正相关，且FIM态和FM态中的MR曲线表现出显著不同的行为。类似于等温磁化M(H)曲线，MR曲线在低场下急剧上升，随后在高场区域呈现线性行为。尖峰状异常随磁场变化更加显著，在磁相变处消失，随后随温度升高再次出现。FIM态中MR符号的反转归因于载流子迁移率的剧烈变化。此外，FM态极低场下观察到正MR，这一现象较为反常和独特。

Conclusion: Mn₃ZnC的磁电阻行为与其磁化强度密切相关，在不同磁有序态中表现出独特的MR特征。FIM态MR符号反转源于载流子迁移率的剧变，而FM态极低场下的正MR是异常现象，值得进一步探讨其物理机制。

Abstract: The magnetoresistance (MR) in magnetic materials reveal an intriguing spin-dependent electron scattering, highlighting the role of spin polarization on the electronic transport properties. This becomes even more interesting for the topological magnetic materials where a finite Berry curvature leads to an intrinsic scattering channel also. In this report, we investigate MR of Mn$_{3}$ZnC, an antiperovskite magnetic nodal line semimetal. Mn$_{3}$ZnC shows a ferromagnetic (FM) transition at $\sim$ 420 K, followed by an ferrimagnetic (FIM) transition at $\sim$ 195 K. We focus on the interpretation of MR data and highlight the relation between MR and its magnetization. The signature of magnetization induced MR shows correlation at low magnetic fields and displays distinct behaviors in the FIM and FM states. Similar to the isothermal magnetization M(H) curve of FM and FIM state, the MR curves exhibit a sharp increase, followed by a linear behavior at higher fields. As the magnetic field varies, the cusp-like anomaly becomes more pronounced, and vanishes at the magnetic phase transition, which then reappears as the temperature increases. The sign change in the MR curves in the FIM state is attributed to a drastic change in the carrier mobility. Interestingly, this system shows a positive MR in FM state at very low field, which is quite unusual.

</details>


### [125] [Manipulation of localized excitons in CrPS$_4$ by temperature and magnetic field](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.03543)
*Dipankar Jana,Swagata Acharya,Amit Pawbake,Dmitrii Litvinov,Aljoscha Soll,Zdenek Sofer,Clement Faugeras,Dimitar Pashov,Mark van Schilfgaarde,Kostya S. Novoselov,Marek Potemski,Maciej Koperski*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 本文结合多体微扰论（many-body perturbation theory）、动力学平均场理论（dynamical mean-field theory）和光致发光（photoluminescence）实验，系统研究了层状范德华反铁磁半导体CrPS₄的电子与激子性质，确定了其直接带隙为2.48 eV，预测了多种亚带隙激子跃迁（包括自旋允许激子和自旋翻转激子），并通过温度与磁场依赖的光学测量识别出磁相变的光学特征信号。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 层状范德华磁性半导体为研究激子现象与自旋、晶格自由度之间的耦合提供了理想平台，激子可作为磁有序的灵敏探针。然而，CrPS₄作为层状反铁磁半导体，其丰富的激子特征的微观起源及其与磁有序的关联尚未被完全理解，这正是本研究的核心动因。

Method: 本研究采用多体微扰论、动力学平均场理论与基于光致发光的实验测量相结合的方法，对块体CrPS₄的电子和激子性质进行系统研究。理论计算用于预测带隙和激子跃迁能级，实验测量则用于验证激子在不同温度和磁场条件下的行为。

Result: 计算确立了CrPS₄为反铁磁相中带隙为2.48 eV的直接带隙（direct-gap）半导体。理论预测了多个亚带隙激子跃迁，包括多个自旋允许激子和一个额外的自旋翻转（spin-flip）激发，这些激子主要局域在Cr³⁺离子上。温度与磁场依赖的光学测量揭示了热驱动的激子在各局域态间的再分布，并识别出可作为磁相变清晰光学特征的特征性能量位移。

Conclusion: 该研究为反铁磁体中的激子跃迁提供了新见解，并提出了实现全光传感（all-optical sensing）和光驱动控制（light-driven control）磁有序的潜在途径。

Abstract: Layered van der Waals magnetic semiconductors provide a versatile platform for exploring excitonic phenomena intertwined with spin and lattice degrees of freedom, enabling excitons to act as sensitive probes of magnetic order. CrPS$_4$ is a layered antiferromagnetic semiconductor that hosts rich excitonic features whose microscopic origin and connection to magnetic ordering remain incompletely understood. Here, we investigate the electronic and excitonic properties of bulk CrPS$_4$ using a combination of many-body perturbation theory, dynamical mean-field theory, and photoluminescence-based experiments. Our calculations establish CrPS$_4$ as a direct-gap semiconductor with a bandgap of 2.48~eV in the antiferromagnetic phase. Several sub-bandgap excitonic transitions are predicted by theory, comprising multiple spin-allowed excitons and an additional spin-flip excitation, predominantly localized on the Cr$^{3+}$ ions. Temperature- and magnetic-field-dependent optical measurements reveal thermally driven exciton redistribution among localized states and identify characteristic energy shifts that provide clear optical signatures of magnetic phase transitions in CrPS$_4$. These results provide new insights into the excitonic transitions of antiferromagnets and suggest potential routes for all-optical sensing and light-driven control of their magnetic order.

</details>


### [126] [Emergence and Detection of Surface altermagnetism in KV$_2$Se$_2$O](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.03551)
*Rodrigo Jaeschke-Ubiergo,Xanthe H. Verbeek,Colin Lange,Sergio Rodriguez,Atasi Chakraborty,Alexander Mook,Jairo Sinov*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 本研究通过钒基硫氧化物 \KVSO 证实了"涌现表面交变磁性（emergent surface altermagnetism）"概念，发现其 (001) 表面在体相反铁磁有序条件下表现出 d 波交变磁性，并通过预测非线性 Edelstein 响应作为关键实验判据，统一解释了此前看似矛盾的中子衍射与光电子能谱实验结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 近期实验结果存在矛盾：中子衍射显示 \KVSO 体相为反铁磁有序，而光电子能谱则观察到 d 波自旋劈裂，两者在传统框架下难以同时解释。作者旨在通过表面涌现交变磁性这一新概念来调和这些看似冲突的实验证据，并为其提供可验证的预测信号。

Method: 作者基于 \KVSO 体相反铁磁有序的电子结构，计算分析了其 (001) 表面表现出的 d 波交变磁性特征，并通过对称性分析预测表面局域的非线性 Edelstein 响应，将其作为验证该概念的关键实验判据。

Result: 研究发现 \KVSO 的 (001) 表面展现出 d 波交变磁性，同时体相保持反铁磁有序，从而完整解释了过去看似矛盾的中子衍射与光电子能谱实验结果。此外，预测出遵循 d 波交变磁性对称性、局域于表面的显著非线性 Edelstein 响应。

Conclusion: 该研究不仅适用于金属态、室温磁性材料 \KVSO，还可推广至多种 Lieb 晶格体系，拓展了探测反铁磁表面涌现交变磁性的技术手段。

Abstract: We demonstrate the recent concept of emergent surface altermagnetism through its unique signatures in \KVSO. We show that for bulk antiferromagnetically ordered \KVSO, the (001) surface exhibits $d$-wave altermagnetism. Our results fully explain the recent seemingly contradicgting experimental evidence, independently showing both an antiferromagnetically ordered bulk from neutron diffraction, and $d$-wave spin splitting from photoemission spectroscopy. To fully verify this conecept, we predict, as a key experimental signature, a large nonlinear Edelstein response, which is localized at the surface, and follows the $d$-wave altermagnetic symmetry. These results are not only relevant for the metallic and room-temperature magnet \KVSO, but also for several other Lieb lattice systems. Our work expands the pool of techniques that can be used to detect altermagnetism emerging at the surfaces of antiferromagnets.

</details>


### [127] [Microscopic Origin of Random Singlet Behavior in B-site Disordered Spin-1/2 Perovskite BaCu_1/3Nb_2/3O_3 Revealed by EXAFS and Thermodynamics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.03671)
*Sagar Mahapatra,Francesco De Angelis,Martin Etter,Edmund Welter,M. P. Saravanan,Rajeev Rawat,Carlo Meneghini,Surjeet Singh*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 通过同步辐射粉末X射线衍射（XRD）和X射线吸收精细结构（XAFS）谱学，对具有B位Cu/Nb无序占据的ABO₃型钙钛矿BaCu₁/₃Nb₂/₃O₃（BCNO）进行了结构-热力学联合研究。结果表明，虽然XRD未发现长程Cu/Nb有序，但XAFS揭示出局域异质原子Cu:Nb关联优先的化学有序，强烈抑制了直接Cu:Cu连接，导致在低温下涌现随机单态（random-singlet, RS）行为，而非自旋玻璃或长程磁有序。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解ABO₃型无序钙钛矿中B位Cu²⁺/Nb⁵⁺无序的微观本质及其与低温随机单态行为之间的关系，特别是揭示为什么在具有可观反铁磁相互作用（Θ_CW ≈ −50 K）的体系中不出现自旋玻璃冻结或长程磁有序。

Method: 结合同步辐射粉末X射线衍射（XRD）和X射线吸收精细结构（XAFS）谱学进行结构表征，结合磁化率χ(T)和比热c_p(T)测量进行热力学研究，分析低温下的幂律行为和T/H单参数标度。

Result: XRD未显示长程Cu/Nb有序，平均占据与化学计量一致；XAFS揭示局域异质原子Cu:Nb关联优先，强烈抑制直接Cu:Cu连接，尽管Cu浓度接近立方格子的渗流阈值。磁化率和比热在宽温度与磁场范围内表现出幂律行为和单参数T/H标度，符合随机单态现象学；极低温下比热从零场的T^{1−γ}（γ≈0.6）行为转变为高场下的T线性依赖。

Conclusion: 局域化学有序导致的交换网络结构解释了在显著反铁磁相互作用下仍无自旋玻璃冻结或长程磁有序的机制，支持随机单态现象学。极低温高场下比热行为的T线性转变表明存在一个不同于随机单态的特定低能区，其微观起源有待进一步研究。

Abstract: We report a combined structural and thermodynamic study of the ABO$_3$-type disordered perovskite BaCu$_{1/3}$Nb$_{2/3}$O$_3$ (BCNO), whose B site is jointly occupied by Cu and Nb in the $1:2$ ratio. Using synchrotron powder x-ray diffraction (XRD) and x-ray absorption fine structure (XAFS) spectroscopy, we investigate the microscopic nature of Cu$^{2+}$/Nb$^{5+}$ disorder on the pseudo-cubic B-sublattice and its relation to the emergent random-singlet (RS) behavior evidenced at low temperatures. While XRD reveals no long-range Cu/Nb ordering and average site occupancy consistent with stoichiometry, XAFS reveals a peculiar local chemical order characterized by preferential heteroatomic Cu$:$Nb correlations. This local arrangement strongly suppresses direct Cu$:$Cu linkages, despite the Cu concentration being close to the percolation threshold of a cubic lattice. The resulting exchange network explains the absence of spin-glass freezing or long-range magnetic order in the presence of substantial antiferromagnetic interactions, as indicated by a Curie-Weiss temperature $Θ_{CW}\approx -50$ K. Instead, the magnetic susceptibility $χ(T)$ and specific heat $c_p(T)$ exhibit power-law behavior and characteristic single-parameter $T/H$ scaling over broad temperature and magnetic-field ranges, consistent with random-singlet phenomenology. Notably, at very low temperatures, the specific heat behavior transitions from $T^{1-γ}$ ($γ\approx 0.6$ from the $T/H$ scaling) in zero-field to a T-linear dependence under high field, indicating a crossover to a distinct low-energy regime whose microscopic origin remains to be established.

</details>


### [128] [Cavity control of quantum phase transitions in a two-dimensional kondo lattice](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.03714)
*Jun Mochida,Atac Imamoglu,Yuto Ashida*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 该论文展示腔量子电动力学（cavity quantum electrodynamics）产生的真空涨落可通过动量依赖的自能修改二维Kondo晶格中的量子相变，纵向、横向和圆偏振投影场对重费米子相与反铁磁相的竞争产生不同的调控效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 莫尔材料已成为研究二维重费米子系统（heavy-fermion systems）及其量子相变的核心平台，Kondo晶格提供了研究量子相边界的原型模型。此前缺乏对腔诱导相互作用如何调节Kondo晶格中量子相变的研究。

Method: 研究基于二维Kondo晶格模型，通过在导带的动量依赖自能（momentum-dependent self-energy）中引入腔诱导相互作用，比较纵向、横向和圆偏振三种投影场结构对Kondo屏蔽与局域磁矩有序竞争的影响。纵向场构造受h-BN双曲声子极化子启发。

Result: 纵向投影场的自能有利于Kondo杂化，扩展重费米子相区域；横向场相对倾向于磁有序相；圆偏振场的效果介于纵向场与横向场之间。腔诱导相互作用可有效改变强关联二维Kondo材料的调控参数。

Conclusion: 电磁真空涨落可以有效调控二维Kondo材料中的量子相变过程，为利用腔量子电动力学控制二维强关联材料量子相提供了新途径。

Abstract: Cavity quantum electrodynamics offers a route to control quantum phases by using vacuum fluctuations of confined electromagnetic fields. In particular, planar cavities based on polar van der Waals materials can generate strongly confined modes and are promising for controlling two-dimensional correlated materials. Recently, moiré materials have become central platforms for studying two-dimensional heavy-fermion systems and their quantum phase transitions. Kondo lattices provide a prototypical model for studying quantum phase boundaries, driven by competition between Kondo screening and the ordering of local magnetic moments. We show that a cavity-induced interaction can shift the quantum phase transitions between a heavy-fermion phase and an antiferromagnetic phase in a two-dimensional Kondo lattice through a momentum-dependent self-energy of the conduction bands. For the longitudinal projected field motivated by h-BN hyperbolic phonon polaritons, the self-energy favors Kondo hybridization and expands the heavy-fermion region. Transverse and circular in-plane model structures give distinct effects, with the transverse case relatively favoring the magnetically ordered phase and the circular case lying between the longitudinal and transverse cases. These results indicate that electromagnetic vacuum fluctuations can effectively modify the control parameters of strongly correlated two-dimensional Kondo materials.

</details>


### [129] [Antisymmetric Dynamical Spin Correlations: Spin-Space-Group Constraints and Frequency-Moment Sum Rules](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.03786)
*Tsutomu Momoi*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 本文通过极化非弹性中子散射研究了磁激发手性的对称性约束，推导了动力学自旋结构因子张量的反对称部分在磁空间群和自旋空间群残余操作下的对称性规则，并建立了严格的零温求和规则。研究发现，在补偿型共线反铁磁体中反对称动力学结构因子被禁止，而在旋转关联的交替磁体中允许存在动量反转偶的xy分量，螺旋磁体中则允许动量反转奇的xy分量，并通过线性自旋波计算加以验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 极化非弹性中子散射可以通过动力学自旋结构因子张量的自旋分量反对称部分直接探测磁激发的手性（handedness），但该部分张量的对称性约束尚不明确，需要系统推导以确定允许的张量分量、动量反转下的宇称以及对称性强制的节点（nodes）。

Method: 推导动力学自旋结构因子张量反对称部分在磁空间群（magnetic space groups）和自旋空间群（spin-space groups）的幺正（unitary）与反幺正（antiunitary）残余操作下的对称性约束；引入对应的反对称对易子谱函数并推导其频率矩的精确零温求和规则；针对交替磁体（altermagnets）和平面螺旋（planar helix）等代表模型进行线性自旋波（linear spin-wave）计算验证。

Result: 零阶矩由均匀磁化决定；对海森堡或XXZ交换哈密顿量，xy分量的第一矩由动量加权的静态矢量手性（static vector chirality）决定。在补偿型共线反铁磁体中，若反演或平移操作联系相反自旋子晶格，则反对称动力学结构因子被禁止；在旋转关联的交替磁体中，动量反转偶的xy分量在一般波矢下被允许，可与零的零阶和第一矩共存；在平面螺旋中，反幺正自旋空间群对称性使允许的xy分量在动量反转下为奇，其第一矩一般非零。

Conclusion: 反对称动力学自旋结构因子的对称性约束与求和规则为区分不同类型的磁有序体系（包括交替磁体和螺旋磁体）提供了明确的理论判据，线性自旋波计算证实了这些对称性和求和规则约束的一致性。

Abstract: Polarized inelastic neutron scattering probes the handedness of magnetic excitations through the spin-component-antisymmetric part of the dynamical spin-structure-factor tensor. We derive its symmetry constraints under unitary and antiunitary residual operations of magnetic space groups and spin-space groups. These constraints determine the allowed tensor components, their parity under momentum reversal, and symmetry-enforced nodes. We then introduce the corresponding antisymmetric commutator spectral function and derive exact zero-temperature sum rules for its frequency moments. The zeroth moment is fixed by the uniform magnetization. For Heisenberg or XXZ exchange Hamiltonians, the first moment of the $xy$ component is fixed by a momentum-weighted static vector chirality. For compensated collinear antiferromagnets, the antisymmetric dynamical structure factor is forbidden when inversion or a translation relates the opposite-spin sublattices. In rotation-related altermagnets, however, an $xy$ component that is even under momentum reversal is allowed at generic wave vectors. Finite antisymmetric spectral weight can therefore coexist with vanishing zeroth and first moments of the commutator spectral function. In a planar helix, by contrast, a residual antiunitary spin-space-group symmetry makes the allowed $xy$ component odd under momentum reversal. Its first moment is generally nonzero and is fixed by a momentum-weighted static vector chirality through the first-moment sum rule. Linear spin-wave calculations for representative altermagnetic and helical models explicitly realize these symmetry and sum-rule constraints.

</details>


### [130] [Local magnetic order in vacancy-disrupted spin ice Ho2TiO5](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.03850)
*Raju Baral,Haidong Zhou,Qiang Zhang,Benjamin A. Frandsen,Stuart Calder*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 本研究通过中子散射技术（中子磁对分布函数、倒空间反向蒙特卡洛和半极化中子粉末衍射）比较了经典烧绿石自旋冰材料 Ho₂Ti₂O₇ 与部分无序的填充型化合物 Ho₂TiO₅ 中的局域磁关联。结果表明，Ho₂TiO₅ 中尽管存在晶格畸变和非磁性 Ti 占据导致的磁性空位，Ho 离子仍保持局域 <111> 伊辛各向异性，但集体冰规则排序被强烈破坏，形成一种空位破坏型自旋冰（vacancy-disrupted spin ice），在拓扑不完整的磁性网络上仍保留局域冰规则倾向。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解局域磁关联如何在经典烧绿石自旋冰 Ho₂Ti₂O₇ 转变为部分无序的填充型化合物 Ho₂TiO₅ 时演化，以探究晶格无序和磁性空位对自旋冰态的影响机制。

Method: 采用实空间磁对分布函数（magnetic pair distribution function）、倒空间反向蒙特卡洛（reverse Monte Carlo）以及半极化中子粉末衍射（half-polarized neutron powder diffraction）三种中子散射分析方法，将平均晶体结构与局域磁关联相关联。

Result: 在 0.3K 下 Ho₂Ti₂O₇ 中 95% 的四面体满足冰规则（ice rule），呈现接近理想的二进二出（two-in/two-out）自旋冰态；Ho₂TiO₅ 中大多数四面体因非磁性 Ti 占据顶点而磁不完整，但占优的不完整构型为二进一出（2-in/1-out）和一进二出（1-in/2-out），与局域冰规则相容。Ho₂TiO₅ 保持 Fd-3m 对称性但存在 Ho-O、Ti-O 和 O-O 键长无序。

Conclusion: 单离子 <111> 伊辛各向异性在两种化合物中均得以保留，但 Ho₂TiO₅ 中集体冰排序被严重破坏，形成空位破坏型自旋冰——在拓扑不完整的磁性网络上局域冰规则倾向持续存在。

Abstract: We investigate how local magnetic correlations evolve when the classical pyrochlore spin-ice Ho2Ti2O7 is transformed into the partially disordered stuffed compound Ho2TiO5. Neutron scattering measurements were analyzed using real-space magnetic pair distribution function, reciprocal-space reverse Monte Carlo, and half-polarized neutron powder diffraction methods to connect the average crystal structure with local magnetic correlations. Both compounds retain long-range $Fd-3m symmetry, but in Ho2TiO5 this average structure distorts locally through Ho-O, Ti-O and O-O bond-length disorder associated with partial Ho/Ti occupancy. Despite this disorder, half-polarized neutron powder diffraction shows that the Ho moments retain local <111> rangle Ising anisotropy and form spin-in/spin-out configurations on the tetrahedral network. In Ho2Ti2O7, real- and reciprocal-space analyses reveal a nearly ideal two-in/two-out spin-ice state at 0.3K, with 95% of tetrahedra satisfying the ice rule, followed by progressive thermal disordering on warming. In Ho2TiO5, most tetrahedra are magnetically incomplete because some tetrahedral vertices are occupied by nonmagnetic Ti rather than Ho; nevertheless, the dominant incomplete configurations are 2-in/1-out and 1-in/2-out, which are locally compatible with the ice rule if the missing Ho spin is restored. The single-ion <111> Ising anisotropy is therefore retained throughout, while the collective ice ordering is strongly disrupted: Ho2TiO5 is a vacancy-disrupted spin ice in which a local ice-rule tendency persists on a topologically incomplete magnetic network.

</details>


### [131] [Accelerated quantum Monte Carlo simulations of the attractive Hubbard model on the kagome lattice](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.03894)
*Jie Zhang,Xiang Li,Yu Wang*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于快速傅里叶变换（fast Fourier transform, FFT）的加速方案，结合延迟更新（delay-update）算法，将行列式量子蒙特卡洛（determinant quantum Monte Carlo, DQMC）模拟的系统尺寸扩大至以往的两倍，并对吸引型kagome晶格Hubbard模型开展大规模模拟，揭示了狄拉克填充处的零温超流量子临界性，并给出了可靠的临界指数估计。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: kagome材料家族及光学kagome晶格实验的发展推动了对kagome晶格上相互作用驱动关联态的大量数值研究，而现有的DQMC模拟可访问系统尺寸有限，难以进行可靠的有限尺寸标度（finite-size scaling）分析。

Method: 开发了基于快速傅里叶变换（FFT）的复合晶格传播子乘法通用加速方案，并将其与延迟更新算法相结合，使DQMC模拟能够在两倍于以往研究的系统尺寸上进行。

Result: 大规模模拟揭示了吸引型kagome晶格Hubbard模型在狄拉克填充处的相互作用驱动的零温超流量子临界性，并提供了可靠的临界指数估计；未发现先前提出的三角规则电荷密度波（triangle-rule charge-density wave）有序态在热力学极限下存活，提示其可能是有限尺寸效应；所提出的FFT与延迟更新组合方案在目前二维光学晶格实验可达的系统尺寸上有效计算成本标度为N^{2.49}，显著低于传统DQMC的O(N^3)计算复杂度。

Conclusion: 所发展的FFT加速DQMC方案显著扩大了可模拟的系统尺寸，为kagome晶格强关联体系的可靠有限尺寸标度分析提供了新手段，并澄清了先前关于电荷密度波有序态的热力学极限性质争议。

Abstract: The recent discovery of several families of kagome materials and experimental realization of optical kagome lattices have stimulated growing numerical studies of interaction-driven correlated states on the kagome lattice. Among the available numerical approaches, determinant quantum Monte Carlo (DQMC) is a powerful method for investigating such strongly correlated states. However, the accessible system sizes of existing DQMC simulations remain limited, preventing reliable finite-size scaling analyses. Here we develop a general acceleration scheme based on fast Fourier transform (FFT) for propagator multiplications on composite lattices and combine it with the delay-update algorithm, enabling simulations on system sizes twice as large as those of previous DQMC studies, allowing reliable finite-size scaling analyses of the attractive kagome-lattice Hubbard model. Our large-scale simulations reveal the interaction-driven zero-temperature superfluid quantum criticality at the Dirac filling and provide reliable estimates of the associated critical exponents. Besides, we find no evidence that the previously proposed triangle-rule charge-density-wave order survives in the thermodynamic limit, suggesting that it is likely a finite-size effect. Moreover, for system sizes accessible in current two-dimensional optical lattice experiments, the combined FFT and delay-update scheme exhibits an effective computational cost scaling as $N^{2.49}$, substantially below the $\mathcal{O}(N^3)$ computational cost of conventional DQMC simulations.

</details>

### [132] [Radiation reaction in the classical relativistic Størmer problem](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.03310)
*Francisco S. N. Lobo,Tiberiu Harko*

Main category: physics.plasm-ph

TL;DR: 本文通过 Landau–Lifshitz 公式将辐射反作用（radiation reaction）引入经典相对论性的 Størmer 问题，建立了静态偶极磁场中耗散带电粒子运动的自治描述；推导出完整的无量纲方程，并与一个仅包含拖曳力（drag-only）的简化模型进行区分。论文给出了平面运动中的精确演化律、正则角动量的精确规律、平均输运方程以及圆分支闭式积分解，并分析了三维情形下 Landau–Lifshitz 力对垂直扰动的指数阻尼效应。这些解析和数值结果提供了一个可控的分析框架，用于研究强非均匀磁场中辐射驱动的相空间输运。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统 Størmer 问题描述了带电粒子在静态偶极磁场中的无耗散运动，但实际天体物理和等离子体物理场景中，粒子会因辐射而损失能量，需要自洽地纳入耗散效应。作者旨在通过 Landau–Lifshitz 公式扩展经典 Størmer 问题，为强非均匀磁场中的辐射驱动的相空间输运提供可控的分析框架。

Method: 采用 Landau–Lifshitz 公式描述辐射反作用力，推导静态偶极磁场下耗散带电粒子运动的完整无量纲方程；将其与一个保持精确能量损失律但省略与磁场梯度相关方向效应的简化（drag-only）模型进行对照。对平面运动推导粒子能量与正则角动量的瞬时演化定律及平均输运方程，对瞬时圆分支求取闭式积分解，并通过数值积分展示轨道变形以及三维垂直扰动的局部指数衰减。

Result: 获得平面运动能量与正则角动量的精确瞬时演化律和规则束缚振荡的平均输运方程；圆分支的长期演化可闭式积分并趋近于简单的大半径幂律；数值模拟显示平面玫瑰花形轨道的非均匀耗散变形。三维中完整 Landau–Lifshitz 力对小幅垂直扰动产生局部指数阻尼，且主要贡献来自简化模型所缺少的场梯度项。

Conclusion: 这些精确、平均、条件性和局部分析结果构成了研究强非均匀磁场中辐射驱动相空间输运的可控分析框架，为未来的全局模拟（global simulations）、动力学描述（kinetic descriptions）以及相关电磁辐射（electromagnetic emission）计算奠定了基础。

Abstract: We extend the classical relativistic Størmer problem by incorporating radiation reaction through the Landau--Lifshitz formulation, thereby providing a self-consistent description of dissipative charged-particle motion in a static dipole magnetic field. We derive the complete dimensionless equations and distinguish them from a reduced drag-only model that preserves the exact energy-loss law while omitting directional effects associated with magnetic field gradients. For planar motion, the reduced system yields exact instantaneous evolution laws for the particle energy and canonical angular momentum, together with averaged transport equations for regular bound librations. When the motion is constrained to the instantaneous circular branch, the secular evolution can be integrated in closed form and approaches a simple large-radius power law. This analytical solution provides a useful benchmark, although the circular branch is radially unstable and therefore does not describe generic nearby trajectories. Numerical integrations further illustrate the nonuniform dissipative deformation of planar rosette-like orbits. In three dimensions, the complete Landau--Lifshitz force produces local exponential damping of small vertical perturbations, with the leading contribution arising from the field-gradient term absent from the reduced model. These exact, averaged, conditional, and local results establish a controlled analytical framework for studying radiation-driven phase-space transport in strongly inhomogeneous magnetic fields and provide a foundation for future global simulations, kinetic descriptions, and calculations of the associated electromagnetic emission.

</details>

### [133] [A Spectral Route to Directed-Polymer Glasses](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.03730)
*Sen Mu,Abbas Ali Saberi,Mehran Kardar*

Main category: cond-mat.dis-nn

TL;DR: 本文通过将多聚合物问题重构为单聚合物转移矩阵乘积的对数特征值填充问题，首次实现了对淬火随机介质中有限密度互斥有向聚合物的直接数值测试，所得结果与复制贝特拟设（replica Bethe ansatz）的预测一致。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 淬火随机介质中有限密度的互斥有向聚合物是玻璃态线状物质（glassy line matter）的最小模型，但此前由于多聚合物转移矩阵的组合爆炸问题，直接数值检验一直无法实现。

Method: 将问题重构为对单聚合物转移矩阵乘积的对数特征值进行填充，从而绕过庞大组合空间，获得淬火自由能（quenched free energy）、其累积量以及无序诱导的线性谱边（linear spectral edge）。

Result: 稀释理论预测的相互作用自由能正比于ρ²，且无序累积量对密度ρ具有不同幂律依赖性；数值结果与复制预测中无序诱导的线性谱边一致。

Conclusion: 该数值方法首次实现了对玻璃态线状物质稀释理论预测的直接检验，验证了复制贝特拟设在有限密度下的正确性。

Abstract: A finite density of mutually avoiding directed polymers in a quenched random medium is a minimal model of glassy line matter. The dilute theory, solved by replica Bethe ansatz, predicts an interaction free energy proportional to $ρ^2$ and disorder cumulants with distinct power-law dependences on the density $ρ$, but direct numerical tests have been hindered by the combinatorially large many-polymer transfer matrix. We recast the problem as filling logarithmic eigenvalues of a single-polymer transfer-matrix product, obtaining the quenched free energy, its cumulants, and a disorder-induced linear spectral edge consistent with the replica prediction.

</details>


### [134] [Analytical results of Typical Medium Theory for the Bethe lattice with Cauchy disorder](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.03770)
*A. Östlin,I. Titvinidze,M. Kollar,D. Jones,L. Chioncel*

Main category: cond-mat.dis-nn

TL;DR: 本文在无限连接度贝特晶格（infinite-connectivity Bethe lattice）上结合数值模拟与解析方法，实现了柯西分布（Cauchy distribution）无序条件下安德森局域化（Anderson localization）的典型介质理论（typical medium theory, TMT）。利用柯西分布与贝特晶格的特殊性质，推导出典型态密度（typical density of states, TDOS）的解析表达式，并建立了简化的TMT自洽方案。结果表明，带中心的典型态密度随无序强度线性下降并在临界无序强度 W_c=D/2 处消失，ω-W 相图揭示了迁移率边（mobility edge）的连续坍缩与扩展态的消失。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 典型介质理论（TMT）为处理无序系统中的安德森局域化提供了重要框架，但其通常需要数值求解。本文旨在利用柯西分布与贝特晶格的数学特殊性，构造一个既在数值上可验证又具有解析可处理性的TMT实现，从而获得关于态密度和无序临界行为的精确结论。

Method: 作者在具有柯西分布原位无序（on-site disorder）的无限连接度贝特晶格上，将数值计算与解析推导相结合。利用柯西分布与贝特晶格的性质，推导出典型态密度（TDOS）的解析表达式，并由此获得一个简化的TMT自洽方案（self-consistency scheme）。

Result: 研究显示，带中心（band center）的典型态密度随无序强度线性递减，并在临界无序强度 W_c=D/2 处归零。所构建的 ω-W 相图表明迁移率边（mobility edge）随无序增强连续坍缩，扩展态（extended states）最终消失。

Conclusion: 本文为TMT提供了一个兼具解析可处理性与数值可行性的范例，揭示了柯西无序下贝特晶格中典型态密度随无序的线性衰减规律，以及在临界无序 W_c=D/2 处系统的完全局域化转变。

Abstract: We present a combined numerical and analytically tractable realization of the typical medium theory (TMT) for Anderson localization on the infinite-connectivity Bethe lattice with Cauchy-distributed on-site disorder. Exploiting the special properties of the Cauchy distribution and the Bethe lattice, we derive an analytical expression for the typical density of states (TDOS) and obtain a simplified TMT self-consistency scheme. We show that the TDOS at the band center decreases linearly with disorder strength and vanishes at the critical disorder $W_c=D/2$. The resulting $ω$--$W$ phase diagram reveals the continuous collapse of the mobility edge and the disappearance of extended states.

</details>
