### [135] [Certifying Nonclassical Proper-Time Histories with a Quantum Clock](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12755)
*Shuai Zeng*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究量子时钟的相对论相位效应能否证明非经典固有时（proper-time）历史，将该区分问题形式化为通道认证（channel certification）问题，并提出基于Choi秩判据和双支Ramsey协议的认证方案。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量子时钟可因运动或引力固有时差异获得相对论相位，但仅减少时钟退相（dephasing）本身并不能证明固有时历史是非经典的，需要严格的判据来区分经典与非经典固有时历史。

Method: 首先证明任何二能级单时退相信号（包括由有效量子固有时标签产生的信号）都允许经典随机固有时表示；随后定义经典混合固有时历史的凸集，并证明条件相干历史重组（conditioned coherent history recombination）的Choi秩分离判据；最后提出一个双支Ramsey协议，给出该经典集之外的明暗端口粒子数见证（witness）。

Result: 双支Ramsey协议能够给出超出经典混合固有时历史集合的明暗端口（bright- and dark-port）布居数见证，实现非经典性认证，且该认证具有操作性和相对性——仅排除相同已实现固有时历史的经典混合，而非所有采用不同历史或控制的经典协议。

Conclusion: 通道认证框架为量子时钟相对论相位的非经典性提供了可操作且可证伪的判据，其有效性依赖于对指定固有时历史集合的选择。

Abstract: Quantum clocks can acquire relativistic phases from motional or gravitational proper-time differences, but reduced clock dephasing alone does not certify nonclassical proper-time histories. We formulate this distinction as a channel-certification problem. First, we show that any two-level single-time dephasing signal, including one generated by an effective quantum proper-time label, admits a classical random proper-time representation. We then define the convex set of classical mixtures of experimentally specified proper-time histories and prove a Choi-rank separation criterion for conditioned coherent history recombination. A two-branch Ramsey protocol gives explicit bright- and dark-port population witnesses outside this classical set. The certification is operational and relative to the specified history set: it rules out classical mixtures of the same implemented proper-time histories, not arbitrary classical protocols with different histories or controls.

</details>


### [136] [The geometry of absolute separability and other convex matrix properties from spectrum](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.03390)
*Jennifer Ahiable,Naga Bhavya Teja Kothakonda,Andreas Winter*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究双量子比特绝对可分态（ASEP）和绝对正部分转置态（APPT）谱集的几何结构，证明了APPT_{m,n}是光谱多面体（spectrahedron）而ASEP_{m,n}一般是半代数集（semialgebraic set），完整刻画了APPT的面和极点，并给出了关于纯度上界、最小冯·诺依曼熵及相对谱体积的定量结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 双部分量子态在任意全局酉变换（global unitary transformation）下保持可分性或PPT（positive partial transpose）性质的谱集其几何结构尚不清楚，理解这些集合的几何性质对于量子信息理论中纠缠的判定与量化具有重要意义。

Method: 首先建立绝对凸矩阵集合（absolute convex sets of matrices）的一般几何性质；提出APPT判据的置换对称重构（permutation-symmetric reformulation），并利用其证明APPT_{m,n}为光谱多面体；借助代数几何与半代数集工具刻画ASEP_{m,n}的结构及APPT_{m,n}的面与极点；通过内接多面体（inscribed polytope）给出定量上下界并辅以数值验证。

Result: APPT_{m,n}对所有m≤n均为光谱多面体且所有面都是暴露的（exposed）；ASEP_{2,n}为光谱多面体但一般的ASEP_{m,n}仅是半代数集；APPT_{m,n}的面由某矩阵的秩决定，其最大真子面的维数为(mn−m−1)；给出了APPT_{m,n}最大可达纯度的下界和最小冯·诺依曼熵的上界，并数值验证了最小熵随n增大趋于内接多面体。

Conclusion: APPT谱集的几何结构比ASEP更为规则，前者为光谱多面体而后者一般仅为半代数集；定量结果表明APPT_{m,n}的相对谱体积随n指数衰减，衰减因子等于内接多面体相对体积的常数倍，作者提出了最大纯度与多面体一致的猜想（除m=n=2情形外）。

Abstract: We investigate the geometric structure of the set of spectra of bipartite absolute separable states ($\mathrm{ASEP}_{m,n}$) and absolute positive partial transpose states ($\mathrm{APPT}_{m,n}$), i.e., bipartite quantum states that remain separable or PPT respectively, under all global unitary transformations. First, we establish general geometric properties of absolute convex sets of matrices, their spectra and extreme points. Regarding absolute separability, we present a permutation-symmetric reformulation of the absolute PPT criterion and use it to demonstrate that $\mathrm{APPT}_{m,n}$ is a spectrahedron for all $m\leq n$: in particular, all its faces are exposed. In contrast, while $\mathrm{ASEP}_{2,n}$ is also a spectrahedron, we prove that in general $\mathrm{ASEP}_{m,n}$ is a semialgebraic set for all $m\leq n$. Furthermore, we provide a complete characterization of the faces and extreme points of $\mathrm{APPT}_{m,n}$ and demonstrate that the dimension of a face is determined by the rank of a certain matrix, with maximal proper faces having dimension $(mn-m-1)$.
  In the quantitative setting, we provide a rigorous lower bound on the maximal attainable purity of $\mathrm{APPT}_{m,n}$ via an inscribed polytope $\mathcal{P}_{m,n}$ and conjecture that the maximal purity of $\mathrm{APPT}_{m,n}$ (along with its spectra) coincides with the polytope for arbitrary dimensions except when $m=n=2$. Additionally, we also provide a rigorous upper bound on the minimal von Neumann entropy of $\mathrm{APPT}_{m,n}$ and demonstrate numerically that the minimum entropy eventually coincides with the polytope $\mathcal{P}_{m,n}$ as the local system dimension $n$ increases. Finally, we show that the relative spectral volume of $\mathrm{APPT}_{m,n}$ decays exponentially in $n$ by a constant multiplicative factor of the relative volume of the inscribed polytope $\mathcal{P}_{m,n}$.

</details>


### [137] [Entangling Topological Invariants](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.03634)
*Kazuki Ikeda,Yaron Oz*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究孤立占据多重态（occupied multiplet）在无全局一致的子系统结构时是否允许局域张量积（tensor-product）描述的问题，通过比较占据多重态的转移函数与两个候选子系统独立基变换生成的转移函数来刻画其障碍。在闭曲面上分解为秩一扇区时，商结构消除行列可加性陈数据并产生混合陈类（mixed Chern classes）；动量相关混合添加 Gauss–Codazzi 曲率，而标签守恒极限下混合类由交叉 Thouless 泵测量。仅指定因子维数 p 和 q 时，通过 S⁴ 上秩 pq 丛的拼接映射比较，全局分解成立当且仅当 C₂ 可被 gcd(p,q) 整除；特别是奇数 C₂ 阻止 2×2 分解。论文用有限八能级哈密顿量说明两种设置，并提供泵浦和占据投影仪层析读出协议。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解占据多重态在缺乏全局子系统结构时能否存在局域张量积描述，并刻画阻碍这一分解的拓扑与微分几何障碍。

Method: 通过比较占据多重态的转移函数（transition functions）与两个候选子系统独立基变换所生成的转移函数来表征障碍；在闭曲面上引入秩一扇区分解并构造商结构以提取混合陈类；利用 S⁴ 上秩 pq 丛的拼接映射（clutching map）分析仅指定因子维数时的全局可分解性。

Result: 全局分解成立当且仅当陈数 C₂ 可被 gcd(p,q) 整除；奇数 C₂ 必然阻碍 2×2 分解。动量相关混合扇区标签引入 Gauss–Codazzi 曲率，标签守恒极限下混合类由交叉 Thouless 泵测量。论文以八能级哈密顿量示例验证，并给出泵浦与占据投影仪层析读出协议。

Conclusion: 占据多重态的张量积分解受全局拓扑障碍（陈数整除条件）与微分几何修正（Gauss–Codazzi 曲率）共同制约，可通过有限能级模型和层析协议在实验上读出这些结构。

Abstract: An isolated occupied multiplet may admit local tensor-product descriptions without a globally consistent subsystem structure. We characterize the obstruction by comparing the transition functions of the occupied multiplet with those generated by independent basis changes in the two candidate subsystems. When a decomposition into rank-one sectors over a closed surface is specified, the resulting quotient removes row- and column-additive Chern data and yields mixed Chern classes. Momentum-dependent mixing of the sector labels adds the Gauss--Codazzi curvature of the moving lines, while in the label-conserving limit the mixed class is measured by a crossed Thouless pump. When only the factor dimensions $p$ and $q$ are specified, the comparison is made at the level of the clutching map of a rank-$pq$ bundle over $S^4$. Product frames generate winding numbers in $q\mathbb Z+p\mathbb Z$, so global factorization is possible exactly when $C_2$ is divisible by $\gcd(p,q)$; in particular, odd $C_2$ obstructs a $2\times2$ factorization. We illustrate the two settings with finite eight-level Hamiltonians and give pumping and occupied-projector tomography protocols for their readout.

</details>


### [138] [Gravitational redshift as a quantum channel: modeling the effects of gravitational redshift in quantum optics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.03549)
*Thomas Mieling,Andreas Wolfgang Schell,David Edward Bruschi*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文指出经典电磁学中光的引力频移（gravitational frequency shift）现象在量子理论中的描述尚未完善，并定位了此前基于有效多模混频器（MMM）模型的量子红移描述不一致性的根本原因。作者提出两种互补修正方案：一种来自场论视角的"自然"方案，另一种适用于有限维系统量子力学语言，后者可将多模传输设置中的红移建模为可在高斯光态（Gaussian states of light）输入约束下用标准量子信息技术刻画的量子信道（quantum channel）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 光的引力频移在经典电磁学中已有完善理解，但量子理论中的描述尚不完整；此前提出的描述引力红移为作用于光模式的有效多模混频器（MMM）模型存在不一致性，且已有工作虽指出问题却未给出完整解决方案。

Method: 识别MMM模型不一致性的根本原因，并提出两种互补修正途径：一是基于场论视角的"自然"方法，二是面向有限维系统量子力学语言的替代方法。第二种方法将多模传输中的红移建模为量子信道，当输入态限制为高斯光态时可用标准量子信息论技术进行表征。

Result: 成功定位了MMM模型不一致性的根本原因，并分别给出场论与有限维量子力学两种视角下的修正方案；其中第二种方案实现了将引力红移建模为可量化的量子信道，证明了在限制输入为高斯光态时该信道可用标准量子信息论方法特征化。

Conclusion: 本研究为引力红移的量子描述提供了两种互补且自洽的理论框架，特别是建立了其与量子信息论的连接，为后续在量子光学和量子信息背景下研究引力效应开辟了途径。

Abstract: The gravitational frequency shift of light is well understood in the theory of classical electromagnetism. Nevertheless, its description in quantum theory is not yet fully developed. Recent work pointed out inconsistencies in previously developed models aimed at describing gravitational redshift as an effective multi-mode mixer (MMM) acting on modes of light, however, a complete solution of the issues was not obtained. Here, we identify the root cause of the MMM model's inconsistency and provide two complementary approaches to correct it: a "natural" one from a field-theoretic perspective, and another adapted to the language of quantum mechanics of finite-dimensional systems. We show that the second approach allows for modeling of the redshift in a multi-mode transmission setup as a quantum channel that can be characterized using standard quantum information-theoretic techniques when restricting the input states to Gaussian states of light.

</details>


### [139] [Structure of matrix product locally purifiable density operators](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.02724)
*Yale Yauk,Yuhan Liu,Ignacio Cirac*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文系统研究了矩阵乘积局部可纯化密度算子（LPDO）的基本定理问题，证明了在可逆性或周期性条件下，两个顺序生成的LPDO（sLPDO）表示生成相同密度矩阵当且仅当它们通过矩阵乘积等距（matrix product isometry）关联，并为混合态对称保护拓扑相提供了新的洞察。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 矩阵乘积态（MPS）的基本定理已得到充分理解，但混合态张量网络（mixed-state tensor networks）缺乏类似的理论框架，尤其是"两个纯化张量（purification tensor）生成相同密度矩阵时如何关联"这一核心问题尚未有答案。

Method: 作者聚焦于顺序生成的LPDO（sequentially generated LPDOs，sLPDO）这一子类，其可通过初始态上连续施加量子信道（quantum channels）来解释；在此框架下，作者基于可逆性或循环条件证明了两类表示等价的充要条件，并通过构造反例（counterexample）检验了一般LPDO在周期边界条件下推广基本定理的可行性。

Result: 证明在合适的可逆性或循环条件下，两个sLPDO表示生成相同密度矩阵当且仅当它们由作用于纯化键上的矩阵乘积等距关联；此外，针对周期边界条件下的一般LPDO，提供了一个阻碍一般基本定理成立的反例。

Conclusion: 该结果对混合态对称保护拓扑相（mixed-state symmetry-protected topological phases）具有重要启示，特别是揭示了仅由弱对称条件（weak symmetry）保护的非平凡相的可能性。

Abstract: Tensor network methods provide powerful analytical and numerical tools for characterizing quantum phases of matter. While the mathematical structure of matrix product states (MPS) is well understood through the MPS fundamental theorem, an analogous understanding for mixed-state tensor networks remains largely absent: if two purification tensors generate the same density matrix, how are they related? In this work, we initiate the study of a fundamental theorem for matrix product locally purifiable density operators (LPDOs) and focus on sequentially generated LPDOs (sLPDOs), a broad subclass admitting an interpretation in terms of successive applications of quantum channels on an initial state. We prove that, under suitable invertibility or cyclic conditions, two sLPDO representations generate the same density matrix for arbitrary system sizes if and only if they are related by a matrix product isometry acting on the purification bonds. Beyond the sLPDO setting, we provide a counterexample that suggests an obstruction to a general fundamental theorem for LPDOs with periodic boundary conditions. Finally, we discuss implications for mixed-state symmetry-protected topological phases, including the possibility of nontrivial phases protected only by weak symmetry conditions.

</details>


### [140] [Dynamic Induction of Lattice Gauge Theories on a Quantum Computer](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.02756)
*Barbara Andrade,Declan Millar,Lewis Anderson,Vincent R. Pascuzzi,Maciej Lewenstein,Ivano Tavernelli,Jad C. Halimeh,Tobias Grass*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出一种全新的量子模拟格点规范理论（lattice gauge theory, LGT）范式，利用高斯定律（Gauss's law）动态地从简单的三体XXX模型生成U(1)规范理论，而非仅用于抑制或检测规范违背。作者在156量子比特的IBM量子处理器上用101个量子比特进行了实验验证，结果与目标规范理论定量一致，且每个Trotter步的纠缠门深度比直接实现降低了五倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的量子模拟格点规范理论方法遵循两种范式：在模拟平台中利用高斯定律能量学地抑制规范违背过程，或在数字设备中检测并丢弃规范违背的结果。作者希望引入第三种范式，将高斯定律从"对称性保护"的角色转变为"哈密顿量工程"的资源，从而更高效地实现规范理论的量子模拟。

Method: 作者从易于编程的三体XXX模型出发，利用实验上高效的单量子比特U(1)规范对称性生成子项（gauge symmetry-generator terms）来诱导U(1)格点规范理论的动力学。该方法避免了直接构建规范理论所需的深层纠缠门结构，通过在156量子比特的IBM量子处理器上使用101个量子比特实现实验验证。

Result: 实验观察到的实时动力学与目标格点规范理论定量一致，且与直接实现相比，每个Trotter步的纠缠门深度减少至原来的五分之一。这证明了高斯定律驱动的哈密顿量工程方法的可行性和资源效率优势。

Conclusion: 该成果确立了规范保护（gauge protection）作为哈密顿量工程资源而非仅仅对称性保持的作用，为在更高空间维度中数字量子模拟日益复杂的规范理论开辟了一条可扩展、资源高效的路径。

Abstract: Gauge invariance is central to modern physics and underpins quantum simulations of lattice gauge theories (LGTs). Existing quantum simulation approaches employ Gauss's law either to energetically suppress gauge-violating processes in analog platforms or to detect and discard gauge-violating outcomes in digital devices. Here we introduce a third paradigm, in which Gauss's law is used to dynamically generate the gauge theory itself from a substantially simpler Hamiltonian. Starting from a readily programmable three-body XXX model, we employ experimentally efficient single-qubit U(1) gauge symmetry-generator terms that induce the dynamics of a U(1) LGT. We implement this approach using 101 qubits on a 156-qubit IBM quantum processor and observe real-time dynamics in quantitative agreement with the target LGT while reducing the entangling-gate depth per Trotter step by a factor of five compared with a direct implementation. Our results establish gauge protection as a resource for Hamiltonian engineering rather than merely symmetry preservation, opening a scalable resource-efficient route towards digital quantum simulations of increasingly complex gauge theories in higher spatial dimensions.

</details>


### [141] [QuScope: An Open-Source Python Framework for Quantum-Circuit Simulation of Transmission Electron Microscopy](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.02782)
*Sean D. Lam,Roberto dos Reis*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: QuScope是一个开源的Python框架，将透射电子显微镜（TEM）的完整成像流程表达为量子电路，并利用量子态矢量模拟（statevector simulation）验证了与经典实现完全一致的成像结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: TEM成像由电子波函数在样品和物镜中的相干演化（coherent evolution）控制，其物理过程天然对应量子计算的门模型（gate model）。作者旨在利用量子计算框架来建模和模拟TEM成像管线。

Method: 将N×N电子波函数振幅编码（amplitude-encoded）到2log₂N个量子比特中；每个光学元件（相位光栅透射、Fresnel传播、像差物镜）实现为经量子傅里叶变换（quantum Fourier transform）共轭的对角酉算符（diagonal unitary）。在此基础上实现相位物体近似下的常规TEM、多切片（multislice）CTEM和STEM管线，并用经典孪生实现进行逐项验证。

Result: 量子与经典多切片出射波的保真度（fidelity）为单位值，所有物理常数均与标准参考值核实一致；提供了每个算法的转译量子资源估算及对角合成（diagonal-synthesis）瓶颈分析。

Conclusion: QuScope v0.2.0验证了量子电路精确模拟TEM成像的可行性，提供了完整、已测试、可pip安装的开源软件包，并揭示了当前近端硬件执行的主要瓶颈，为量子计算在显微成像领域的应用奠定基础。

Abstract: Image formation in transmission electron microscopy (TEM) is governed by the coherent evolution of the electron wavefunction through the specimen and the objective lens. This physics maps naturally onto the gate model of quantum computation. We present QuScope, an open-source Python framework that expresses the complete TEM image-formation pipeline as quantum circuits. The $N\times N$ electron wavefunction is amplitude-encoded in $2\log_2 N$ qubits, and every optical element, including phase-grating transmission, Fresnel propagation between specimen slices, and the aberrated objective lens, is implemented as a diagonal unitary conjugated by quantum Fourier transforms. On this foundation, QuScope v0.2.0 implements validated imaging pipelines, covering conventional TEM under the phase-object approximation, full multislice CTEM and STEM for thick specimens. All quantum results reported here come from exact, noise-free statevector simulation of the circuits on classical hardware, and every result is validated against a classical twin implementation. Quantum and classical multislice exit waves agree to unit fidelity, and all physical constants are verified against standard references. We provide transpiled quantum-resource estimates for each algorithm, an analysis of the diagonal-synthesis bottleneck that governs near-term hardware execution, and a fully tested, documented, and pip-installable package. QuScope v0.2.0 is available at https://github.com/QuScope/QuScope.

</details>


### [142] [Typical Output States of Monitored Random Clifford Circuits: A Graph-Theoretic Approach](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.03102)
*Yu-Xuan Zhang,Yu-Xiang Zhang*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文发展了一个基于图态（graph state）的框架来分析受监控的随机Clifford电路（monitored random Clifford circuits）的典型输出态。作者证明在大N极限下随机稳定子态（stabilizer states）的图表示收敛到Erdős–Rényi随机图系综G(N,1/2)，从而解决了GHZ纠缠的开放问题；在体积律（volume-law）相中发现密集子图结构，解释了量子纠错能力，并通过图上的平均场论证精确定位了测量诱导相变（MIPT）的临界点p_c=0.1608。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 受监控随机Clifford电路是研究非平衡量子多体动力学的重要平台，其核心物理特征是测量诱导相变（MIPT），即二分纠缠在体积律与面积律（area-law）相之间的转变。然而，典型输出态的图表示结构尚未被系统理解，GHZ纠缠在随机稳定子态中的行为也是一个悬而未决的问题，需要发展新框架来直接访问这些输出态。

Method: 作者建立了基于图态（graph state）的表象框架，将随机稳定子态映射为图上的描述；在热力学极限下证明了图表示收敛到Erdős–Rényi随机图系综G(N,1/2)；针对一维监控电路，分析了输出图中的密集子图结构，并引入感染-恢复（infection-recovery）玩具模型来重现密集子图沿一维量子比特链的空间聚类效应；最后通过图上的平均场论证（mean-field argument）计算MIPT临界点。

Result: 解析推导出随机稳定子态中平均GHZ纠缠含量g_3：偶数N时为1.204，奇数N时为1.325；发现体积律相中输出态图呈现出G(N_sub,1/2)形式的涌现密集子图，表明输出态等价于N_sub个量子比特上的酉电路输出被其余N−N_sub个携带少量纠缠的量子比特弱扰动；感染-恢复玩具模型重现了密集子图的空间聚类效应并展示测量诱导的吸收态相变；图上的平均场论证给出临界点p_c=0.1608，与数值结果p_c≈0.16高度吻合。

Conclusion: 该图态框架为受监控随机Clifford电路提供了直接访问典型输出态的有效工具，统一解释了体积律相的量子纠错能力、GHZ纠缠统计以及MIPT临界行为，展示了图结构方法在非平衡量子多体物理中的强大适用性。

Abstract: Monitored random Clifford circuit is a paradigmatic platform for exploring non-equilibrium quantum many-body dynamics using quantum-information methods. It is well-known for exhibiting a measurement-induced phase transition (MIPT) between volume-law and area-law phases of bipartite entanglement. In this Article, we develop a graph-state based framework that grants direct access to the typical output states of monitored random Clifford circuits. We first show that, in the large-$N$ limit, where $N$ denotes qubit number, the graph representations of random stabilizer states converge to the Erdős--Rényi random graph ensemble $G(N,1/2)$. This observation allows us to resolve the open problem of Greenberger--Horne--Zeilinger (GHZ) entanglement in random stabilizer states. We derive analytically the mean GHZ content, $\langle g_3\rangle=1.204$ for even $N$ and $1.325$ for odd $N$. For monitored one dimensional (1D) circuits in the volume-law phase, we uncover an emergent dense subgraph of the form $G(N_{\mathrm{sub}},1/2)$ in the output-state graphs. This implies that the output state of a monitored circuit is equivalent to an output of a unitary circuit on $N_{\mathrm{sub}}$ qubits, weakly perturbed by the remaining $N-N_{\mathrm{sub}}$ qubits carrying little entanglement. This result directly accounts for the quantum error-correcting capability of the volume-law phase. We further identify a clustering effect in the spatial distribution of the dense subgraph along the 1D qubit chain, and reproduce it with an infection-recovery toy model that exhibits a measurement-induced absorbing-state phase transition. Finally, we locate the critical point of the MIPT at $p_c = 0.1608$ through a mean-field argument on the graph, in excellent agreement with the numerical result $p_c\approx 0.16$.

</details>


### [143] [Single-molecule strong optomechanical regimes in SERS via hybrid plasmonic cavities](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.03307)
*Miguel Á. Martínez-García,Johannes Feist,Diego Martín-Cano*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 该论文研究混合金属-介电腔（hybrid metallo-dielectric cavities）与共振荧光分子耦合所增强的表面增强拉曼散射（Surface-Enhanced Raman Scattering, SERS）相互作用，在远低于不可逆损伤阈值的强度下观察到斯托克斯和反斯托克斯谱的增强劈裂，并利用二阶光子关联揭示了强光机耦合的非经典特性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索在常规SERS中难以实现的强相干相互作用，利用混合金属-介电腔提供窄带混合等离激元模式，以实现强光机耦合和非线性振动修饰。

Method: 通过窄带混合等离激元模式照射略失谐的电子跃迁至饱和状态，观测斯托克斯与反斯托克斯谱中的增强劈裂；计算二阶光子关联函数以识别强光机相互作用的非经典特征。

Result: 观测到增强的斯托克斯与反斯托克斯谱劈裂，表明在远低于SERS不可逆损伤阈值的强度下即出现强光机耦合和非线性振动修饰；二阶光子关联揭示了这些相互作用与SERS构型中的非经典特性。

Conclusion: 混合金属-介电腔可实现增强的强相干SERS相互作用，并为光学表征振动模式的非简谐特性（anharmonic character）提供了可行途径。

Abstract: We identify enhanced strong coherent Surface-Enhanced Raman Scattering (SERS) interactions facilitated by hybridized metallo-dielectric cavities with a resonant fluorescent molecule. By illuminating a detuned electronic transition near saturation via a narrowband hybrid plasmonic mode, we observe enhanced splittings in the Stokes and anti-Stokes spectra, revealing strong optomechanical coupling and nonlinear vibrational modifications at intensities far below irreversible SERS damage thresholds. We compute the second-order photon correlations that allows identifying the nonclassical character associated to these strong optomechanical interactions and SERS configurations that enables them to optically characterize the anharmonic character of the vibrational modes.

</details>


### [144] [Few-photon degenerate parametric resonance in a two-tone driven microwave resonator](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.03871)
*Orjan Ameye,Jakob Koenig,Clinton Potts,Oded Zilberberg,Gary Steele*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究了双相干微波音调驱动的约瑟夫森结Kerr振荡器，通过四波混频激励简并参量下转换。透射谱学显示响应保留半经典Kerr参量振荡器结构（包括失稳瓣和双稳态相空间拓扑），但单模约化无法定量描述系统；完整的三音调量子描述准确复现实验可观测量，揭示了量子涨落主导、经典宏观瓣经历根本性重整化的深层少光子（few-photon）物理。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多音调外部驱动为无需直接调制器件即可实现参量物理提供了途径，但少光子（few-photon）区间的参量响应有效性尚未得到充分探索，这构成了本研究的核心动机。

Method: 作者将两个相干微波音调施加到约瑟夫森结Kerr振荡器上，通过四波混频（four-wave mixing）激励简并参量下转换（degenerate parametric downconversion），并利用透射谱学（transmission spectroscopy）观测系统响应，同时对比单模约化与全三音调量子描述的预测能力。

Result: 实验观测到响应保留半经典Kerr参量振荡器结构，包括失稳瓣（instability lobe）和双稳态相空间拓扑（bistable phase-space topology）。然而，当单光子Kerr位移K超过腔线宽κ时，传统单模约化严重低估AC Stark位移且分布可能不物理；全三音调量子描述则准确复现实验可观测量，显示驱动音调的量子涨落动态主导耗散，三个相互作用音调均处于深层少光子极限。

Conclusion: 研究表明双音调驱动的Kerr振荡器可作为潜在参量放大器（parametric amplifiers），并为驱动-耗散电路（driven-dissipative circuits）中量子至经典交叉（quantum-to-classical crossover）的研究开辟了新方向。

Abstract: Multi-tone external driving offers a route to parametric physics without directly modulating the device. However, the validity of the parametric response in the few-photon regime remains underexplored. Here, we apply two coherent microwave tones to a Josephson-junction Kerr oscillator and stimulate degenerate parametric downconversion via four-wave mixing. Using transmission spectroscopy, we observe that the response retains the qualitative semiclassical Kerr parametric oscillator structure, including its instability lobe and bistable phase-space topology. Interestingly, we demonstrate that a conventional single-mode reduction fails to capture the system quantitatively: the predicted AC Stark shift is severely underestimated, and the reported distributions might not be fully physical when the single-photon Kerr shift $K$ exceeds the cavity linewidth $κ$. Instead, we show that a full three-tone quantum description accurately reproduces the experimental observables. There, quantum fluctuations of the drive tones become dynamically dominant over dissipation, and all three interacting tones operate in a deep few-photon limit where the expected semiclassical macroscopic lobes undergo fundamental renormalization due to profound mixing with quantum variance. Our results establish two-tone-driven Kerr oscillators as potential parametric amplifiers and open new horizons to explore the quantum-to-classical crossover in driven-dissipative circuits.

</details>

### [145] [Pattern formation: reactivity is not necessary for chemotaxis--driven instabilities](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.03685)
*Angela Monti*

Main category: math.DS

TL;DR: 本文研究了在趋化性（chemotaxis）存在的情况下，经典"反应性（reactivity）是扩散驱动（Turing）不稳定性的必要条件"这一结论是否依然成立。作者证明，当趋化贡献足够强时，反应性可以被放松，即反应性虽是Turing不稳定性的必要条件，却不是趋化驱动不稳定性的必要条件。同时，文章将面向反应-扩散系统的矩阵化数值框架推广到反应-扩散-趋化模型，并通过两个代表性例子（趋化扩展的Schnakenberg模型和捕食者-被捕食者模型）展示了理论与数值结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典结果（Neubert, Caswell and Murray）表明，空间均匀平衡态的反应性是扩散驱动（Turing）不稳定性（diffusion-driven instability）的必要条件。本研究旨在探讨当引入趋化通量（chemotactic flux）后，这一经典结论是否仍然成立，以揭示趋化与扩散两种空间自组织机制之间的根本差异。

Method: 考虑一个与趋化通量耦合的一般反应-扩散系统，建立渐近不稳定性的必要条件；将原本用于反应-扩散系统的矩阵导向（matrix-oriented）数值公式推广到反应-扩散-趋化模型，其中趋化输运项以与扩散算子矩阵近似兼容的形式离散化，形成高效的数值框架。

Result: 研究表明，当趋化贡献足够强时，经典的反应性要求可以被放松：反应性仍然是Turing不稳定性的必要条件，但不再是趋化驱动不稳定性的必要条件。文中给出了不稳定区域的几何解释，并通过两个示例验证：趋化扩展的Schnakenberg模型中趋化改变了经典Turing模式；捕食者-被捕食者模型中，在既无反应性又无扩散驱动不稳定性的条件下，趋化单独即可诱导图案形成（pattern formation）。

Conclusion: 这些结果揭示了扩散驱动与趋化驱动空间自组织机制之间的根本差异，为非对称输运过程（non-symmetric transport processes）在生物图案形成中的作用提供了新的理论与计算视角。

Abstract: A classical result by Neubert, Caswell and Murray states that reactivity of a spatially homogeneous equilibrium is a necessary condition for diffusion-driven (Turing) instability. In this work, we investigate whether the same conclusion remains valid in the presence of chemotaxis. We consider a general reaction--diffusion system coupled with a chemotactic flux and establish necessary conditions for asymptotic instability. We show that the classical requirement of reactivity can be relaxed when the chemotactic contribution is sufficiently strong. In particular, while reactivity remains necessary for Turing instability, it is not a necessary condition for chemotaxis-driven instability. From a computational viewpoint, we extend the matrix-oriented formulation developed for reaction--diffusion systems to the more general class of reaction--diffusion--chemotaxis models. The chemotactic transport term is discretized in a form compatible with the matrix-oriented approximation of the diffusion operator, yielding an efficient numerical framework for the simulation of chemotaxis-driven pattern formation. A geometric interpretation of the instability region is presented, highlighting the distinct roles played by diffusion and chemotaxis. The theoretical and numerical developments are illustrated through two representative examples: a chemotaxis-extended Schnakenberg model, showing how chemotaxis modifies classical Turing patterns, and a predator--prey model, demonstrating that chemotaxis alone can induce pattern formation in the absence of both reactivity and diffusion-driven instability. These results reveal a fundamental difference between diffusion-driven and chemotaxis-driven mechanisms of spatial self-organization and provide new theoretical and computational insights into the role of non-symmetric transport processes in biological pattern formation.

</details>

### [146] [Topological properties and phase diagram of the triangular Hofstadter model with staggered flux](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.03278)
*Qi Gao,Wei Chen*

Main category: cond-mat.other

TL;DR: 本研究分析了三角霍夫施塔特模型（triangular Hofstadter model）在相邻三角形中带交错磁通（staggered flux）下的拓扑性质与相图，发现磁通谱在特定磁通值时具有P对称性，通过二聚化打破该对称性可产生丰富的拓扑相，且在不同有理外磁通下呈现奇偶q不同的共性相图特征。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 该模型可用于描述双层过渡金属二硫化物（twisted bilayer transition metal dichalcogenides, TMD）在一定位移电场范围内的低能物理，因此理解其拓扑性质与相图对于相关实验系统具有重要意义。

Method: 研究交错磁通三角霍夫施塔特模型的能谱，分析特定磁通下的P对称性；随后通过二聚化打破平移对称性与P对称性，系统研究不同有理外磁通Φ_B = 2πp/q下二聚化模型的能隙结构与拓扑相，利用一维参数化链的反演对称性对零陈数（Chern number）区域进行进一步分类。

Result: 霍夫施塔特谱一般情况下不对称，仅在交错磁通3φ = π/2 mod π时因附加P对称性而对称。二聚化模型在偶q时通常有三个能隙区域：小二聚化区域具有有限陈数，另两个区域陈数为零；奇q时任意有限二聚化下均为零陈数。零陈数区域可通过φ = 0 mod π/3处一维参数化链的反演对称性进一步区分为拓扑非平凡与平凡两类。

Conclusion: 该模型丰富的拓扑相图可在弱相互作用的扭曲双层TMD、光学晶格中的冷原子系统或近期实验实现的光子晶体中得到验证，为这类系统的拓扑性质研究提供了可实验检验的预测。

Abstract: We study the topological properties and phase diagram of the triangular Hofstadter model with staggered flux in adjacent triangles in this work. This lattice can be used to describe the low energy physics of the twisted bilayer transition metal dichalcogenides (TMD) in a certain range of the electric displacement field between the two layers. We show that the Hofstadter spectrum of this model is generally asymmetric except at specific staggered flux $3φ= π/2 \mod π$ due to an additional P symmetry at such $φ$. Breaking the translation symmetry by dimerization lifts the P symmetry and results in rich topological phases in the system. The dimerized model with different rational external magnetic flux $Φ_B = 2πp/q$ has phase diagram with the following common features. For even q, the dimerized model generally has three gapped regimes. The one with small dimerization has finite Chern number and the other two have zero Chern number. For odd q, the model is gapped with zero Chern number at any finite dimerization. For both q even and odd, the two regimes with zero Chern number can be further characterized by the inversion symmetry of the parametrized one-dimensional chains of the system at φ= 0 \mod π/3$, and one regime is topologically non-trivial and the other is trivial. Our results may be tested in twisted bilayer TMD with weak interaction or cold atom systems in optical lattice or photonic crystals achieved in recent experiments.

</details>
