### [147] [A table-top few-femtosecond broadband extreme-ultraviolet absorption spectrometer with cryogenic cooling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.03955)
*Sheng-Chih Lin,Alfred Zong,Emma Berger,Bailey R. Nebgen,Marcus Hui,Shuaiwei Pan,Jackson McClellan,Michael W. Zuerch*

Main category: physics.ins-det

TL;DR: 本文介绍了一套桌面型低温超快宽带XUV吸收光谱（c-UBXAS）光束线，覆盖22-73 eV能区，结合自动图像配准与最低20 K的低温样品控制，实现亚50-meV能量分辨率和亚10飞秒仪器响应函数。以范德华多铁材料NiI₂为基准实验，展示了跨磁性相变的温度依赖光谱演化及元素特定瞬态响应能力，为研究强关联系统中的非平衡动力学与涌现态提供了新工具。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 强关联系统中少飞秒尺度的电子响应长期难以被现有时间分辨技术探测，理解光-物质相互作用中最初始的固态材料响应需要具备超快时间分辨且元素特定的光谱手段。

Method: 该光束线集成宽带高次谐波产生（high-harmonic generation, HHG）光源（22-73 eV）、自动化图像配准（image registration）以及可降至20 K的低温样品控制系统，实现平衡与非平衡条件下元素特定的测量；关键性能指标包括亚50-meV能量分辨率与亚10-fs仪器响应函数。

Result: 在NiI₂（范德华多铁材料）上的基准实验揭示了其跨磁性相变的温度依赖光谱演化，成功识别了基态及其飞秒瞬态响应中的元素特定贡献，并展现出适合长时间超高灵敏度测量的长期稳定性。

Conclusion: 该仪器为揭开光-物质相互作用中固态材料的最初响应提供了机会，有助于理解强关联系统中非平衡动力学与涌现态的复杂相空间，填补了其他时间分辨技术难以触及的少飞秒电子响应的测量空白。

Abstract: We present a table-top cryogenic ultrafast broadband XUV absorption spectroscopy (c-UBXAS) beamline designed for temperature-dependent and time-resolved investigations of quantum materials. The instrument combines a broadband high-harmonic generation source spanning 22-73 eV with automated image registration and cryogenic sample control down to 20 K, enabling element-specific measurements under both equilibrium and nonequilibrium conditions. The beamline provides sub-50-meV energy resolution and a sub-10-fs instrument response function, while maintaining long-term stability suitable for extended, ultrasensitive measurements. Benchmark experiments on NiI$_2$, a van der Waals multiferroic, reveal temperature-dependent spectral evolution across its magnetic phase transitions, demonstrating the ability to identify element-specific contributions to the ground state and its transient response in the femtosecond regime. This instrument introduces the opportunity to unravel the very first response of a solid-state material during light-matter interaction, paving the way for understanding the complex phase space of nonequilibrium dynamics and emergent states in strongly correlated systems where the few-femtosecond electronic response has eluded most other types of time-resolved techniques.

</details>


<div id='cond-mat.mtrl-sci'></div>

# cond-mat.mtrl-sci [[Back]](#toc)

### [148] [Investigation of GeSn aspect ratio trapping growth up to 8% Sn](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.02710)
*Hryhorii Stanchu,Quang Minh Thai,Fernando M. de Oliveira,Mourad Benamara,Stephen Margiotta,Matthew Cook,Xiaoxin Wang,Jifeng Liu,Perry C. Grant,Baohua Li,Wei Du,Gregory Salamo,Shui-Qing Yu*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 本文报道了锗锡（GeSn）纵横比陷阱（aspect ratio trapping, ART）生长技术的系统性研究，成功实现了Sn含量高达8%的GeSn ART生长，并观察到两种配置：自诱导Ge核/GeSn壳结构（Sn含量6%-8%）和体GeSn ART结构（Sn含量<1%）。通过不同轮次和设计的生长动力学研究，揭示了ART岛金字塔形状与Sn掺入成功之间的关联，以及生长选择性和局部加热的作用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: GeSn是面向CMOS兼容光电子器件的重要材料体系，但此前关于GeSn ART生长的动力学、形貌及其从薄膜生长演化而来的知识仍然缺失，且成功的ART生长本身尚未被证实。本研究旨在填补这一空白，系统理解GeSn ART生长机制。

Method: 采用纵横比陷阱（ART）技术在图案化衬底上进行GeSn局部生长，通过多个生长轮次和不同设计对比，系统研究GeSn ART生长动力学（growth kinetics）、形貌演化（morphology evolution）、生长选择性（growth selectivity）及局部加热（local heating）对Sn掺入的影响。

Result: 成功实现了Sn含量高达8%的GeSn ART生长，观察到两种生长配置：Sn含量6%-8%的自诱导Ge核/GeSn壳结构，以及Sn含量低于1%的体GeSn ART结构。研究揭示了ART岛的棱锥形状（pyramid shape）与Sn成功掺入之间存在关联，并阐明了生长选择性和局部加热在其中的作用。

Conclusion: 该研究建立了GeSn ART生长与薄膜生长之间的演化联系，为面向CMOS兼容光电子器件集成和焦平面阵列成像等应用的GeSn ART技术提供了关键的动力学和形貌学基础。

Abstract: Aspect ratio trapping (ART) growth of germanium-tin (GeSn) is a promising approach to target important objectives on the quest towards commercialization of complementary metal-oxide-semiconductor (CMOS)-compatible GeSn optoelectronics devices. Its local growth on patterned substrate allows for versatile device integration into photonics integrated circuit or for stand-alone structure like focal plane array imager. Additionally, high aspect ratio from nano-sized window can terminate early threading dislocation propagation on the oxide sidewalls, leaving subsequent growth defect-free and potentially improving the device performance. Knowledge remains missing regarding GeSn ART growth kinetics, morphology and how they evolve from thin film growth, with successful growth itself yet to be demonstrated. In this work, we report GeSn ART growth up to 8% Sn. Two configurations -- self-induced Ge core/GeSn shell for Sn content between 6% and 8%, and bulk GeSn ART for Sn content below 1% -- are observed. We present a comprehensive study on GeSn ART growth kinetics through different growth rounds and designs, showing a link between pyramid shape of ART island and successful Sn incorporation, as well as the role of growth selectivity and local heating.

</details>


### [149] [Physics-Informed and Knowledge-Driven Generative AI for Autonomous Discovery of Porous Oxide Energy Materials: Opportunities and Challenges](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.02858)
*Dibakar Datta*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 本文提出了一条超越晶体结构生成、迈向物理信息驱动（physics-informed）且应用感知（application-aware）的生成式人工智能用于能源存储材料逆向设计（inverse design）的路线图。以多孔过渡金属氧化物电极为代表材料平台，作者提出七层物理信息逆向设计框架，并识别出"缺失数据问题"（Missing Data Problem）作为关键瓶颈，进而引入自主知识生成框架，构建合成感知（synthesis-aware）、闭环自主发现（closed-loop autonomous discovery）的基础。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 下一代能源存储材料的发现日益受限于底层设计问题的复杂性，而非单纯的计算能力。多孔过渡金属氧化物（porous transition-metal oxides）作为一类具有挑战性的电池材料，其性能源于晶体化学、孔隙结构、离子输运、电化学、电化学-力学耦合、合成、制造与电池系统运行之间的复杂交互。现有生成式人工智能方法大多仅关注晶体学有效性与热力学稳定性，缺乏对应用场景和合成可行性的考量。

Method: 本文以多孔氧化物电极为代表性材料平台，提出一个七层物理信息逆向设计框架，整合化学、热力学、输运、电化学、耐久性、电池兼容性和可制造性七个层级。同时，作者识别"缺失数据问题"为制约应用感知型AI的根本瓶颈，并提出由多孔氧化物能源材料本体论（Porous Oxide Energy Materials Ontology）和持续演进的"知识库"（Knowledge Base）支撑的自主知识生成框架。

Result: 作者提出了一个用于AI驱动的能源存储材料自主发现的通用框架，即"合成感知、闭环自主发现"（Synthesis-Aware, Closed-Loop Autonomous Discovery）。该框架将生成式AI从单纯的晶体结构生成推进至物理信息驱动、应用感知且合成感知的逆向设计，可推广至能源存储材料及其他功能材料。

Conclusion: 文章指出，生成式AI在材料设计中的下一阶段目标不应仅停留在生成化学上合理的晶体结构，而应整合物理约束、应用需求与实际制造可行性。该路线图为AI赋能的多孔氧化物及其他功能材料的自主发现提供了理论基础与模块化实施路径。

Abstract: The discovery of next-generation energy-storage materials is increasingly limited by the complexity of the underlying design problem rather than by computational capability alone. Porous transition-metal oxides represent a particularly challenging class of battery materials because their performance emerges from coupled interactions among crystal chemistry, pore architecture, ion transport, electrochemistry, electro-chemo-mechanics, synthesis, manufacturing, and battery-system operation. Recent advances in generative artificial intelligence (AI) have demonstrated remarkable capabilities for generating chemically plausible crystal structures. However, current approaches remain largely focused on crystallographic validity and thermodynamic stability. This perspective presents a roadmap for advancing generative AI beyond crystal generation toward physics-informed, application-aware, and synthesis-aware inverse design. Using porous oxide electrodes as a representative materials platform, we propose a seven-tier physics-informed inverse-design framework integrating chemistry, thermodynamics, transport, electrochemistry, durability, cell compatibility, and manufacturability. We further identify the "Missing Data Problem" as a fundamental bottleneck limiting application-aware AI and introduce an autonomous knowledge-generation framework supported by a Porous Oxide Energy Materials Ontology and a continuously evolving "Knowledge Base". Together, these concepts establish the foundation for Synthesis-Aware, Closed-Loop Autonomous Discovery, providing a general framework for AI-enabled autonomous materials discovery across energy-storage materials and other functional materials.

</details>


### [150] [Symmetry-Guided Computational Screening of Two-Dimensional Altermagnets with ab initio Hubbard Corrections](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.02925)
*Anumita Bose,Nataliia Manko,Marco Gibertini,Antimo Marrazzo*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 本研究对Materials Cloud 2D Crystals（MC2D）数据库中的2710种二维材料进行高通量计算搜索，结合对称性筛选与含Hubbard-$U$修正的第一性原理密度泛函理论计算，识別出42种具有交变磁（altermagnetic）基态的材料，其中24种经第一性原理确定$U$参数后仍保持稳定，包括4种已报道材料和20种新预测候选材料，显著扩展了二维交变磁材料的候选池。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 交变磁体（altermagnets）将补偿反铁磁序与动量依赖的自旋劈裂相结合，为无宏观磁化或杂散磁场的自旋电子学应用提供了有前景的平台。尽管三维交变磁体已被广泛识别，二维交变磁体却相对稀缺，亟需系统性搜索以扩充候选材料池。

Method: 本研究采用高通量计算方法，对MC2D数据库中2710种材料进行对称性筛选，并结合含自洽Hubbard-$U$修正的第一性原理密度泛函理论（DFT）计算，系统探索磁性构型及其能量稳定性，以可靠地确定候选材料的磁性基态。

Result: 研究共识别出42种具有交变磁基态的材料（对至少一个$U$值），其中24种在从第一性原理确定Hubbard-$U$参数后仍保持稳定，包括4种已有文献报道的材料和20种新预测的候选材料。代表性单层材料包括金属性Fe₂Si₂SbO₉（自旋劈裂约294 meV）和绝缘性CoBrO（自旋劈裂约330 meV）。

Conclusion: 该工作显著扩充了具有良好剥离能量的潜在二维交变磁候选材料池，为实验努力提供了有价值的指导；同时建立了可复现的高通量计算框架，用于交变磁材料的发现与表征。

Abstract: Altermagnets combine compensated antiferromagnetic order with momentum-dependent spin splitting, offering a promising platform for spintronic applications without macroscopic magnetization or stray magnetic fields. Although a wide range of three-dimensional (3D) materials have been identified as altermagnets, two-dimensional (2D) altermagnets remain comparatively limited. In this work, we perform a high-throughput computational search for altermagnetism across 2710 materials in the Materials Cloud 2D Crystals (MC2D) database. Our approach combines symmetry-based screening with first-principles density functional theory calculations, including self-consistent Hubbard-$U$ corrections, to reliably capture magnetic ground states. Through a systematic exploration of magnetic configurations and their energetic stability, we identify 42 materials exhibiting altermagnetic ground states for at least one value of $U$, of which 24 remain robust upon determination of the Hubbard-$U$ parameters from first principles--including 4 materials previously reported in the literature and 20 newly predicted candidates. These comprise promising monolayers such as metallic Fe$_2$Si$_2$SbO$_9$, and insulating CoBrO, with spin splittings about 294 meV and 330 meV, respectively. Our results significantly expand the pool of potential 2D altermagnet candidates with favorable exfoliation energetics and provide valuable guidance for experimental efforts. In addition, this work establishes a high-throughput computational framework for reproducible discovery and characterization of altermagnetic materials.

</details>


### [151] [Surface Variables Description of Axion Topological Materials](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.10509)
*Francisco J. Solis,Hugo García-Compeán*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 该论文研究了拓扑绝缘体（topological insulator）在外部静态电场和磁场下的宏观准静态磁电响应，采用源自软凝聚态物理的表面变量（surface variables）技术，将系统完全用表面电荷密度和表面电流密度描述，推导出积分方程，并给出了轴对称情形的精确解析解以及基于边界有限元方法（boundary finite element method）的数值求解方案，应用于球体、空心球和环形曲面的拓扑绝缘体。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究对拓扑绝缘体磁电响应（magnetoelectric response）的理解仍不够完整，尤其是在复杂几何构型下，难以用传统体积方法进行高效且完整的描述，因此有必要发展一种基于表面自由度的统一框架。

Method: 借鉴软凝聚态物理中处理多均质材料异质体系的表面变量技术，将整个系统的描述简化为表面电荷密度和表面电流密度；针对轴向对称情形给出精确解析解，并进一步提出边界有限元数值方法以处理一般几何情形。

Result: 推导得到了表面变量所满足的积分方程组，获得轴对称情形的精确解析解，并将数值方法成功应用于球体、空心球和环形曲面等不同几何构型的拓扑绝缘体，同时证明变分原理（variational principle）可用于恢复表面变量方程。

Conclusion: 该表面变量框架能够完整且统一地描述拓扑绝缘体的宏观准静态磁电响应，为任意几何形状的系统提供了解析与数值两方面的求解路径，拓展了对轴子拓扑材料（axion topological materials）电磁响应的理解。

Abstract: We study the response of axion topological materials under the presence of external static electric and magnetic fields. We focus on the macroscopic quasi-static magnetoelectric response of topological insulators. We use techniques based on surface variables that have been previously employed in soft condensed matter problems for the description of heterogeneous systems composed of multiple homogeneous materials. A complete description of the whole system is written in terms of surface degrees of freedom, which in this case correspond to effective surface charge and surface current densities. We obtain a set of integral equations satisfied by these variables. We present exact analytic solutions for axial-symmetric cases. We develop a numerical method for the solution of the integral equations by means of a boundary finite element method. We apply the numerical method to topological insulators with different geometries such as spheres, hollow spheres and toroidal surfaces. We show that that a variational principle can be used to recover the surface variables equations.

</details>


### [152] [Optical properties of Ag, Au, and Cu from first principles](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.02968)
*Xiao Zhang,Emmanouil Kioupakis*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 本文提出了一套基于第一性原理（first principles）研究金属光学响应的综合框架，结合密度泛函理论（DFT）、多体微扰理论（many-body perturbation theory）以及基于极大局域Wannier函数的高效插值技术，应用于银（Ag）、金（Au）、铜（Cu）三种金属，考虑单粒子直接激发、声子辅助激发及电阻性Drude贡献，结果与实验光学测量高度吻合。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 金属材料的光学响应在红外波段涉及复杂的单粒子和集体激发机制，需要一种既能准确描述这些机制又具有广泛适用性的第一性原理方法，以填补现有理论框架在金属光学性质计算上的不足。

Method: 该方法整合了密度泛函理论、多体微扰理论和基于极大局域Wannier函数（maximally localized Wannier functions）的高效插值技术，并同时考虑单粒子直接激发、声子辅助（phonon-assisted）激发以及电阻性Drude（resistive Drude）贡献来评估金属的光学性质。

Result: 该方法在银、金、铜三种金属上得到与实验光学测量总体高度一致的结果，并表明单粒子和集体激发对于捕捉它们红外波段的光学响应均至关重要。

Conclusion: 该框架为理解金属光学响应提供了基础性认知，并且具有普适性，可用于研究新兴金属材料的（光）电子学（optoelectronic）性质。

Abstract: We present a comprehensive framework for investigating the optical response of metals from first principles that combines density functional theory, many-body perturbation theory, and efficient interpolation techniques based on maximally localized Wannier functions, and apply it to analyze the optical properties of silver (Ag), gold (Au), and copper (Cu). We evaluate the optical properties of these metallic materials considering both single-particle direct and phonon-assisted excitations, as well as the resistive Drude contribution. We find an overall excellent agreement with experimental optical measurements for these materials, and show that both single-particle and collective excitations are important in capturing their optical response in the infrared. Our methodology provides fundamental understanding of the optical response of metals and is generally applicable to investigate the optoelectronic properties of emerging metallic materials.

</details>


### [153] [Electrostatic Superlattices beyond 1:1 Stoichiometry](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.02981)
*Binay P. Nayak,Prapti Kakkar,Wesley P. Korba,Honghu Zhang,Wenjie Wang,Surya K. Mallapragada,Alex Travesset,David Vaknin*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 本研究发现，通过调控聚合物接枝纳米粒子（polymer-grafted nanoparticles）的电荷失配，可以组装出高化学计量比的立方超晶格（cubic superlattices），包括CaF2、Th3P4等离子晶格类比物以及无原子对应物的单组分A3和A7超晶格，其中A3晶格理论上被识别为光子带隙晶格。这些结构源于1:1"母体"晶格在局部电荷中性无法满足时发生渐进间隙填充或重组为更大基元。加热时呈现可逆晶格收缩和显著负热膨胀，且缺陷的能垒随纳米粒子尺寸增大而增加，有利于可规模化制备高质量开放超晶格用于光子学应用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 纳米粒子的软度（softness）和电荷调控（charge regulation）通常被视为结构控制的障碍，但作者试图将这些特征转化为优势，探索如何利用受调控的电荷失配来构建传统上难以实现的高化学计量比超晶格结构，并寻找具有实际应用价值（如光子学）的新型晶格拓扑。

Method: 通过同时调控接枝密度（grafting density）、粒子尺寸和体相组成三个参数，在聚合物接枝纳米粒子体系中实现电荷失配的受控调节。基于1:1"母体"晶格在局部电荷中性无法满足时的两种演化路径——渐进间隙填充（progressive interstitial filling）或重组为更大基元（reorganization into a larger basis）——来系统构建多种超晶格结构。例如，系统占据ZnS四面体位点生成CaF2，配体交换引起的对称性破缺将CsCl转化为Th3P4。

Result: 实验实现了CaF2和Th3P4等离子晶格类比物的组装，以及单组分A3和A7超晶格（后者无原子对应结构）；其中A3晶格近期在理论上已被识别为光子带隙晶格。加热时体系表现出可逆晶格收缩和显著负热膨胀（negative thermal expansion）行为。此外，缺陷的能垒随纳米粒子尺寸增大而升高，这有利于实现高质量开放超晶格的规模化生产。

Conclusion: 受调控的电荷失配为纳米粒子自组装提供了新的结构控制维度，能够生成具有光子学应用潜力的高化学计量比超晶格。该发现将所谓的"障碍"转化为设计工具，并为可扩展制备高质量、开放拓扑的光子晶格材料提供可行途径。

Abstract: Exotic nanoparticle superstructures can be accessed by harnessing nanoparticle softness and charge regulation, features often viewed as obstacles to structural control. Here, we show that regulated charge mismatch in polymer-grafted nanoparticles enables the assembly of high-stoichiometry cubic superlattices. By co-tuning grafting density, particle size, and bulk composition, we realize ionic-lattice analogues such as CaF2 and Th3P4, as well as single-component A3 and A7 superlattices without atomic counterparts. The A3 lattice has recently been identified theoretically as a photonic band-gap lattice. These phases emerge from a 1:1 "parent" lattice when local charge neutrality cannot be satisfied, driving either progressive interstitial filling or reorganization into a larger basis. For instance, the systematic occupation of ZnS tetrahedral sites yields CaF2, while ligand-swapping symmetry breaking converts CsCl into Th3P4. Upon heating, the assemblies exhibit reversible lattice contraction and pronounced negative thermal expansion. Furthermore, the energetic penalty for defects increases with nanoparticle size, facilitating the scalable production of high-quality, open superlattices for photonic applications.

</details>


### [154] [Apparent Dresselhaus coefficient in (001) GaAs quantum wells: Correlation-time renormalization in D'yakonov-Perel' spin relaxation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.03128)
*Yuzo Ohno,Jun Ishihara,Satoshi Iba*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 本文通过非弹道蒙特卡洛模拟研究体GaAs和(001)GaAs量子阱中的D'yakonov-Perel'自旋弛豫，发现二维投影模型中关联时间重整化导致Dresselhaus系数出现表观值（apparent coefficient），解释了二维测量与体材料立方系数不一致的原因。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 二维量子阱中提取的Dresselhaus系数与体材料中的立方系数不一致，需要从微观机制上理解这种差异的根源。

Method: 采用非弹道蒙特卡洛（nonballistic Monte Carlo）模拟方法，分别研究体GaAs和(001)GaAs量子阱中的D'yakonov-Perel'自旋弛豫；在二维投影模型中用静态期望值&lt;k_z²&gt;替代k_z²，并通过解析推导关联核K_{1+3}^{2D}来分析关联时间重整化效应。

Result: 体GaAs中立方Dresselhaus系数γ_{3D}≈12.4 eVÅ³，关联时间τ_3^{3D}≈150 fs；二维投影模型中将关联核从K_3^{2D}转变为混合核K_{1+3}^{2D}，其关联时间τ_{1+3}^{2D}在严格二维极限下为235 fs（相对于τ_3^{2D}=130 fs），导致表观系数γ_{2D}^{app}≈9.2 eVÅ³，与(001)GaAs量子阱实验一致。

Conclusion: 关联时间重整化（correlation-time renormalization）是理解二维自旋弛豫与体材料测量所得Dresselhaus系数差异的关键机制，为量子阱中线性与立方Dresselhaus参数的物理解释提供了统一框架。

Abstract: We study the Dresselhaus coefficient inferred from D'yakonov-Perel' spin relaxation in bulk GaAs and (001) GaAs quantum wells using nonballistic Monte Carlo simulations. In bulk GaAs, inelastic LO-phonon simulations reproduce the spin relaxation with a cubic Dresselhaus coefficient γ_{3D}\simeq12.4 eVÅ^3 and a cubic-field correlation time τ_3^{3D}\simeq150 fs. In a projected two-dimensional quantum-well model, however, k_z^2 is replaced by the static expectation value \langle k_z^2 \rangle, converting the dominant Dresselhaus field into a term linear in the in-plane wave vector. We show that this projection changes the D'yakonov-Perel' correlation kernel from K_3^{2D} to a first- and third-order hybridized kernel K_{1+3}^{2D}, for which the correlation time τ_{1+3}^{2D} approaches 235 fs in the strict two-dimensional limit, in contrast to the third-order correlation time τ_3^{2D} of 130 fs. Consequently, the coefficient entering the projected two-dimensional model is not the intrinsic cubic coefficient itself but an apparent coefficient, γ_{2D}^{app}=γ_{3D} \sqrt{τ_3^{2D}/τ_{1+3}^{2D}}. Since τ_{1+3}^{2D}\simeq1.8τ_3^{2D} under LO-phonon-dominated scattering, γ_{2D}^{app}\simeq9.2 eVÅ^3, consistent with spin relaxation in (001) GaAs quantum wells. This correlation-time renormalization clarifies why Dresselhaus coefficients extracted from two-dimensional spin relaxation can differ from the cubic bulk coefficient and provides a useful framework for interpreting linear and cubic Dresselhaus parameters in quantum wells.

</details>


### [155] [AccelNet: Exact backward-compatible acceleration of polynomial angular descriptors through Cartesian moment factorization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.03280)
*Yuki Nagai*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 本文提出 AccelNet，一种精确且向后兼容的方法，无需重新训练即可加速现有的 aenet 和 n2p2 神经网络势（neural-network potentials）。通过利用角度项中可分离单邻居权重与 cosθ 的有限多项式依赖所隐含的有限秩（finite-rank）结构，将显式的邻居对循环替换为单邻居笛卡尔矩（Cartesian moments），从而在保持浮点舍入精度内一致的前提下提升计算效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的 aenet 和 n2p2 神经网络势在分子动力学（molecular dynamics）模拟中计算开销较大，直接重新训练模型成本高昂且不切实际，因此需要一种不改变已训练模型精度的加速方法。

Method: 针对具有可分离单邻居权重且对 cosθ 具有有限多项式依赖的角度项，识别其隐藏的有限秩结构，将显式的邻居对循环（neighbor-pair loops）替换为单邻居笛卡尔矩计算，实现精确加速且无需重训练。

Result: 在 H₂O 和 TiO₂ 模型上验证了 AccelNet 生成的描述符（descriptors）、能量（energies）和解析力（analytic forces）与原始模型在浮点舍入精度内一致，并成功在 LAMMPS 分子动力学模拟中测试了加速后的势函数。

Conclusion: AccelNet 实现了对现有神经网络势的向后兼容加速，且其实现、模型转换工具和 LAMMPS 接口均已作为开源软件发布。

Abstract: We present AccelNet, an exact, backward-compatible method for accelerating existing trained aenet and n2p2 neural-network potentials without retraining. For angular terms with separable one-neighbor weights and a finite polynomial dependence on $\cos θ$, the method exploits their hidden finite-rank structure to replace explicit neighbor-pair loops by one-neighbor Cartesian moments. AccelNet reads models trained with either package and reproduces their descriptors, energies, and analytic forces to floating-point roundoff. We verified this equivalence for H$_2$O and TiO$_2$ models and tested the resulting potentials in LAMMPS molecular-dynamics simulations. The implementation, model-conversion tools, and LAMMPS interfaces are released as open-source software.

</details>


### [156] [Bias Tunable Transport Modulation and Gas Selectivity in Layered BiOI: A DFT NEGF Study](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.03308)
*Jemal Yimer Damte,Jiří Houška,Pavel Baroch,Xue Yong*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 该论文通过第一性原理（first-principles）计算，结合密度泛函理论（density functional theory）与非平衡格林函数（nonequilibrium Green's function）输运方法，系统研究了层状碘氧化铋（BiOI）对NO2、NH3、CO2及挥发性有机化合物（VOC）等气体的吸附与偏压依赖的输运响应，提出以费米能级附近透射通道演化为核心的气体选择性机制，并揭示了电学可调灵敏度与吸附可逆性之间的权衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解吸附热力学与电荷输运调控之间的相互作用，是合理设计低功耗、偏压可调气体传感器的关键。该研究旨在从输运视角而非单纯吸附强度出发，建立层状BiOI的气体选择性理论框架。

Method: 采用密度泛函理论（DFT）与非平衡格林函数（NEGF）输运计算相结合的综合第一性原理方法，系统考察了BiOI对NO2、NH3、CO2及代表性VOC的吸附特性与偏压依赖的输运响应。通过分析费米能级附近的透射通道演化和电荷转移，结合恢复时间分析评估传感性能。

Result: NH3和NO2表现为强化学吸附并引起局部电子扰动，而CO2仅通过弱物理吸附（physisorption）作用，表明吸附强度本身不决定传感性能。费米能级附近透射通道的演化才是传感响应的主导因素。偏压依赖计算显示电学可调的灵敏度层级，弱吸附的CO2在低偏压下表现出显著灵敏度，尽管其电荷转移极小。恢复时间分析进一步揭示了强吸附物种在输运调制与可逆性之间的权衡。

Conclusion: 该研究建立了以输运为中心的层状BiOI气体选择性框架，证明传感响应由输运通道演化而非吸附强度主导，并为环境条件下电场控制气体传感提供了机理层面的见解。

Abstract: Understanding the interplay between adsorption energetics and charge-transport modulation is essential for the rational design of low-power and bias-tunable gas sensors. Here, we present a comprehensive first-principles study of gas selectivity in layered bismuth oxyiodide (BiOI) by integrating density functional theory with nonequilibrium Green's function transport calculations. The adsorption and bias-dependent transport responses toward NO2, NH3, CO2, and representative volatile organic compounds are systematically examined. While NH3 and NO2 exhibit strong chemisorption and localized electronic perturbations, CO2 interacts through weak physisorption, demonstrating that adsorption strength alone does not determine sensing performance. Instead, the evolution of transmission channels near the Fermi level governs the sensing response. Bias-dependent calculations reveal an electrically tunable sensitivity hierarchy, in which weakly adsorbed CO2 preserves conductive pathways and exhibits pronounced low-bias sensitivity despite minimal charge transfer. Recovery-time analysis further highlights the trade-off between transport modulation and reversibility for strongly adsorbed species. These results establish a transport-centered selectivity framework for layered BiOI and provide mechanistic insight into electric-field-controlled gas sensing under ambient conditions.

</details>


### [157] [Picometre-scale real-time drift correction in TEM and STEM by dynamic control of the specimen stage for atomic-resolution imaging](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.03344)
*Christophe Gatel,Julien Dupuy,Teresa Hungria,Martin J. Hytch*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 本文提出一种名为"动态控制"（dynamic control）的实时反馈框架，通过主动控制样品台（specimen stage）实现皮米（picometre）精度的样品漂移补偿，无需任何硬件修改即可作为软件插件运行，并在多种TEM和STEM仪器上验证了其对原子级分辨率成像和长时间曝光实验的显著改善。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 电子显微镜实验中的样品漂移（specimen drift）会严重限制长时间曝光成像和原位（in situ）实验的质量，而既有方法多依赖数值后处理对齐或硬件改动，难以实现实时的主动补偿。

Method: 通过动态控制框架，持续从探测器数据流（detector data stream）中监测实验变量并实时补偿其演化，结合自动化标定（automated calibration）流程，在图像采集的并行过程中以软件插件形式驱动样品台进行主动位移控制，适用于多种可控的实验不稳定性。

Result: 在采用传统机械台和压电台（piezoelectric stages）的多种TEM与STEM仪器上实现了实时漂移校正，涵盖中分辨率TEM、高分辨率TEM、HR-STEM及原位观测；采用压电台时可实现皮米量级的样品稳定化，从而获得漂移校正的原子级分辨率成像。

Conclusion: 基于样品台的主动稳定化方法可显著提升长曝光成像与原位实验质量，增加有效曝光时间、保持视场、维持一致的光学条件并免除大数据集的数值对齐需求；动态控制为电子显微镜实验的实时调控提供了通用框架，为定量成像、自动化原位研究和多模态采集开辟了新前景。

Abstract: In this work, we show how the specimen drift can be actively compensated by controlling the stage with precision down to the picometre scale. We do this by dynamic control, a generic real-time feedback framework designed to actively stabilize electron microscopy experiments by continuously monitoring an experimental variable from the detector data stream and compensating its evolution during acquisition. The framework, applicable to a broad range of controllable experimental instabilities, includes automated calibration procedures, operates in parallel with image acquisition and is implemented as a software plugin without requiring any hardware modification of the microscope. Results for live drift correction are shown for a selection of TEM and STEM instruments using conventional mechanical stages as well as piezoelectric stages. Experimental results are presented for medium resolution TEM, high-resolution TEM, HR-STEM and in situ observations. With piezoelectric stages, specimen stabilization down to the picometre scale was achieved allowing drift-corrected atomic-resolution imaging. Specimen stage-based stabilization significantly improves long-exposure imaging and in situ experiments by increasing the effective exposure time, preserving the field of view, maintaining identical optical conditions and eliminating the need for numerical alignment of large datasets. Beyond the specific application presented here, dynamic control provides a versatile framework for real-time regulation of electron microscopy experiments and opens new perspectives for quantitative imaging, automated in situ studies and multimodal acquisitions.

</details>


### [158] [Alloy engineering of excitonic properties in TMD monolayers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.03347)
*Eirini Katsipoulaki,Adlen Smiri,Panagiotis Spiliotakis,Konstantinos Mourzidis,Danae Katrisioti,Takashi Taniguchi,Kenji Watanabe,Georgios Kopidakis,Zdenek Sofer,Gang Wang,Emmanuel Stratakis,George Kioseoglou,Iann C. Gerber,Xavier Marie,Ioannis Paradisanos*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 本文利用光学光谱系统研究了单层MoS₂ₓSe₂₍₁₋ₓ₎合金在全组分范围内的性质，发现光学带隙可连续调谐约0.35 eV，B–A激子劈裂系统性减小，平均声子能量从Se富集到S富集合金按约化质量标度模型递增，且圆极化率从MoSe₂的近零单调增加至MoS₂的约15%（78 K）。极化演化归因于合金引起的电子结构修饰对亮–暗激子混合及谷退极化的影响，证明合金工程是调控TMD单层激子性质的有效策略。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 过渡金属硫族化合物（TMD）单层的激子性质可通过合金化进行调控，但以往研究缺乏对全组分范围内光学性质的系统性理解。

Method: 采用光学光谱（optical spectroscopy），结合温度依赖测量和偏振分辨光谱（polarization-resolved spectroscopy），并辅以密度泛函理论（density functional theory）计算进行对比验证。

Result: 光学带隙连续调谐约0.35 eV；B–A激子劈裂随组分系统性减小，与DFT计算吻合；平均声子能量从Se富集到S富集合金递增，符合约化质量标度模型；78 K下圆极化率从MoSe₂的近零单调增至MoS₂的约15%。

Conclusion: 合金工程（alloy engineering）被证实为调控TMD单层激子性质的有效策略，其对电子结构的修饰可显著影响亮–暗激子混合与谷退极化过程。

Abstract: We investigate monolayer MoS$_{2x}$Se$_{2(1-x)}$ alloys across the full composition range using optical spectroscopy. We demonstrate continuous tuning of the optical gap over $\sim$0.35 eV, accompanied by a systematic reduction of the B--A exciton splitting, in agreement with density functional theory calculations. Temperature-dependent measurements reveal a progressive increase of the average phonon energy from Se-rich to S-rich alloys that follows a simple reduced-mass scaling model. Polarization-resolved spectroscopy further shows a monotonic increase of the circular polarization from nearly zero in MoSe$_2$ to $\sim$15\% in MoS$_2$ at 78 K. The observed evolution of the polarization is attributed to alloy-induced modifications of the electronic structure that modify bright--dark exciton mixing and the associated valley depolarization. These findings establish alloy engineering as an effective strategy for controlling excitonic properties in TMD monolayers.

</details>


### [159] [Magnetic and crystal electric field excitations in a spin-orbit coupled frustrated hyperkagome magnet Nd$_3$Li$_3$W$_2$O$_{12}$](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.03365)
*R. Kolay,S. Guchhait,Indrajeet S. Rathore,S. Nandi,M. D. Le,N. B. Christensen,R. Nath*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 本文通过磁化率、比热和非弹性中子散射（INS）实验，对新型稀土石榴石Nd₃Li₃W₂O₁₂（一种Nd³⁺基超石榴石（hyperkogome）反铁磁体）进行了全面研究，确定了其晶体电场（CEF）方案，并证实其在低温下具有有效自旋J_eff=1/2的Kramers双重态基态。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 稀土基石榴石为研究超石榴石晶格中受挫驱动的磁性性质提供了可行平台。此前对该类体系中Nd³⁺基化合物的磁性与晶体电场效应研究尚不充分，因此作者旨在通过多种实验手段全面表征一种新化合物Nd₃Li₃W₂O₁₂的磁性行为和CEF能级结构。

Method: 采用磁化率测量、热容量测量和非弹性中子散射（inelastic neutron scattering, INS）三种实验手段，对Nd₃Li₃W₂O₁₂进行系统表征。通过同时拟合不同温度下的INS谱确定CEF哈密顿量（CEF Hamiltonian）和Kramers双重态（Kramers' doublets）的能量本征值，并利用获得的CEF参数模拟磁化率、磁等温线和磁比热数据。

Result: 磁化率测量显示主导的反铁磁相互作用，低温居里-外斯温度（Curie-Weiss temperature）θ_CW^LT ≈ -0.2 K。磁场下磁比热数据呈现两个宽峰，表明存在由CEF效应导致的多级Schottky反常，并显示两步磁熵释放。在低至0.1 K的温度下未观察到磁长程有序。INS实验揭示Nd³⁺（J=9/2）离子在D₂点群对称性下的非色散CEF激发，通过CEF参数模拟成功复现了实验磁化率、磁等温线和磁比热数据。

Conclusion: 热力学性质和INS衍生的晶体电场方案共同证实，该化合物在低温下具有有效自旋J_eff=1/2的Kramers双重态基态，为理解超石榴石晶格中的受挫磁性和低维量子磁性行为提供了重要实验依据。

Abstract: Rare-earth based garnets provide a viable platform for studying the frustrated driven magnetic properties of the hyperkagome lattices. Herein, we report a comprehensive study of the magnetic properties and crystal electric field (CEF) scheme of a new Nd$^{3+}$ based hyperkagome antiferromagnet, Nd$_3$Li$_3$W$_2$O$_{12}$ belonging to the garnet family via magnetization, heat capacity, and inelastic neutron scattering (INS) measurements. Magnetization measurement reveals a dominant antiferromagnetic interaction with a low temperature Curie-Weiss temperature $θ_{\rm CW}^{\rm LT} \simeq -0.2$ K. Two broad maxima are observed in the magnetic heat capacity data under magnetic fields, implying multilevel Schottky anomalies due to the effect of CEF and display a two-step magnetic entropy release. No magnetic long-range order is observed down to 0.1 K. The CEF excitations of the Nd$^{3+}$ ($J=9/2$) ion with $D_2$ point group symmetry, probed via INS experiments, show non-dispersive excitations characterizing the transitions among the CEF energy levels. The simultaneous fit of the INS spectra at different temperatures enabled the mapping of the CEF Hamiltonian and the energy eigenvalues of the Kramers' doublets. The simulation using the obtained CEF parameters reproduces the experimental magnetic susceptibility, magnetic isotherms, and magnetic heat capacity data. The thermodynamic properties and INS-derived crystal-field scheme confirm a Kramers' doublet ground state with an effective spin $J_{\rm eff} = 1/2$ at low temperatures.

</details>


### [160] [Anisotropic Phonon Heat Flow and Thermoelectric Response in Tetragonal GeS$_2$ and GeSe$_2$](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.03478)
*Neeraj Kulhari,Krishna Swaroop Sharma,K. C. Bhamu*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 本研究通过密度泛函理论（density functional theory）等多种计算方法，系统研究了四方晶系GeS₂和GeSe₂的电子结构、晶格动力学、成键、弹性响应及各向异性热电输运性质，发现两者具有显著的晶格热导率各向异性，并预测了中等水平的热电优值（zT）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 揭示四方晶系GeX₂（X=S, Se）化合物的热电输运各向异性机制，评估其作为各向异性热电材料的潜力。

Method: 采用密度泛函理论、密度泛函微扰理论（density functional perturbation theory）、Wannier插值（Wannier interpolation）和考虑散射的玻尔兹曼输运（scattering-aware Boltzmann transport）方法进行计算；使用HSE03/Wannier方法计算带隙，ShengBTE的弛豫时间近似（RTA）计算晶格热导率，AMSET计算电子输运系数，LOBSTER分析成键性质。

Result: GeS₂和GeSe₂的HSE03带隙分别为2.48 eV和1.23 eV；S被Se取代使高频声子频率从约13.6 THz降至10.3 THz。300 K下面内/跨面晶格热导率分别为26.86/1.19 W m⁻¹K⁻¹（GeS₂）和18.74/1.52 W m⁻¹K⁻¹（GeSe₂）。n型跨面GeS₂在800 K、1×10¹⁹ cm⁻³时zT=0.257；p型跨面GeSe₂在800 K、3×10²⁰ cm⁻³时zT=0.066。成键分析表明Ge–X具有混合共价-离子性质，Ge–S键的ICOHP幅值大于Ge–Se键。

Conclusion: 四方晶系GeX₂化合物是强各向异性热电材料，具有中等大小的zT值；其跨面（cross-plane）热电响应受益于被抑制的晶格热输运。

Abstract: The electronic structure, lattice dynamics, bonding, elastic response, and anisotropic thermoelectric transport properties of tetragonal GeS$_2$ and GeSe$_2$ were investigated using density functional theory, density functional perturbation theory, Wannier interpolation, and scattering-aware Boltzmann transport. The relaxed structures are mechanically and dynamically stable within the calculated harmonic description. The HSE03/Wannier band gaps are 2.48 eV for GeS$_2$ and 1.23 eV for GeSe$_2$, while substitution of S by Se lowers the upper phonon frequency from approximately 13.6 to 10.3 THz.
  The phonon Boltzmann transport calculations reveal pronounced lattice-transport anisotropy. Within the relaxation-time approximation, the 300 K in-plane and cross-plane lattice thermal conductivities are 26.86 and 1.19 W m$^{-1}$ K$^{-1}$ for GeS$_2$, and 18.74 and 1.52 W m$^{-1}$ K$^{-1}$ for GeSe$_2$, respectively. At 800 K, these values decrease to 10.22 and 0.46 W m$^{-1}$ K$^{-1}$ for GeS$_2$, and 7.25 and 0.58 W m$^{-1}$ K$^{-1}$ for GeSe$_2$. Frequency-resolved analysis shows that low-frequency phonons carry most of the heat, whereas the small cross-plane values reflect restricted out-of-plane phonon transport.
  Combining the ShengBTE RTA lattice tensors with AMSET electronic coefficients gives $zT=0.257$ for n-type cross-plane GeS$_2$ at 800 K and $10^{19}$ cm$^{-3}$. The corresponding PBE-AMSET estimate for GeSe$_2$ is $zT=0.066$ for p-type cross-plane transport at 800 K and $3\times10^{20}$ cm$^{-3}$. LOBSTER analysis identifies mixed covalent--ionic Ge--X bonding, with Ge--S bonds having a larger stabilizing ICOHP magnitude than Ge--Se bonds ($-5.27$ versus $-4.74$ eV per bond). These results identify tetragonal GeX$_2$ compounds as strongly anisotropic thermoelectrics with moderate calculated $zT$ values whose cross-plane response benefits from suppressed lattice heat transport.

</details>


### [161] [Spin-Chirality-Driven Bulk Photovoltaic Effect in van der Waals Magnet CrSBr](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.03492)
*Dezhao Wu,Yong Xu,Meng Ye,Wenhui Duan*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 本文在双层反铁磁（antiferromagnetic）CrSBr材料中，理论证明了由矢量自旋手性（vector spin chirality）驱动的新型时间反演偶（time-reversal-even）体光伏效应（bulk photovoltaic effect），包括手性位移电流（chiral shift current, CSC）和手性注入电流（chiral injection current, CIC），并展示了其通过磁场诱导自旋倾斜实现开关、反向和强度连续调谐的非凡可控性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统体光伏效应依赖于非中心对称晶体结构或共线磁有序，其调控手段有限；磁性材料中体光伏效应可被大幅丰富，需要探索新的可调物理机制。作者旨在建立一种基于矢量自旋手性的新型磁体光伏效应，作为可调旋钮（tunable knob）来实现非常规的光电流生成与控制。

Method: 以双层反铁磁CrSBr为原型材料，通过理论计算证明CSC和CIC的涌现。利用磁场诱导自旋倾斜（spin canting）作为调控手段，系统考察倾斜方向与倾角对两种电流的方向、开关状态和强度的影响，并揭示其背后的光学跃迁通道。

Result: CSC和CIC在CrSBr中产生，其量级可与源自非中心对称晶体结构或共线磁有序的传统光伏电流相当。两者展现出卓越的可调性：可被磁场诱导的自旋倾斜开关，随倾斜方向反转而反向，并可通过倾斜角调节强度连续变化。此外，还发现了一种控制这两种电流的非常规光学跃迁通道。

Conclusion: 该工作建立了具有显著可控性的非常规磁性体光伏效应，为在非共线磁体（noncollinear magnets）中发展光电子学（optoelectronics）和磁传感（magnetic sensing）应用开辟了道路。

Abstract: The bulk photovoltaic effect (BPVE) can be greatly enriched in magnetic materials. Here, we establish vector spin chirality as a tunable knob for generating an unconventional time-reversal-even magnetic BPVE, comprising the chiral shift current (CSC) and chiral injection current (CIC). Using bilayer antiferromagnetic (AFM) CrSBr as a prototype, we theoretically demonstrate the emergence of CSC and CIC. Compared with conventional photovoltaic currents arising from noncentrosymmetric crystal structures or collinear magnetic orderings, CSC and CIC not only possess comparable magnitudes but also exhibit exceptional tunability. Specifically, they can be switched on and off by magnetic-field-induced spin canting, reversed in direction upon canting-direction reversal, and continuously modulated in intensity via canting-angle variation. Furthermore, we reveal an unusual optical transition channel governing both currents in CrSBr. Our work establishes an unconventional magnetic BPVE with remarkable controllability, paving the way for applications in optoelectronics and magnetic sensing in noncollinear magnets.

</details>


### [162] [The New High-entropy Compound RhMnFeCoGe4 with Cubic Non-centrosymmetric B20 Structure](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.03523)
*V. A. Sidorov,V. N. Krasnorussky,A. V. Bokov,Z. N. Volkova,A. P. Gerashchenko,N. M. Chtchelkatchev,M. V. Magnitskaya,D. A. Salamatin,A. V. Semeno,V. V. Brazhkin,A. V. Tsvyashchenko*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 本文报道了一种新型高熵化合物RhMnFeCoGe₄，其在高压高温条件下合成，具有立方非中心对称B20结构。该材料呈现铁磁性行为，居里温度T_C = 146 K，每个化学式单元的自发磁矩为2.5 μ_B。通过核磁共振（NMR）和从头算（ab initio）计算确定了各成分的磁矩，发现晶格压缩会导致T_C升高。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索具有非中心对称B20结构的新型高熵化合物，并系统研究其电输运与磁性性质，以拓展高熵材料家族并深化对其物理特性的理解。

Method: 在高压高温条件下合成RhMnFeCoGe₄；测量其在常压和高压下的电输运及磁性；在4.2 K下获取NMR谱；结合从头算（ab initio）计算确定晶格参数、磁矩、态密度（density of states）和能带结构（band structure）。

Result: 材料呈铁磁性，T_C = 146 K，自发磁矩2.5 μ_B/分子式单位；临界区磁化数据分析得临界指数β = 0.337(1)、γ = 1.121(1)、δ = 4.326(1)；NMR测得Mn和Co磁矩分别为2.2 μ_B和0.5 μ_B；从头算计算给出合理的晶格参数与各组分磁矩，并确定了顺磁与铁磁态的态密度和能带结构；晶格压缩导致T_C升高。

Conclusion: RhMnFeCoGe₄是一种新型铁磁性高熵化合物，其基本磁性与电子结构性质已被系统表征。外部压力引起的晶格压缩可增强磁有序，提高居里温度，为该类材料的进一步研究提供了基础。

Abstract: A novel high-entropy compound, RhMnFeCoGe$_4$, with a cubic non-centrosymmetric B20 struc- ture, has been synthesized under conditions of high pressure and temperature. The electrical transport and magnetic properties of the obtained compound at both ambient and elevated pres- sures have been investigated. In addition, nuclear magnetic resonance (NMR) spectra were obtained at 4.2 K and ab initio calculations were performed. The new material exhibits ferromag- netic behavior with a critical temperature of $T_C$ = 146 K and a spontaneous moment of 2.5 $μ_B$ per formula unit. The magnetization data obtained at the critical region yielded the critical temperature and exponents, which were found to be $T_C$ = 146(1) K, $β$ = 0.337(1), $γ$ = 1.121(1), and $δ$ = 4.326(1). The magnetic moments of Mn and Co were determined from NMR spectra to be 2.2 $μ_B$ and 0.5 $μ_B$, respectively. Ab initio calculations yielded reasonable values for the lattice parameter and the magnetic moments of all constituents. The density of states and band structure are determined for both paramagnetic and ferromagnetic states. Lattice compression results in the increase in the $T_C$.

</details>


### [163] [Strain Tuning of Orbital-Driven Giant Magnetoresistance in van der Waals ferrimagnet Mn$_3$Si$_2$Te$_6$](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.03568)
*Abdul Ahad,Miuko Tanaka,Shunta Aoki,Darius-Alexandru Deaconu,Varun Shah,Tenta Kitamura,Hao Ou,Mohammad Saeed Bahramy,Jiang Pu,Toshiya Ideue*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 本文系统研究了范德华磁体Mn₃Si₂Te₆在单轴应变（uniaxial strain）下的磁输运行为，发现应变可调控手性轨道流畴（chiral orbital-current domains），并统一解释了其非常规巨磁阻（colossal magnetoresistance）的微观机制，提出应变可作为范德华磁体的高效调控参数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 应变工程（strain engineering）有望通过机械方式调控量子磁体的电子态和磁畴结构，为可编程自旋电子学（spintronics）提供新路径。范德华磁体兼具高结晶度和良好的力学柔性，可承受大且精确可控的应变，从而呈现传统固体无法实现的应变诱导功能。

Method: 作者对范德华磁体Mn₃Si₂Te₆块体晶体施加大应变（in situ large strain），系统测量其电阻随应变的变化；同时在剥离获得的单畴薄片器件上进行应变调控实验，结合轨道磁矩（orbital magnetic moment）对电子结构的影响进行对照分析。

Result: 实验发现单轴应变可显著调制块体晶体的电阻；该效应可一致地由应变可调的手性轨道流畴模型解释。在仅含单个手性畴的剥离薄片器件中，观测到应变直接调控受轨道磁矩影响的电子结构，从而建立了非常规巨磁阻的统一微观机制。

Conclusion: 应变被确认为调控范德华磁体中电子态与磁态的高效控制参数，研究为基于轨道自由度实现自旋-应变电子学（spin-straintronics）功能提供了概念框架。

Abstract: Strain engineering of magnetotransport offers a powerful strategy for uncovering emergent electronic and domain phenomena in quantum magnetic materials, while providing a promising pathway toward next-generation mechanically programmable spintronic technologies. Van der Waals magnets are particularly attractive in this context because their high crystallinity and mechanical flexibility allow exceptionally large, precisely controllable strain, enabling access to strain-induced functionalities unattainable in conventional solids. Here, we report systematic strain control of the van der Waals magnet Mn$_3$Si$_2$Te$_6$, which exhibits an unconventional colossal magnetoresistance whose microscopic origin remains under debate. We demonstrate in situ large-strain modulation of the electrical resistance in bulk crystals and show that the effect can be consistently explained by strain-tunable chiral orbital-current domains. Furthermore, measurements on exfoliated flake devices containing a single chiral domain reveal direct strain control of the electronic structure affected by orbital magnetic moment, establishing a unified microscopic mechanism for the unconventional colossal magnetoresistance. These results identify strain as an exceptionally effective control parameter for tailoring electronic and magnetic states in van der Waals magnets and provide a conceptual framework for realizing spin-straintronic functionalities based on orbital degrees of freedom.

</details>


### [164] [Toward two dimensional MoS2 electrets](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.03602)
*Eugenio Lunedei,Andrea Liscio,Francesco Borgatti,Edoardo Chini,Pasquale DAngelo,Fiorenza Esposito,Niccolo Borghi,Matteo Mannini,Denis Gentili,Luca Seravalli,Massimiliano Cavallini*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 本文证明通过受控硫空位工程可将单层MoS2转化为驻极体（electret），使其在单个原子层内具备存储准永久静电电荷的能力。硫空位通过印章辅助无电极电化学纳米光刻技术生成，实现亚微米空间控制和可编程缺陷密度，产生高达1 μC/cm²的表面电荷密度并保持数百天。空位区域同时作为深电子陷阱和激子猝灭区，实现静电与光学对比度的共定位，确立缺陷工程MoS2为首个二维驻极体。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 二维材料中能否在单个原子层内实现静电电荷的准永久存储尚属未知，探索原子级缺陷作为功能单元编码静电和激子行为的潜力，是拓展二维材料功能化应用的关键问题。

Method: 采用印章辅助无电极电化学纳米光刻（stamp-assisted electrode-free electrochemical nanolithography）在单层MoS2中生成硫空位，实现亚微米级空间控制和缺陷密度从10¹⁰到10¹³ cm⁻²的可编程调节；通过开尔文探针显微镜（Kelvin probe microscopy）和静电力显微镜（electric force microscopy）直接表征静电图案，并结合时间分辨光致发光检测激子寿命变化。

Result: 硫空位区域作为深电子陷阱产生高达1 μC/cm²的表面电荷密度，电荷在环境条件下可保持数百天；静电图案精确复刻光刻构型。空位同时形成激子猝灭区，使表观激子寿命从15 ns缩短至180 ps，且光学响应持续一年以上，证明硫空位而非瞬态电荷态是图案化功能的来源。

Conclusion: 该研究确立了缺陷工程MoS2作为首个二维驻极体的地位，证明原子空位可作为功能单元在单个原子层内同时编码静电和激子行为，为二维材料在信息存储、传感和光电器件中的集成应用开辟了新途径。

Abstract: Here, we show that controlled sulfur vacancy engineering converts monolayer MoS2 into an electret, imparting the ability to store quasi-permanent electrostatic charge within a single atomic layer. Sulfur vacancies are generated with submicrometer spatial control by stamp-assisted electrode-free electrochemical nanolithography, yielding programmable defect densities from 10 10 to 10 13 1 cm-2. The resulting vacancy domains act as deep electron traps and produce surface charge densities up to 1 uCcm-2, with charge retention in the order of hundreds days under ambient conditions. Kelvin probe and electric force microscopies directly reveal stable electrostatic patterns that replicate the lithographic motif. The same vacancy landscape simultaneously defines exciton-quenching regions, generating co-localized optical and electrostatic contrast and reducing the apparent exciton lifetime from 15 ns to 180 ps through enhanced nonradiative recombination. The persistence of the optical response over one year identifies sulfur vacancies, rather than transient charge states, as the origin of the patterned functionality. These results establish defect-engineered MoS2 as the first two-dimensional electret and demonstrate that atomic vacancies can be exploited as functional elements for encoding electrostatic and excitonic behavior in a single atomic layer.

</details>


### [165] [Epitaxial SiGeSn alloys for CMOS-compatible thermoelectric devices](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.03638)
*Patrizio Graziosi,Damiano Marian,Andrea Tomadin,Stefano Roddaro,Omar Concepción,Johnny Tiscareño-Ramírez,Agnieszka Anna Corley-Wiciak,Dan Buca,Giovanni Capellini,Michele Virgilio*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 本文评估了新型CMOS兼容材料体系——异质外延SiGeSn合金的热电性能。通过Boltzmann输运方程并结合3-ω测量得到的低晶格热导率，计算发现该材料在300–400 K（典型片上温度）范围内p型和n型材料的优值系数（figure of merit）ZT均超过1，功率因子（power factor）约为20 μW/cm·K²，表明该材料在实际应用中具有极佳前景。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 将热电（thermoelectric）器件集成到主流微电子技术平台，可能在Green-IT领域实现重大突破。因此有必要评估新型CMOS兼容材料体系SiGeSn合金的热电性能，以探索其在热电器件中的潜在应用。

Method: 首先通过3-ω测量方法实验测定SiGeSn/Ge/Si层的晶格热导率，约为1–2 W/m·K；随后利用Boltzmann输运方程（Boltzmann transport equation）计算优值系数ZT，计算中考虑了该多谷（multi-valley）材料体系特有的谷间散射（inter-valley scattering）过程。

Result: 在300–400 K（典型片上温度）范围内，p型和n型材料的ZT值均超过1；功率因子达到具有竞争力的约20 μW/cm·K²。

Conclusion: 该新型Si基材料体系在实际应用中具有极好的前景，可进一步激发该领域相关的科学研究。

Abstract: The integration of thermoelectric devices into mainstream microelectronic technological platform could be a major breakthrough in various fields within the \emph{so-called} Green-IT realm. In this article, the thermoelectric properties of heteroepitaxial SiGeSn alloys, a novel CMOS compatible material system, are evaluated to assess their possible application in thermoelectric devices. To this purpose, starting from the experimentally low lattice thermal conductivity of SiGeSn/Ge/Si layers of about $\sim$1-2 W/m$\cdot$K assessed by means of 3-$ω$ measurements, the figure of merits are calculated through the use of Boltzmann transport equation, taking into account the relevant inter-valley scattering processes, peculiar of this multi-valley material system. Values for the figure of merit $ZT$ exceeding $1$ have been obtained for both p- and n- type material at operating temperatures within the 300---400 K range, i.e. at a typical On-Chip temperatures. In this interval, the predicted power factor also features very competitive values of the order of 20 $\rm{μW/cm\cdot K^2}$. Our finding indicates that this new class of Si-based materials has extremely good prospects for real-world applications, and can further stimulate scientific investigation in this ambit.

</details>


### [166] [Machine Learning-Guided Screening of Advantageous Solvents for Solid Polymer Electrolytes in Lithium Metal Batteries](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.03688)
*Jiadong Shen,Junjie Chen,Xiaosa Xu,Jin Li,Zhenyu Wang,Pengzhu Lin,Zixiao Guo,Yu Wang,Jing Sun,Baoling Huang,Tianshou Zhao*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 本文通过高吞吐量密度泛函理论（high-throughput DFT）对约10,000种溶剂进行机器学习，建立了连接电子性质（HOMO、LUMO）与宏观性质（介电常数、偶极矩、极化率）的通用筛选准则，并识别出N-甲氧基-N-甲基-2,2,2-三氟乙酰胺等候选溶剂。实验将该溶剂引入聚偏氟乙烯-六氟丙烯（PVDF-HFP）基体后，实现了4.5 V电化学窗口、5.5×10⁻⁴ S cm⁻¹的电导率（30°C）和0.78的锂离子迁移数，在多种正极体系中展现出优异的循环稳定性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 固体聚合物电解质（solid polymer electrolytes, SPEs）中残留溶剂的痕量存在显著影响电解质和界面性质，但溶剂种类繁多、性质复杂，缺乏通用的筛选方法。作者希望建立一种普适性准则，以系统性地筛选最优残留溶剂，从而提升离子电导率（ionic conductivity）和迁移数（transference number）。

Method: 基于约10,000种溶剂的高吞吐量DFT数据集，利用机器学习（machine learning）建立电子性质（HOMO、LUMO能级）与宏观性质（介电常数、偶极矩、极化率）之间的关联，形成统一的筛选标准。随后将筛选出的候选溶剂实验性地掺入聚偏氟乙烯-六氟丙烯基体中，进行电化学表征和电池性能测试。

Result: 识别出N-甲氧基-N-甲基-2,2,2-三氟乙酰胺和2,2,2-三氟-N,N-二甲基乙酰胺两种溶剂。掺入前者的PVDF-HFP电解质实现了4.5 V电化学窗口、5.5×10⁻⁴ S cm⁻¹电导率（30°C）和0.78的锂离子迁移数；LiFePO₄电池在500次循环后保持86.7%容量，LiNi₀.₉Co₀.₀₅Mn₀.₀₅O₂电池在2C倍率下200次循环后保持98.7%容量，性能优于四种对照溶剂体系。

Conclusion: 该集成方法建立了面向合理SPE设计的通用溶剂筛选范式，通过电子-宏观性质的协同调控实现了平衡的离子传输、宽电化学稳定窗口和优异循环耐久性，为开发更安全、高能量密度的锂金属电池（lithium metal batteries）和加速下一代电池开发提供了路径。

Abstract: Trace residual solvents in solid polymer electrolytes (SPEs) significantly affect electrolyte and interface properties, where optimal selection enhances ionic conductivity and transference numbers. However, solvent complexity hinders general screening methods. We establish a universal criterion linking electronic (HOMO, LUMO) and macroscopic properties (dielectric constant, dipole moment, polarizability) via machine learning on an approximately 10,000-solvent dataset from high-throughput DFT. Two solvents, N-methoxy-N-methyl-2,2,2-trifluoroacetamide and 2,2,2-trifluoro-N,N-dimethylacetamide, were identified. Experimental incorporation of trace N-methoxy-N-methyl-2,2,2-trifluoroacetamide into a poly(vinylidene fluoride-co-hexafluoropropylene) matrix achieves a 4.5 V window, 5.5x10^-4 S cm^-1 conductivity (30 C), and 0.78 Li+ transference number. The cell retains 86.7% capacity over 500 cycles (LiFePO4) and 98.7% after 200 cycles at 2C (LiNi0.9Co0.05Mn0.05O2), outperforming 2,2,2-trifluoro-N,N-dimethylacetamide, dimethylformamide, N-methyl-2-pyrrolidone, and dimethyl sulfoxide. This synergy enables balanced ion transport, wide stability, and cycling durability, advancing safer, high-energy lithium metal batteries. Our integrated approach establishes a solvent screening paradigm for rational SPE design, accelerating next-generation battery development.

</details>


### [167] [Guided Synthesis of EMT Zeolites by Machine Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.03760)
*Emmanuel A. Olanrewaju,Santosh Adhikari,Zhiyin Niu,Michael Nikolaou,Jeremy C. Palmer,Jeffrey D. Rimer,Mingjian Wen*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 本研究开发了机器学习（machine learning）模型来指导EMT沸石的合成条件发现，基于174次实验合成数据，利用经典ML方法和预训练基础模型（foundation models）预测沸石骨架类型。模型成功识别出6个有前景的新合成条件，其中5个经实验验证成功合成EMT晶体，证明了数据驱动方法在加速沸石合成中的有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: EMT沸石可在温和条件（低温、无需有机结构导向剂）下合成，具有低成本和环境可持续的生产优势，但选择性生成EMT而非类似骨架（如FAU）的具体合成条件尚未明确建立，因此需要系统化的方法来确定关键合成参数。

Method: 构建包含174次实验合成尝试的数据集，记录反应时间、温度、硅源和铝源、Si/Al化学计量比等合成参数；同时应用经典ML方法（classical ML methods）和预训练基础模型（pretrained foundation models）来预测沸石骨架结果，并通过特征重要性（feature importance）分析识别关键参数，进而探索合成空间以发现新条件。

Result: 特征重要性分析确认了EMT形成的关键参数并验证已知合成原理；模型探索出6个有前景的新合成条件，其中5个经实验验证成功结晶出EMT，包括2个Si/Al化学计量比超出训练集范围的情况；独立文献报道的合成条件评估进一步证明了模型的泛化能力（generalizability）。

Conclusion: 该工作展示了数据驱动方法在加速沸石合成中的潜力，实现了机器学习预测与实验验证的闭环（closing the loop），为材料合成条件的智能化发现提供了可行范式。

Abstract: Zeolites are microporous crystalline materials with diverse frameworks, widely used in industrial applications such as petroleum refining and molecular separation. Unlike most zeolites, EMT can be synthesized under mild conditions (at low temperatures and without the use of organic structure-directing agents), making it attractive for cost-effective and environmentally sustainable production. However, the specific synthesis conditions that selectively produce EMT rather than similar frameworks like FAU are not yet well established. In this work, we develop machine learning (ML) models to guide the discovery of synthesis conditions for EMT zeolites. Our dataset comprises 174 experimental synthesis attempts, recording reaction time, temperature, silica and alumina sources, Si/Al stoichiometric ratio, and other synthesis parameters. We apply both classical ML methods and pretrained foundation models to predict zeolite framework outcomes from these synthesis parameters. Feature importance analysis identifies critical parameters for EMT formation, validating known synthesis principles. Leveraging the ML models, we explore the synthesis space and identify six promising new conditions for EMT formation. Experimental validation confirms EMT crystallization in five cases, including two with Si/Al stoichiometric ratios outside the training dataset's range. Evaluation on independent literature-reported synthesis conditions further demonstrates the generalizability of the model. This work demonstrates a data-driven approach to accelerating zeolite synthesis, closing the loop between ML prediction and experimental validation.

</details>


### [168] [From phase transformation to amorphization: damage accumulation in Yb-implanted \b{eta}-Ga2O3](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.03798)
*Joanna Matulewicz,Renata Ratajczak,Ewa Grzanka,Maciej Oskar Liedke,Damian Kalita,Eric Hirschmann,Andreas Wagner,Mikolaj Grabowski,Michal A. Stróżyk,Cyprian Mieszczyński,Przemyslaw Jóźwik,Ulrich Kentsch,Rene Heller,Frederico Garrido*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 本文系统研究了不同取向的β-Ga₂O₃单晶在Yb离子注入（通量范围5×10¹²至1×10¹⁶ cm⁻²，对应0.04–74 dpa）下的辐照响应与结构演化，揭示了多阶段缺陷演化过程：在约0.4 dpa临界阈值下发生由单斜β相到缺陷尖晶石γ相的结构相变，随后γ相内缺陷持续积累，最终在约7 dpa时表面层非晶化并逐渐扩展取代γ相，挑战了该材料此前被认为具有高辐照稳定性的观点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 氧化镓（gallium oxide）作为宽禁带半导体在功率电子和深紫外光电器件中具有重要应用前景，但对其在高通量离子辐照条件下的辐射耐受性（radiation tolerance）尚缺乏系统认识，特别是此前研究暗示其γ相具有高辐照稳定性，需要更全面的实验证据予以检验。

Method: 采用多技术联用手段（multi-technique approach），包括卢瑟福背散射/沟道谱（RBS/c）、正电子湮没谱（PAS）、高分辨透射电子显微镜（HRTEM）和高分辨X射线衍射（HRXRD），对不同取向的β-Ga₂O₃单晶在宽通量范围的Yb离子注入下的损伤累积机制进行综合分析。

Result: 实验揭示多阶段缺陷演化：在约0.4 dpa时晶格应变积累触发β→γ相变并伴随应变弛豫；继续辐照导致γ-Ga₂O₃晶格内缺陷发展，损伤累积曲线出现明显下降特征且注入层亚表面区域出现层错；在约7 dpa时表面层非晶化，随后非晶层扩展并逐渐取代瞬态γ相；缺陷的类型和密度表现出强烈的深度依赖性，位移原子和空位型缺陷均显著增加。

Conclusion: 氧化镓的辐射耐受性高度依赖于离子-材料特异相互作用和应变诱导的不稳定性，这挑战了该材料在高通量离子辐照下具有鲁棒性的假设，表明此前关于γ-Ga₂O₃高辐射稳定性的报道需要修正。

Abstract: This study provides a comprehensive analysis of the radiation response and structural evolution of differently oriented $β$-Ga$_{2}$O$_{3}$ single crystals subjected to Yb ion implantation over a wide fluence range from $5 \times 10^{12}$ to $1 \times 10^{16}$~cm$^{-2}$ ($0.04$--$74$~dpa). A multi-technique approach (RBS/c, PAS, HRTEM, and HRXRD) was employed to investigate the mechanisms of damage accumulation. The results reveal a multi-stage process of defect evolution. At a critical threshold of around $0.4$~dpa, the accumulation of lattice strain triggers a phase transformation from monoclinic $β$-Ga$_{2}$O$_{3}$ to a defective spinel structure of $γ$-Ga$_{2}$O$_{3}$. Notably, the formation of this new phase is accompanied by strain relaxation. With further irradiation, defects develop within the crystal structure of $γ$-Ga$_{2}$O$_{3}$. The associated atomic reorganization at this stage is reflected by a distinct dip in the damage accumulation curve and the appearance of stacking faults in the subsurface region of the implanted layer. In contrast to previous reports suggesting high radiation stability of this phase, the present study clearly demonstrates that continuous defect accumulation results in a significant increase in both displaced atoms and vacancy-type defects, with a strong depth dependence in their type and density. Ultimately, at an irradiation level of approximately $7$~dpa, the surface layer amorphizes. With further irradiation, the amorphous layer expands, gradually replacing the transient $γ$-Ga$_{2}$O$_{3}$ phase. These findings reveal that the radiation tolerance of gallium oxide is highly sensitive to ion-specific interactions and strain-induced instabilities, thereby challenging the previously assumed robustness of this material under high-fluence ion irradiation.

</details>


### [169] [Physics-Informed Machine Learning for Refractory Alloy Design](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.03805)
*Blaise Awola Ayirizia,Bimal K C,Jorge A. Munoz San Martin*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 本文开发了一个物理信息机器学习框架（physics-informed machine learning framework），结合分位数梯度提升（quantile gradient boosting）与 Born 稳定性约束，用于预测难熔复杂浓缩合金（refractory complex concentrated alloys, CCAs）的八种力学性能。基于 393 个 DFT 计算的合金数据，模型在保留测试集上取得 R² 0.89–0.97 的精度，且所有预测均满足 Born 稳定性判据。成分筛选识别出 MoReW 等高剪切性能候选合金，并建立了 VEC = 6.0–6.4、δ < 10% 及 Re-Mo-Cr 三元体系的设计规则，实现从万亿级成分空间中加速发现高性能难熔合金。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 难熔复杂浓缩合金在高温下具有优异力学性能，但其成分空间极为庞大（约 10^15 种组合），实验探索面临巨大挑战，迫切需要高效筛选策略来缩小候选范围。

Method: 构建物理信息机器学习框架，将分位数梯度提升（quantile gradient boosting）与 Born 稳定性约束（Born stability constraints）相结合；利用 393 个 DFT 计算的合金数据（涵盖 Cr、Hf、Mo、Nb、Re、Ta、Ti、V、W、Zr 十元素设计空间），训练模型预测八种力学性能（体模量 B、剪切模量 G、维氏硬度 Hv、弹性常数 C11/C12/C44、杨氏模量 E 及泊松比 ν）。

Result: 在 59 个保留测试样本上，六个性能的决定系数（R²）达 0.89 至 0.97，平均绝对误差（MAE）为 0.85 至 14.74 GPa；所有测试集预测均 100% 满足 Born 稳定性判据。成分筛选发现 MoReW 具有最高预测剪切刚度（C44 = 139 GPa）；元素分析显示铼（Re）出现在 100% 的高性能合金中、钼（Mo）占 90%、铬（Cr）占 80%。

Conclusion: 研究建立了量化设计规则：价电子浓度（VEC）在 6.0 至 6.4、原子尺寸失配（δ）小于 10%，且 Re-Mo-Cr 三元体系能最大化剪切阻力。该物理信息筛选框架能够从万亿级成分空间中加速发现力学稳定、高性能的难熔合金。

Abstract: Refractory complex concentrated alloys (CCAs) exhibit exceptional mechanical properties at elevated temperatures, but their vast compositional space (approximately 10^15 possible combinations) poses significant challenges for experimental exploration. We develop a physics-informed machine learning framework combining quantile gradient boosting with Born stability constraints to predict eight mechanical properties (bulk modulus B, shear modulus G, Vickers hardness Hv, elastic constants C11, C12, C44, Young's modulus E, and Poisson's ratio nu) of refractory CCAs. Using 393 density functional theory (DFT)-computed alloys from a 10-element design space (Cr, Hf, Mo, Nb, Re, Ta, Ti, V, W, Zr), we achieve coefficient of determination (R^2) values of 0.89 to 0.97 with mean absolute errors of 0.85 to 14.74 GPa across six properties on a held-out test set (n = 59). Remarkably, all test predictions satisfy the Born stability criteria (100% compliance), demonstrating the efficacy of physics-informed constraints. Compositional screening in valence electron concentration (VEC)-atomic size mismatch (delta) space identifies high-performance candidates, with MoReW exhibiting the highest predicted shear rigidity (C44 = 139 GPa). Elemental analysis reveals that rhenium appears in 100% of the top-performing alloys, molybdenum in 90%, and chromium in 80%, establishing quantitative design rules: VEC = 6.0 to 6.4, delta < 10%, and Re-Mo-Cr ternary systems maximize shear resistance. This physics-informed screening framework enables accelerated discovery of mechanically stable, high-performance refractory alloys from the trillion-scale compositional space.

</details>


### [170] [Generalized Space Groups from Internal Configuration Spaces](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.03808)
*Zeying Zhang,Zhenye Li,Zhi-Ming Yu,Gui-Bin Liu,Yugui Yao*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种统一构造方案，为具有非常规内部自由度的晶体生成广义空间群（generalized space groups）。该框架通过内部变换全群与参考对象稳定群出发，利用Goursat引理（Goursat's lemma）将内部商群与空间商群耦合，将普通、磁性、自旋和颜色空间群作为特例涵盖其中，并以十二面体对象为例验证了框架的有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统空间群（space group）理论仅适用于常规晶体的平移与点群对称性描述，难以统一处理具有磁矩、自旋等非常规内部自由度（internal degrees of freedom）的晶体系统。作者旨在建立一种统一的数学框架，将各类广义空间群纳入同一构造体系。

Method: 从内部变换的允许全群$G_P$和参考对象的稳定子（stabilizer）$P$出发，确定允许构型集的逐点对称性（pointwise symmetry）$J$与集合级对称性（setwise symmetry）$K$；然后利用Goursat引理将内部商群$K/J$与空间商群耦合成完整的广义空间群结构。

Result: 该框架统一涵盖了普通、磁性、自旋及颜色空间群（color space groups）等已知类型。作为示例，对具有$P=I\simeq A_5$稳定群的十二面体对象，获得了非平凡对$T\triangleleft O$（满足$O/T\simeq\mathbb Z_2$），由此构成的广义空间群支持拓扑荷（topological charge）$|C|=12$的点节点（point node）。

Conclusion: 所提出的统一构造框架为研究非常规晶体对称性提供了系统性数学工具，并展示了其在产生高拓扑荷节点的广义空间群中的应用潜力。

Abstract: We develop a unified construction of generalized space groups for crystals with unconventional internal degrees of freedom. Starting from the full group $G_P$ of allowed internal transformations and the stabilizer $P$ of a reference object, we determine the pointwise and setwise symmetries, $J$ and $K$, of the allowed configuration set. Goursat's lemma then couples the internal quotient $K/J$ to a spatial quotient. The framework includes ordinary, magnetic, spin, and color space groups as special cases. As an example, we consider a dodecahedral object with $P=I\simeq A_5$, for which we obtain the nontrivial pair $T\triangleleft O$ with $O/T\simeq\mathbb Z_2$. The resulting generalized space group hosts a point node with topological charge $|C|=12$.

</details>


### [171] [Optical centers in cubic boron nitride and diamond: remarkable similarities](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.03901)
*Konstantin Iakoubovskiia,Andrey Katrusha,Weihua Peng,Jianguo Peng*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 本文对同一立方压机中高温高压（high-pressure high-temperature）法制备的立方氮化硼（cubic boron nitride, cBN）与金刚石（diamond）的光吸收和发光特性进行了比较研究，发现两者在光谱和空间依赖性上存在显著相似性。基于金刚石缺陷的已有识别结果，作者初步将cBN中黄色对应的光学中心归因于氮位替代氧，并将多个发光中心（RC1、RC3、GC1、1.816 eV峰、BN1）分别归属为含氧缺陷、镍相关缺陷、硅-空位复合体及间隙原子相关缺陷。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 立方氮化硼与金刚石同为超硬材料，其光学缺陷中心的识别对理解材料性质及器件应用具有重要意义；然而cBN中缺陷的归属信息相对缺乏，需要借助与金刚石的对比来推进。

Method: 采用同一立方压机（cubic press）高温高压生长cBN与金刚石样品，对两者进行系统的光吸收（optical absorption）和发光（luminescence）光谱及空间分布测量，并将cBN的谱图与金刚石中已确认的缺陷特征进行对照分析。

Result: 发现cBN与金刚石在光谱和空间依赖性上具有显著相似性；据此将cBN中黄色对应的光学中心初步归因于氮位替代氧，RC1和RC3中心分别归因于含氧与硼空位复合体（中性态/负电荷态），GC1中心归因于镍相关缺陷，1.816 eV（683 nm）发光峰归因于硅-空位复合体，BN1中心归因于间隙原子相关缺陷。

Conclusion: 通过与金刚石的类比，可在尚未建立完整缺陷理论的cBN中建立初步的光学缺陷归属；这些结果为进一步验证和深入研究cBN缺陷结构与发光机制提供了基础。

Abstract: We present a comparative study of optical absorption and luminescence from cubic boron nitride (cBN) and diamond grown by the high-pressure high-temperature technique in the same cubic press. We note remarkable similarities in spectral and spatial dependences for these two materials. Using the previous identification of defects in diamond, we tentatively assign the optical center responsible for yellow color in some cBN crystals to substitutional oxygen at the nitrogen site, the RC1 and RC3 centers to a defect comprising substitutional oxygen and a boron vacancy in the neutral and negative charge states, respectively, the GC1 center to a nickel-related defect, the 1.816 eV (683 nm) luminescence peak to a Si-vacancy complex, and the BN1 center to an interstitial-related defect.

</details>


### [172] [Phonon-based determination of elastic coefficients in the Weyl semimetal TaAs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.03903)
*Fabián Jofré-Parra,Debankita Ghosh,Enrique Muñoz*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 本文通过第一性原理声子计算与弹性连续介质模型（elastic continuum model）相结合的方法，确定了Weyl半金属（Weyl semimetal）TaAs的完整弹性模量集合，所得结果与文献报道值吻合良好，并进一步推导出体积模量、剪切模量、杨氏模量及泊松比等标准弹性参数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 拓扑半金属中弹性性质的可靠确定对于理解应变相关效应至关重要，但常受方法学和计算方面的限制所阻碍，因此需要一种高效且可靠的计算途径。

Method: 将第一性原理声子计算（first-principles phonon calculations）与弹性连续介质模型相结合，从声学声子分支中提取声速，进而获得完整的弹性模量集合；随后推导标准弹性参数，包括体积模量（bulk modulus）、剪切模量（shear modulus）、杨氏模量（Young's modulus）和泊松比（Poisson ratio）。

Result: 获得的弹性模量与文献中此前报道的值表现出良好的一致性；该方法避免了传统基于应变的方法在施加剪切时需要使用大型超胞以及由此可能引发的电子基组中的应变诱导不一致性问题。

Conclusion: 该工作提供了一种计算上高效、可替代传统应变方法的途径，有望与Weyl半金属晶格动力学的实验表征手段（如拉曼散射（Raman scattering）或布里渊-曼德尔施塔姆散射（Brillouin-Mandelstam scattering））相衔接。

Abstract: Reliable determination of elastic properties in topological semimetals is essential for understanding strain-related effects, but is often hindered by methodological and computational limitations. In this work, we combine first-principles phonon calculations with an elastic continuum model to determine the elastic properties of Weyl semimetal TaAs. The sound velocities extracted from the acoustic phonon branches are used to obtain the full set of elastic moduli, which show good agreement with previously reported values in the literature. From these results, we derive standard elastic parameters such as bulk, shear, and Young's moduli, and the Poisson ratio. This approach highlights a computationally efficient alternative to conventional strain-based methods, avoiding the need for large supercells when shear is applied, and thus possible strain-induced inconsistencies in the electronic basis, while providing a possibility to connect with experimental characterizations (e.g. Raman or Brillouin-Mandelstam scattering) of the lattice dynamics in Weyl semimetals.

</details>


### [173] [Understanding and Designing Phase Change Materials: Insights from Atom Probe Tomography](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.03932)
*Jan Köttgen,Nils von den Driesch,Alexander Pawlis,Matthias Wuttig*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 本文综述了利用原子探针层析技术（atom probe tomography, APT）解释相变材料（phase change materials, PCMs）在非晶态与晶态之间快速可逆切换时光电性能显著变化的研究进展。核心发现是晶态PCMs具有高于55%的多元事件概率（Probability of Multiple Events, PME），表明其采用独特的金属价键（metavalent bonding, MVB）机制，而非晶态PCMs表现为典型的共价固体行为。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 相变材料在非晶态与晶态之间切换时伴随显著的光电性能变化，但其微观键合机制尚不明确，需借助先进表征手段揭示这一现象背后的物理本质。

Method: 通过APT技术获取两个关键的键合描述符，其中最重要的是多元事件概率（PME），该指标衡量激光辅助场蒸发中单个激光脉冲成功脱离一个以上离子的可能性；结合量子化学计算并构建化学键合图谱来定位和量化不同固体的键合特征。

Result: 晶态PCMs的PME高于55%，而金属和离子共价固体不具备此特征，证实晶态PCMs采用金属价键（MVB）机制；非晶态PCMs的PME则显著较低，表现为共价固体行为。高PME的晶态固体位于金属与离子共价固体之间的窄电导率范围内，表明电子局域化与离域化之间存在竞争。化学键合图谱显示金属价键固体位于相邻原子约共享一个电子且离子性不强的区域。

Conclusion: PCMs在结晶过程中发生键合机制的根本变化，从共价键合转变为金属价键（MVB）键合，这与量子化学计算一致。化学键合图谱现已被用于发现和解释PCMs在各应用领域中与性能相关的趋势。

Abstract: Phase Change Materials (PCMs) can be rapidly and reversibly switched between their amorphous and crystalline state; a transition which is accompanied by a pronounced change of optoelectronic properties. Here progress is reviewed to explain these property changes, focusing on advances by atom probe tomography (APT). This technique classifies bonding by providing two crucial bonding descriptors. Most important is the Probability of Multiple Events (PME), which is related to the likelihood that more than one ion is dislodged per successful laser pulse in laser assisted field evaporation. Crystalline PCMs are characterized by a PME above 55%, not found for metals or iono-covalent solids. This confirms that crystalline PCMs employ a unique bonding mechanism coined metavalent bonding (MVB). While crystalline PCMs employ MVB, amorphous PCMs behave as covalent solids characterized by a much lower PME. PCMs thus change their bonding upon crystallization, consistent with quantum-chemical calculations of bonding. Crystalline solids with a high PME lie in a narrow conductivity range between metals and iono-covalent solids, indicative for a competition between electron localization and delocalization. A map quantifying chemical bonding locates metavalent solids in a region where approximately one electron is shared between adjacent atoms and bonding is not too ionic. This quantum chemical bonding map is now used to find and explain property trends relevant for PCMs in various application domains.

</details>


### [174] [Emergence of Chiral Helimagnetic Order in Chromium-intercalated Tantalum Disulfide CrTa$_3$S$_6$ Powders with Controlled Intercalation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.03953)
*Ayaka Toshima,Keigo Mizutani,Yusuke Kousaka,Kazuki Ohishi,Hiroaki Shishido,Yoshihiko Togawa*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 本文报道了一种手性Cr插层过渡金属硫族化合物Cr_xTa_3S_6中磁性质的高度敏感性变化：Cr插层量x低于0.996时粉末样品呈现手性螺旋磁性（chiral helimagnetism），高于1.000时则表现为铁磁性（ferromagnetism）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究Cr_xTa_3S_6这一手性Cr插层过渡金属硫族化合物（transition-metal dichalcogenide）中磁性质随Cr插层量的敏感性变化，理解螺旋磁周期与样品维度之间的关系。

Method: 利用磁化曲线（magnetization curves）和小角中子散射（small-angle neutron scattering）对粉末样品进行磁性表征，并结合粉末与微加工晶体的样品尺寸差异分析螺旋磁周期的涌现与温度演化。

Result: 磁化曲线和小角中子散射数据表明，Cr插层量x低于0.996时粉末样品呈手性螺旋磁性，而x高于1.000时表现为铁磁性，磁性质对Cr插层量高度敏感。

Conclusion: 螺旋磁周期的出现及其随温度的变化可能与粉末颗粒和微加工晶体的样品尺寸效应有关，反映了尺寸对磁性有序行为的重要影响。

Abstract: We report a highly sensitive change in magnetic properties of a chiral Cr-intercalated transition-metal dichalcogenide Cr$_{x}$Ta$_{3}$S$_{6}$. Magnetization curves and small-angle neutron scattering data revealed that the powder samples exhibit chiral helimagnetism with a Cr intercalation quantity $x$ below 0.996, while they show ferromagnetism above 1.000. The emergence and temperature-dependent evolution of the helimagnetic period are argued in terms of sample dimensions of powders and microfabricated crystals.

</details>


<div id='cond-mat.mes-hall'></div>
