### [175] [Nonlocality-induced critical-length hierarchy from non-Hermitian competition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.02746)
*Mengjie Yang,Alexander N. Poddubny,Ching Hua Lee*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 该论文研究了非厄米晶格（non-Hermitian lattices）中由长程跳跃（long-range hoppings）引起的谱相变临界行为的重组，发现了与传统短程系统对数标度律不同的层次化标度律体系，并提出了两个新的非局域机制来解释这一现象。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在短程耦合双链系统中，非互易趋肤累积与分量间杂化的竞争通常导致对数临界长度律（logarithmic critical-length law）$N_c\sim\ln D$。本文旨在探究长程跳跃如何从根本上重新组织这一临界行为，揭示其是否产生新的标度律结构。

Method: 研究通过构建双耦合链模型，系统性地引入两类长程相互作用：仅杂化耦合项按幂律衰减（指数为$\alpha$），以及链内跳跃也按幂律衰减的情形。通过解析推导临界阈值方程，分析不同$\alpha$范围内的标度行为，并识别驱动新临界层级的非局域机制。

Result: 当仅杂化耦合为幂律衰减时，临界长度呈现代数标度$N_c\sim D^{\alpha/3}$；当链内跳跃同样为幂律衰减且$\alpha<2$时，系统进入尺度协变（scale-covariant）区域，临界阈值方程仅依赖纵横比$N_c/D$；$\alpha=2$时出现边际对数修正，$\alpha>2$时出现代数修正。论文识别出两个新的非局域机制：来自长程链内跳跃的非解析带边色散（nonanalytic band-edge dispersion），以及由非互易性引起的宇称混合杂化（parity-mixing hybridization）。

Conclusion: 非局域性系统地消除了传统非厄米趋肤临界行为所依赖的物理长度尺度（趋肤深度），提供了一个平台无关的理论框架，可在可编程拓扑电路、光子晶格和数字量子模拟器中实验验证。

Abstract: Spectral transitions in non-Hermitian lattices often arise from the competition between non-reciprocal skin accumulation and inter-component hybridization. In short-range systems formed by two coupled chains, this competition conventionally leads to the logarithmic critical-length law $N_c\sim\ln D$, where $D$ is the transverse separation between the chains. Here we show that long-range hoppings fundamentally reorganizes this critical behavior, producing a hierarchy of distinct scaling laws. When only the hybridization couplings are power-law decaying with exponent $α$, the onset becomes algebraic, $N_c\sim D^{α/3}$. When the hoppings within each chain are themselves also power-law decaying, in addition to the hybridization couplings, the system enters a scale-covariant regime for $α<2$, in which the criticality threshold equation depends only on the system aspect ratio $N_c/D$. At $α=2$ and beyond, this regime is followed by a marginal logarithmically corrected and algebraically corrected regimes, respectively. We identify two new non-local mechanisms that enable this unconventional critical hierarchy: a nonanalytic band-edge dispersion from long-range intra-chain hoppings, and parity-mixing hybridization induced by non-reciprocity. Our results show that nonlocality systematically removes the physical length scales i.e. skin depth underlying conventional critical non-Hermitian skin behavior, offering a platform-independent framework testable in programmable topoelectrical circuits, photonic lattices and digital quantum simulators.

</details>


### [176] [Broken site symmetry of Fe adatoms on Bi$_2$Te$_3$](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.02770)
*Duy Nguyen,Hari Paudyal,Joseph R. Sink,Michael E. Flatte,Jay A. Gupta*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 本文通过扫描隧道显微镜（scanning tunneling microscopy, STM）与原子级理论计算相结合，研究了Fe原子在Bi₂Te₃(111)表面上的吸附行为。实验发现Fe原子在fcc和hcp空位处的形貌图呈现C₃ᵥ对称的三重对称对比，但微分电导（dI/dV）图显示对称性显著降低。密度泛函理论（DFT）计算表明该体系发生了静态Jahn–Teller畸变，将吸附对称性从C₃ᵥ降低至C₁ᵥ，畸变构型能量上更有利（低72.5 meV）。轨道投影态密度及紧束缚格林函数模拟的局域态密度图与实验dI/dV图一致，证实了对称性降低源于C₁ᵥ结构畸变。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解磁性过渡金属原子在拓扑绝缘体表面上的吸附行为及其电子结构，对于揭示对称性破缺的微观机制具有重要意义。

Method: 采用扫描隧道显微镜（STM）在4.5 K下对Fe/Bi₂Te₃(111)进行形貌图和dI/dV图联合测量；同时结合密度泛函理论（DFT）计算静态Jahn–Teller畸变、轨道投影态密度，以及基于紧束缚格林函数（tight-binding Green's function）的方法模拟局域态密度（local density of states, LDOS）图。

Result: 实验结果表明Fe吸附原子在fcc和hcp空位处的形貌图呈现三重对称（C₃ᵥ），但dI/dV图显示对称性降低至C₁ᵥ；DFT计算确认了静态Jahn–Teller畸变的发生，畸变构型能量更低（72.5 meV）。占据态主要由d_xz和d_yz轨道贡献，未占据态以d_z²、d_x²⁻y²和d_xy为主，模拟的LDOS图与实验dI/dV图定性吻合。

Conclusion: Fe/Bi₂Te₃(111)体系中Fe吸附原子的对称性降低源于C₁ᵥ的静态Jahn–Teller结构畸变，而非简单的吸附位点对称性所决定。

Abstract: We report a combined scanning tunneling microscopy and atomistic theoretical study of Fe adatoms on the Bi$_2$Te$_3$(111) surface. Topographic imaging at $4.5$~K shows Fe adatoms in fcc and hcp hollow sites exhibit a threefold-symmetric contrast, consistent with the $C_{3v}$ symmetry of the adsorption site. However, simultaneously acquired differential conductance ($dI/dV$) maps reveal a pronounced reduction in symmetry, evidenced by differential contrast observed at nearest-neighbor Te sites. Density functional theory calculations show that the Fe/Bi$_2$Te$_3$ system undergoes a static Jahn--Teller distortion, reducing the adsorption symmetry from $C_{3v}$ to $C_{1v}$, with the distorted configuration favored by $72.5$~meV. Orbital-projected density of states calculations show that the occupied states near the Fermi level are dominated by $d_{xz}$ and $d_{yz}$ orbitals, whereas the unoccupied states are primarily of $d_{z^2}$, $d_{x^2-y^2}$ and $d_{xy}$ character. The local density of states from these orbitals is in good qualitative agreement with experimental $dI/dV$ spectra. Furthermore, simulated local-density-of-states maps using a tight-binding Green's function approach are in good agreement with experimental $dI/dV$ maps, confirming that the reduced symmetry originates from the $C_{1v}$ structural distortion.

</details>


### [177] [Magneto-oscillations, nonlinearity, and nonreciprocity of Coulomb drag in quantum circuits](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.02812)
*Alex Levchenko,Mingyang Zheng,Dominique Laroche*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 本文研究由绝热收缩结构（量子点接触和短量子线通道）构成的相互作用耦合量子电路中的库仑拖曳效应（Coulomb drag），揭示了空间限制与磁场共同导致的拖曳电流随栅压和磁场的振荡响应，并给出了线性及非线性拖曳电导的解析表达式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解相互作用耦合量子电路中的库仑拖曳机制，特别是空间限制和磁场对拖曳响应的影响，是目前低维介观物理中的重要问题。

Method: 基于绝热收缩结构构建理论模型，推导线性拖曳电导的闭式表达式，并分析长热长度与线间耦合范围相比的实验极限以简化相互作用核；同时研究非线性区拖曳电流与跨导的关系，并讨论耗散诱导的非互易性模型。

Result: 拖曳电流呈现随栅压和磁场的丰富振荡响应：拖曳峰随磁电亚带的去布居而移动，在逆磁场下渐近周期，可见性由温度与场锐化的收缩隧穿宽度的竞争控制。

Conclusion: 扩展模型表明塞曼劈裂、相互作用对势垒透射的重整化、高场背散射及电路耦合的频率结构均各自在拖曳振荡的温度依赖性和线形上留下特征印记。

Abstract: We consider the problem of Coulomb drag in interactively coupled quantum circuits built of adiabatic constrictions: quantum point contacts and short quantum-wire channels. The interplay of spatial confinement and magnetic field leads to a rich oscillatory response of the drag current as a function of gate voltage and magnetic field: drag peaks track the depopulation of magnetoelectric subbands, are asymptotically periodic in inverse field, and their visibility is controlled by the competition of temperature with the field-sharpened tunneling width of the constriction. We derive a closed expression for the linear drag conductance whose interaction kernel simplifies dramatically in the experimentally relevant limit of a long thermal length compared with the range of the interwire coupling, investigate the drag in the nonlinear regime, where the drag current measures the transconductance of the drive channel at any field, and discuss physically motivated models of dissipation-induced nonreciprocity of the drag signal. Extensions accounting for Zeeman splitting, interaction renormalization of the barrier transmission, backscattering at high field, and the frequency structure of the circuit coupling delineate how each mechanism imprints itself on the temperature dependence and lineshapes of the drag oscillations.

</details>


### [178] [Lifshitz transitions and isospin polarization in twist-decoupled monolayer-bilayer graphene](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.02899)
*Alex Boschi,Leonardo Sabattini,Sergey Slizovskiy,Vaidotas Mišeikis,Zewdu M. Gebeyehu,Stiven Forti,Antonio Rossi,Kenji Watanabe,Takashi Taniguchi,Fabio Beltram,Vladimir I. Fal'ko,Camilla Coletti,Sergio Pezzini*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 本文采用扭转解耦的单层石墨烯（monolayer graphene, MLG）作为局域栅极，在无外加位移场（displacement field, D）的条件下对Bernal堆叠双层石墨烯（Bernal-stacked bilayer graphene, BLG）进行静电调控，从而在D=0处观测到三重简并的量子霍尔（quantum Hall）态，并通过掺杂、磁场和电场驱动多重相变，揭示了价带边缘附近的自发对称破缺现象。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Bernal堆叠双层石墨烯的价带鞍点（saddle point）附近存在与低能Lifshitz转变（Lifshitz transition）相关的关联电子相，但进入该物理区域需要同时具备极低电荷无序和对垂直电场的精确控制，这是一项实验上的严峻挑战。

Method: 作者使用一块扭转解耦的单层石墨烯（MLG）作为近端调控层，在不施加外部位移场（D=0）的条件下对邻近的BLG进行静电偏置，从而避免外部电场引入的额外扰动，并通过磁输运测量系统研究霍尔态与量子振荡随掺杂、磁场和电场的变化。

Result: 在D=0处观测到三重简并的量子霍尔态，并发现由掺杂、磁场和电场驱动的多重相变；价带边缘附近出现自发对称破缺，其标志是异常频率的量子振荡和显著增大的准粒子有效质量（quasiparticle mass）。

Conclusion: 结果表明，在原子级贴近的单层石墨烯存在的情况下，BLG中的电子相互作用得以保持，同时展示了CVD生长石墨烯多层结构在探索关联物质相方面的潜力。

Abstract: Bernal-stacked bilayer graphene (BLG) hosts correlated electronic phases tied to low-energy Lifshitz transitions at saddle points in its valence band. To access this regime, ultralow charge disorder and control over a vertical electric field are simultaneously required. Here, we employ a twist-decoupled monolayer (MLG) to bias a proximal BLG in the absence of an external displacement field (D). We thereby reveal three-fold degenerate quantum Hall states at D = 0, with multiple transitions driven by doping, magnetic and electric field. Spontaneous broken symmetry in the vicinity of the valence band edge is signaled by the emergence of quantum oscillations with anomalous frequencies and large quasiparticle mass. These results indicate that electronic interactions in BLG are preserved in presence of an atomically close MLG, while showcasing the potential of CVD-grown graphene multilayers for the exploration of correlated phases of matter.

</details>


### [179] [Chaos and Diffusion in Twisted Bilayer Graphene](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.02916)
*Olga Arroyo-Gascón,Manuel Pino*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 本文通过数值分析研究了实验相关的扭转双层石墨烯（twisted bilayer graphene）模型中的混沌与扩散行为，发现有限系统中无论旋转角是否公度（commensurate）均表现出混沌，边界诱导散射是导致扩散和混沌的关键单粒子散射机制，且该效应远强于单层石墨烯。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 扭转双层石墨烯是近年来凝聚态物理研究的热点，但其混沌和扩散行为的数值研究尚不充分。作者旨在通过数值分析揭示实验中相关模型在单粒子层面的散射机制，特别是边界效应在其中的作用。

Method: 作者对公度（commensurate）与非公度（incommensurate）旋转角的有限尺寸扭转双层石墨烯模型进行数值模拟，分析系统的混沌特性；在中间扭转角下计算系统的Thouless能量（Thouless energy），以理解与谱的混沌性质相关的扩散过程。

Result: 数值结果表明，有限系统在公度与非公度旋转角下均表现出混沌行为。无相互作用电子的平均自由程（mean free path）随系统尺寸成比例缩放，这与边界诱导散射（border-induced scattering）引起的扩散过程一致。

Conclusion: 边界在扭转双层石墨烯中扮演了实验相关的单粒子散射机制的角色，其效应远强于单层石墨烯；无论旋转角如何、晶格是否公度，边界效应均能诱导混沌与扩散行为。

Abstract: We numerically analyze chaos and diffusion in experimentally-relevant models of twisted bilayer graphene. Our results indicate that finite systems at both commensurate and incommensurate rotation angles exhibit chaos. We compute the Thouless energy of the system at intermediate twist angles to further understand the diffusive processes associated with the chaotic nature of the spectrum. We find that the mean free path of the non-interacting electrons scales with the system size, which is consistent with diffusive processes caused by border-induced scattering. In summary, our results show the role of borders as a experimentally relevant single-particle scattering mechanism, which is much stronger than in single-layer graphene and induces chaos and diffusion regardless of the rotation angle and the lattice commensurability.

</details>


### [180] [Hidden Quantum Geometry in Bilayer Exciton Condensates](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.03049)
*Xuzhe Ying,Benjamin T. Zhou*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 本文揭示了双层激子凝聚（exciton condensate, EC）体系中一个基本性质：即使非相互作用能带是平凡的（trivial），激子序也能在关联的电子-空穴能带中产生非平凡的隐藏量子几何效应。这种由EC驱动的量子几何可通过施加面内交流电场时产生的特征性垂直极化响应来探测，其中二阶响应与EC序参量呈反平方标度关系。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当电子掺杂层与空穴掺杂层以近似相等的载流子密度堆叠时，层间库仑相互作用使双层体系转变为激子凝聚态。作者希望揭示这种双层EC体系中由激子序引发的基本量子几何性质，即使非相互作用能带是平凡的，激子序仍可产生隐藏的量子几何效应。

Method: 作者通过理论分析，研究双层EC体系的量子几何特性，考察在面内交流电场作用下体系的极化响应。他们特别计算了二阶非线性响应，并分析了其与双层EC序参量之间的标度关系。

Result: 研究发现，激子序在原本平凡的电子-空穴能带中诱发了非平凡的隐藏量子几何效应，该效应通过特征性的面外极化响应表现出来。二阶响应表现出与双层EC序参量的特征性反平方标度规律。

Conclusion: 该发现揭示了由电子-空穴关联驱动的隐藏贝里相位（Berry phase）效应，并将双层EC确立为研究丰富非线性物理的有前景的平台。

Abstract: When an electron-doped layer is stacked with a hole-doped layer with approximately equal carrier density, inter-layer Coulomb interaction turns the bilayer system into an exciton condensate (EC). In this Letter, we reveal a fundamental property of bilayer ECs: the excitonic order gives rise to nontrivial hidden quantum geometric effects in the correlated electron-hole bands, even when the non-interacting bands are trivial. Such peculiar EC-driven quantum geometry manifests itself in a characteristic out-of-plane polarization response upon applying an in-plane AC electric field to the bilayer EC system. In particular, the second-order response exhibits a characteristic inverse square scaling with the bilayer EC order parameter. Our finding reveals a fundamental hidden Berry phase effect driven by electron-hole correlations, and establishes bilayer EC as a promising platform for rich nonlinear physics.

</details>


### [181] [Quaternion-Kahler geometry of time reversal symmetric crystals](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.03178)
*Hyeongmuk Lim,Junseo Jung,Yuting Qian,Bohm-Jung Yang*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 本文提出Kramers对能带的几何结构本质上是四元数（quaternionic）的，并据此构建了四元数量子几何张量（quaternionic quantum geometric tensor），该张量将量子度规与三个SU(2)贝里曲率分量统一起来，其非负性约束导出了非阿贝尔理想Chern能带的对偶，并在四维情形下与四维量子霍尔效应相联系。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量子几何（quantum geometry）的标准表述仅适用于孤立复能带，其贝里曲率（Berry curvature）为阿贝尔型。然而，具有自旋的时间反演对称晶体因Kramers简并使贝里曲率变为非阿贝尔SU(2)场，需要新的几何语言来描述——这构成了本文的核心研究动机。

Method: 本文将Kramers对能带（Kramers pair bands）的几何刻画为四元数几何：最小Kramers对定义了一个到四元数射影空间（quaternion projective space）的映射，由此构造一个四元数量子几何张量，它同时包含量子度规（quantum metric）和三个SU(2)贝里曲率分量。该张量的非负性给出了局域的度规-曲率不等式，其饱和条件对应于非阿贝尔版本的理想Chern能带。

Result: 研究发现四元数量子几何张量的非负性约束导出局域度规-曲率不等式，该不等式的饱和定义出非阿贝尔对应物，即理想Chern能带的推广。在四维空间中，该理想极限进一步产生了与四维量子霍尔效应（four-dimensional quantum Hall effect）相关的代数结构。

Conclusion: 本工作将理想量子几何从Chern能带的阿贝尔几何推广到时间反演对称量子物质的四元数、非阿贝尔几何，为理解该类系统中关联量子现象提供了新的几何框架。

Abstract: Quantum geometry reveals how the shape of Bloch wave functions governs correlated quantum phenomena. Its standard formulation describes isolated complex bands, where Berry curvature is Abelian and ideal geometry is Kahler. However, time reversal symmetric crystals with spin require a different language since Kramers degeneracy pairs Bloch states and turns Berry curvature into a non-Abelian SU(2) field. Here we show that Kramers pair band geometry is quaternionic. A minimal Kramers pair defines a map into quaternion projective space, and its quaternionic quantum geometric tensor unifies the quantum metric with the three SU(2) Berry curvature components. The non-negativity of this tensor imposes local metric-curvature inequalities, whose saturation defines the non-Abelian counterpart of ideal Chern bands. In four dimensions, the ideal limit further yields an algebraic structure related to the four-dimensional quantum Hall effect. Our results promote ideal quantum geometry from the Abelian geometry of Chern bands to the quaternionic, non-Abelian geometry of time reversal symmetric quantum matter.

</details>


### [182] [Helicity in monoaxial chiral magnets](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.03389)
*Victor Laliena,Diego Gironés-Magaña,Javier Campo*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 本文研究了单轴手性磁体（monoaxial chiral magnet）与非磁性介质接触时新出现的亚稳态螺旋态（metastable helical states）的拓扑起源及静态和动态性质。作者提出了一种称为"动力学拓扑保护"（dynamical topological protection）的新机制，解释了这些态在弱磁场和极化电流下保持绕数（winding number）不变的原因，并展示了通过足够强的磁场和电流可在不同螺旋态之间切换的可能性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统上，单轴手性磁体在非磁性介质中的平衡态是单一波矢的螺旋织构，系统中从未观察到亚稳态。然而近期研究发现，当手性磁体与两个单轴铁磁体（uniaxial ferromagnets）紧密接触时，会出现大量亚稳态螺旋态。理解这些态的稳定机制及其物理性质，对于基础物理研究和自旋电子学（spintronics）应用都具有重要意义。

Method: 通过理论分析阐明亚稳态螺旋态稳定化的拓扑起源，提出"动力学拓扑保护"机制；在此基础上系统研究这些态的静态性质（如绕数在弱磁场和极化电流下的保守行为）和动态响应（对足够强磁场和电流的演化行为，包括绕数密度非均匀分布及带间切换机制）。

Result: 研究表明，由于动力学拓扑保护的作用，在足够弱的磁场和极化电流条件下，亚稳态螺旋态的绕数保持不变。当施加极化电流时，亚稳态螺旋态达到静态构型，虽然绕数保持不变，但绕数密度呈非均匀分布并集中在一个铁磁界面附近。足够强的磁场和电流可提供不同螺旋态之间的切换机制。

Conclusion: 由于磁性质依赖于螺旋度（helicity），这些亚稳态螺旋态在自旋电子学和磁子学（magnonics）领域具有广阔的应用前景，为实现可调控的磁性器件提供了新的物理基础。

Abstract: The equilibrium state of a monoaxial chiral magnet surrounded by a non-magnetic medium (such as air or vacuum) is a helical texture characterized by a single, well-defined wave vector. No metastable states have ever been observed in such systems. Recently, however, it was demonstrated that when a chiral magnet is in close contact with two uniaxial ferromagnets, a large number of metastable helical states emerge in addition to the equilibrium state [Phys. Rev. B 109, 214424]. These helical states are distinguished by their wave number (helicity). In the present work, we elucidate the topological origin of the stabilization of these states --a mechanism we term dynamical topological protection-- and investigate their static and dynamic properties. We find that, as a consequence of this dynamical topological protection, the winding number of the helical states remains constant under the application of sufficiently weak magnetic fields and polarized electric currents. Furthermore, when a polarized current is applied to a metastable helical state, a static configuration is reached. This state retains the original winding number, but its winding number density becomes non-homogeneously distributed, concentrating near the interface with one of the ferromagnets. The dynamic response to sufficiently large magnetic fields and currents provides mechanisms to switch between different helical states. Since the magnetic properties depend on helicity, these metastable helical states are highly promising for applications in spintronics and magnonics.

</details>


### [183] [Vector-field control and emergent basal-plane anisotropy of magnetic textures in noncentrosymmetric (Fe$_{0.63}$Ni$_{0.3}$Pd$_{0.07}$)$_3$P](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.03513)
*Victor Ukleev,Oleg I. Utesov,Lorenzo Ubilla,Chen Luo,Radu-Marius Abrudan,Peter Wild,Holger Kropf,Moritz Winter,Sebastian Schneider,Alexander Tahn,Bernd Rellinghaus,Tim A. Butcher,Simone Finizio,Sebastian Wintz,Markus Weigand,Max T. Birch,Yoshinori Tokura,Yasujiro Taguchi,Kosuke Karube,Florin Radu*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 该论文通过结合共振小角X射线散射（resonant small-angle x-ray scattering）与叠层衍射成像（ptychography）技术，对具有$S_4$对称性的室温磁体(Fe$_{0.63}$Ni$_{0.3}$Pd$_{0.07}$)$_3$P在矢量磁场中进行了宽温度范围的系统性研究，证明该材料可作为矢量场操控手性自旋织构的模型体系。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: (Fe$_{0.63}$Ni$_{0.3}$Pd$_{0.07}$)$_3$P作为一种具有$S_4$对称性的室温磁体，蕴含着丰富的拓扑自旋织构（topological spin textures），但其在不同温度和磁场下的行为机制尚未被系统阐明，缺乏对矢量场操控这些自旋织构能力的实验验证。

Method: 研究采用共振小角X射线散射与叠层衍射成像两种互补技术，在矢量磁场（vector magnetic field）条件下对样品进行宽温度范围的联合表征，系统地观测磁性条状畴（magnetic stripe domains）的取向与构型变化。

Result: 实验证明面内磁场可通过从手性条状畴（chiral stripe）到非手性扇形构型（achiral fan configuration）的转变连续旋转条状畴取向；50 K及以下时条状畴取向被亚稳锁定。室温下波矢大小在基面内近乎各向同性，而冷却过程中出现显著的各向异性相互作用演化，其中20-50 K区间形成了反映磁晶各向异性、各向异性交换和Dzyaloshinskii-Moriya相互作用（DMI）共同作用的非平凡各向异性轴，且该DMI有效取向随温度旋转。

Conclusion: 该工作确立了(Fe$_{0.63}$Ni$_{0.3}$Pd$_{0.07}$)$_3$P作为矢量场操控手性自旋织构的模型体系的地位，并揭示了非中心对称磁体中有效DMI景观随温度演化的此前未被认识的新机制。

Abstract: (Fe$_{0.63}$Ni$_{0.3}$Pd$_{0.07}$)$_3$P is a room-temperature magnet with $S_4$ symmetry that hosts a rich variety of topological spin textures. Here, we report a combined resonant small-angle x-ray scattering and ptychography study of (Fe$_{0.63}$Ni$_{0.3}$Pd$_{0.07}$)$_3$P in vector magnetic fields over a broad temperature range. We demonstrate deterministic vector-field control of magnetic stripe domains, where in-plane fields continuously rotate their orientation via a transition from a chiral stripe to an achiral fan configuration. Furthermore, at 50 K and below, the stripe orientation becomes metastably pinned and retains its field-trained direction. While the magnitude of the wavevector is nearly isotropic within the basal plane at room temperature, a pronounced temperature evolution of anisotropic interactions emerges upon cooling. In particular, non-trivial anisotropy axes develop at 20-50 K reflecting the combined effects of magnetocrystalline anisotropy, anisotropic exchange, and Dzyaloshinskii-Moriya interaction (DMI), whose effective orientation is found to rotate with temperature. These results establish (Fe$_{0.63}$Ni$_{0.3}$Pd$_{0.07}$)$_3$P as a model system for vector-field control of chiral spin textures and reveal a previously unrecognized temperature-driven evolution of the effective DMI landscape in a noncentrosymmetric magnet.

</details>


### [184] [Enhancement of far-field thermal emission via polaritonic cavity modes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.03621)
*Maelie Coral,Jose Ordonez-Miranda,Georges Hamaoui,Roman Anufriev,Laurent Jalabert,Masahiro Nomura,Yannick De Wilde,Sebastian Volz*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 本文通过实验与模拟证明了由热激发导模（thermally excited guided modes, TEGMs）引发的可调远场热辐射通道，SiO2覆盖的硅腔在极化激元共振附近可将发射率提升高达200%，提供了一种无需复杂纳米加工即可调控热辐射的简单腔体平台。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 热辐射控制在热光伏、热传感、成像和隐身等应用中至关重要，但此前研究仅关注腔体内热激发导模的发射，对其向腔外远场辐射的贡献尚未探索。作者旨在填补这一空白，并寻找一种简单、可扩展的远场热辐射调控方案。

Method: 作者将表面声子极化激元（surface phonon-polaritons）与腔共振耦合以产生热激发导模，并结合红外发射率实验与涨落电动力学（fluctuational electrodynamics）模拟，对比了二氧化硅覆盖硅腔与裸硅腔的热发射特性。

Result: 实验与模拟结果识别出区分二维受限极化激元模式向三维辐射通道转换的特征谱线。SiO2覆盖的硅腔在极化激元谱共振附近可将发射率提升高达200%，而裸硅腔仅产生宽带增强效应。

Conclusion: 这些发现为热激发导模提供了实验证据，并确立了一种简单、可扩展的腔体架构，无需复杂纳米加工即可有效调控热辐射，为热辐射工程提供了一个实用平台。

Abstract: Controlling thermal emission is crucial for applications involving thermophotovoltaics, thermal sensing, imaging, and camouflage. While prior studies focused on the emission of thermally excited guided modes (TEGMs) inside cavities, their contribution to the far-field radiation outside cavities has remained unexplored. Here, we demonstrate a tunable far-field thermal channel enabled by TEGMs arising from the coupling of surface phonon-polaritons and cavity resonances. By combining infrared emissivity experiments with fluctuational electrodynamics simulations, we identify distinct spectral features marking the conversion of two-dimensionally confined polaritonic modes into three-dimensional radiative channels. We find that silicon cavities covered with SiO2 enhance the emissivity by up to 200% near the polaritonic spectral resonance, whereas bare silicon cavities yield only broadband enhancement. These findings provide experimental evidence of TEGMs and establish a simple cavity architecture as an effective and scalable platform for tailoring thermal radiation without complex nanofabrication.

</details>


### [185] [Machine Learning Bandgap Prediction of Nanoporous Graphenes with Water](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.03680)
*Sneha Mittal,Alan E. Anaya Morales,Victor Rosendal,Mads Brandbyge*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 本文结合密度泛函理论（density functional theory, DFT）、从头算分子动力学（ab initio molecular dynamics, AIMD）和可解释机器学习（interpretable machine learning），揭示了水分子如何调控纳米多孔石墨烯（nanoporous graphene, NPG）及其氮掺杂杂化体系（h-NPG）的量子输运性质，发现带隙调制幅度超过两倍，并识别出控制该效应的关键物理因素。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 纳米多孔石墨烯作为碳基纳电子器件的新兴候选材料，其与环境中水分子相互作用的微观机制尚不清楚，而理解水对其量子输运性质的调控是推动实际应用的关键前提。

Method: 研究综合运用密度泛函理论（DFT）和从头算分子动力学（AIMD）进行电子结构和动力学模拟，并开发了基于平滑原子位置重叠（Smooth Overlap of Atomic Positions, SOAP）描述符的黑箱（black-box）与物理知情的灰箱（physics-informed grey-box）机器学习模型，其中高斯过程回归（Gaussian process regression）模型在接近DFT精度的同时支持物理解释。

Result: 在不同局部水合结构下，NPG和氮掺杂杂化体系（h-NPG）的带隙变化范围超过两倍；机器学习分析识别出水偶极取向、水-基底距离、水的几何中心以及能带分辨偶极矩为调控带隙的主导因素。

Conclusion: 水合结构通过偶极取向和空间分布等微观特征显著调控纳米多孔石墨烯体系的电子带隙，可解释的机器学习模型为理解水-纳米结构界面输运机制提供了有效途径。

Abstract: The structure and dynamical behavior of water confined at or within nanostructures is a topic central to many fields, from biology to emerging electronics such as carbon nanostructures. Nanoporous graphene (NPG) containing periodic nanoscale pores with specific topologies has emerged as a promising material in carbon-based nanoelectronics; however, its interaction with ambient water remains poorly understood. Here, we combine density functional theory (DFT), ab initio molecular dynamics (AIMD), and interpretable machine learning (ML) to reveal how water controls quantum transport in NPGs. Depending on the local hydration structure, the bandgap varies by more than a factor of two across NPG and nitrogen-doped hybrid (h-NPG) systems. To uncover the underlying mechanism, we develop Smooth Overlap of Atomic Positions (SOAP)-based black-box and physics-informed grey-box ML models. The Gaussian process regression model achieves near-DFT accuracy while enabling physical interpretation. Analysis identifies water dipole orientation, water-substrate distance, water center-of-geometry, and ribbon-resolved dipole moments as the dominant factors controlling bandgap modulation across NPG and h-NPG systems.

</details>


### [186] [Controllable interaction between photons and distant spins via vacuum Rabi oscillations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.03809)
*Xiao Xue,Jurgen Dijkema,Tobias Bonsen,Patrick Harvey-Collard,Maximilian Rimbach-Russ,Sander L. de Snoo,Guoji Zheng,Amir Sammak,Giordano Scappucci,Lieven M. K. Vandersypen*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 本文通过在门定义量子点（gate-defined quantum dots）中超导腔（superconducting cavity）与两个空间分离的双量子点自旋量子比特（spin qubits）进行耦合，观测到了多次真空拉比振荡（vacuum Rabi oscillations），实现了单光子介导的自旋间能量转移，并观察到Fock态导致的加速拉比频率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在门定义量子点中观测到强自旋-光子耦合后，在时域中探测这种相互作用一直是主要目标，这为在单能量量子水平上利用光-物质相互作用提供了可能。

Method: 精心设计了一个包含两个空间分离的双量子点（每者容纳单个电子自旋量子比特）以及一个用于容纳微波光子的超导腔组成的器件，通过工程化调控实现自旋与原子的耦合，并串联两次真空拉比振荡以实现自旋间的光子介导能量转移。

Result: 观测到每个自旋量子比特与腔之间的多次真空拉比振荡；通过串联涉及两个自旋的振荡，实现了能量激发以光子形式从一个量子比特发射并转移至另一个量子比特；单光子发射后腔被制备为Fock态，导致真空拉比频率加速。

Conclusion: 这些结果为探索光-物质相互作用以及将半导体自旋量子比特与光子链路（photonic links）接口对接提供了基础构建模块。

Abstract: Vacuum Rabi oscillations between a single photon and a single spin demonstrate the capability of harnessing light-matter interaction at the level of a single quantum of energy. Since the observation of strong spin-photon coupling in gate-defined quantum dots, probing this interaction in the time-domain has been a major objective. Here, we carefully engineer a device composed of two spatially separated double quantum dots hosting single electron spin qubits and a superconducting cavity to accommodate microwave photons. We observe multiple vacuum Rabi oscillations between each spin qubit and the cavity. By concatenating vacuum Rabi oscillations involving the two spins, an energy excitation in one qubit can be emitted as a photon and then transferred to the other qubit. When a single photon is emitted, the cavity is prepared in a Fock state, leading to an accelerated vacuum Rabi frequency. These results serve as building blocks not only in exploring light-matter interactions, but also in interfacing semiconductor spin qubits to photonic links.

</details>


### [187] [Wavelength-Selective control of Atomic Scale Au Contacts](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.03831)
*Werner Brämer-Escamilla,Floralba Lopez,Laila Procel,David Llerena,Carlos Sabater,Ernesto Medina*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 本研究在机械可控的金（Au）断裂结中展示了波长选择性的原子运动控制：约530nm波长的光激发驱动间隙闭合和金属桥形成，而约407nm波长的光激发驱动颈部变薄、桥断裂及间隙开启。与依赖热膨胀的传统光学开关不同，该系统在不同波长下产生方向相反的原子漂移，并通过时间分辨电导迹线区分了隧穿区和金属区的两种动力学机制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统金属接触中的光学开关主要依靠热膨胀（thermal expansion）起作用，缺乏对不同波长光响应的选择性控制能力，因此需要在金属纳米接触中探索一种独立于热效应、可通过光波长定向调控原子运动的新机制。

Method: 使用机械可控断裂结（mechanically controllable break junction）装置，在不同波长（约530nm和约407nm）下激发金电极，并利用时间分辨电导迹线（time-resolved conductance traces）监测间隙闭合与开启过程。在隧穿区，通过指数电导瞬态测量间隙坐标的漂移速度；在金属区，利用Sharvin关系将线性√G/G0瞬态转化为颈部生长与变薄的径向速度。

Result: 在约530nm激发下观测到间隙闭合与金属桥形成，而在约407nm激发下观测到颈部变薄、桥断裂与间隙开启。隧穿区的指数电导瞬态给出了间隙闭合和开启对应的漂移速度；金属区的Sharvin关系分析得出颈部生长与变薄速度具有可比量级。该结果表明不同波长能驱动方向相反的原子漂移而非单纯的热效应。

Conclusion: 该研究确立了光选择原子漂移（optically selected atomic drift）作为金属纳米接触可逆控制的新机制，并提供了一种定量追踪等离激元辅助原子重排（plasmon-assisted atomic rearrangements）的实时方法。

Abstract: We demonstrate wavelength-selective control of atomic motion in a mechanically controllable Au break junction. Excitation at $λ_{\mathrm{form}}\simeq 530~{\rm nm}$ drives gap closure and metallic bridge formation, whereas excitation at $λ_{\mathrm{rup}}\simeq 407~{\rm nm}$ drives neck thinning, bridge rupture, and subsequent gap opening. Unlike conventional optical switching in metallic contacts, where illumination primarily acts via thermal expansion, the present experiment reveals oppositely directed atomic drift at different wavelengths. Time-resolved conductance traces allow us to distinguish two dynamical regimes. In the tunneling regime, exponential conductance transients measure the drift velocity of the gap coordinate for both gap closure and gap opening. In the metallic regime, the Sharvin relation converts linear $\sqrt{G/G_0}$ transients into radial neck-growth and neck-thinning velocities of comparable magnitude. These results establish optically selected atomic drift as a mechanism for reversible control of metallic nanocontacts and provide a quantitative route to follow plasmon-assisted atomic rearrangements in real time.

</details>


### [188] [Magnetic-field control of Fermi polaron fine structure and polarization in strained monolayer semiconductors](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.03853)
*Zakhar A. Iakovlev*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 本文发展了一套理论，研究在外加磁场和单轴应变作用下，掺杂二维半导体中吸引型费米极化子（attractive Fermi polaron）的能谱精细结构和偏振特性。通过格林函数方法计算了能量能级和斯托克斯参数，发现应变与磁场可协同调控能级劈裂和本征态偏振，并识别出居民载流子完全谷极化（valley polarization）的临界磁场。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在二维半导体中，多体激子准粒子（many-body excitonic quasiparticles）的光学响应受到实磁场与应变诱导的赝磁场（pseudomagnetic field）的共同影响。理解费米极化子能谱精细结构及其偏振性质的竞争机制，对于通过外场调控二维半导体的光学响应具有重要意义。

Method: 基于格林函数方法（Green's function approach）计算吸引型费米极化子态的能级与斯托克斯参数（Stokes parameters）；引入简化的有效二能级模型，解析研究塞曼劈裂（Zeeman splitting）与赝塞曼劈裂（pseudo-Zeeman splitting）的相互作用；同时计算吸收谱和反射谱，涵盖圆二色性（circular dichroism）和线二色性（linear dichroism）。

Result: 线偏振的应变诱导劈裂双峰在低磁场下表现出二次塞曼位移，随磁场增大转变为具有椭圆偏振的线性塞曼劈裂。识别出一个临界磁场，超过该磁场后居民载流子完全谷极化，仅保留一个圆偏振费米极化子态。应变、磁场和电子密度可实现能级劈裂与本征态偏振的连续调谐。

Conclusion: 实磁场和应变诱导的赝磁场为控制二维半导体中多体激子准粒子的光学响应提供了互补工具。费米极化子g因子（g factor）的重整化源于与谷极化费米海（valley-polarized Fermi sea）中居民载流子的关联效应，是理解该体系中自旋与谷自由度操控的关键。

Abstract: The theory of attractive Fermi polaron energy spectrum fine structure and polarization in doped two-dimensional semiconductors in external magnetic field is developed. Fermi polaron $g$ factor renormalization due to correlations with the valley-polarized Fermi sea of resident charge carriers is calculated. We study the competition between Zeeman and strain-induced splittings in monolayers under uniaxial strain. The control of strain, magnetic field and electron density allows continuous tuning of energy splitting and eigenstate polarization. Linearly polarized strain-induced split doublet exhibits quadratic Zeeman shift changing to linear Zeeman splitting with elliptical polarization with the increase of magnetic field. We identify a critical magnetic field above which resident charge carriers become fully valley polarized and only one circularly polarized Fermi polaron state remains. Within the Green's function approach we calculate energy levels and Stokes parameters of attractive Fermi polaron states and introduce a simplified effective two-level model allowing us to study analytically the interplay of Zeeman and pseudo-Zeeman splitting. We calculate absorption and reflection spectra, including circular and linear dichroism in the trion spectral range. These results show that real and strain-induced pseudomagnetic fields provide complementary tools for controlling the optical response of many-body excitonic quasiparticles in two-dimensional semiconductors.

</details>


### [189] [Surface-Selective Probe of Spin-Triplet Superconductivity in Rhombohedral Graphene](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.03870)
*Kilian Krötzsch,Zekang Zhou,Kryštof Kolář,Yonggen Li,Kenji Watanabe,Takashi Taniguchi,Moty Heiblum,Cyprian Lewandowski,Mitali Banerjee*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 本研究利用单侧WS₂邻近效应作为表面选择性自旋-轨道探针，研究菱面体五层石墨烯中的自旋三重态超导性。通过位移场调控载流子与自旋-轨道微扰的重叠，观察到三个超导口袋（SC1、SC2、SC3）的非对称行为，其中稳健超导态在高态密度载流子向WS₂界面极化时被抑制，从而为自旋三重态配对提供了新证据。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 菱面体石墨烯（rhombohedral graphene）中已在多种层数下观察到超导性，且有越来越多的证据指向自旋三重态（spin-triplet）配对，但缺乏对超导自旋结构的互补性探测手段。

Method: 在菱面体五层石墨烯（R5G）中使用单侧WS₂邻近效应作为表面选择性自旋-轨道耦合（spin-orbit coupling）探针；通过位移场（displacement field）调节超导载流子与自旋-轨道微扰的重叠；结合门控追踪特征、量子振荡（quantum oscillations）和自洽能带结构计算来识别层极化和费米面性质。

Result: 观察到强不对称的超导景观：SC1和SC2两个稳健口袋仅存在于位移场符号相反的两侧，SC3则明显较弱。当高态密度（high-DOS）载流子向WS₂界面极化时，稳健超导态消失或被显著削弱。由于Ising自旋-轨道耦合与时间反演的自旋单态配对相容，但会通过倾斜或钉扎自旋织构而竞争自旋三重态配对，这种表面选择性抑制为包含空穴型和电子型载流子的自旋三重态超导提供了额外证据。

Conclusion: 单侧TMD邻近效应可作为一种位移场可调的超导自旋结构探针，应用于菱面体石墨烯系统，为验证自旋三重态超导配对机制提供了一种互补的实验手段。

Abstract: Superconductivity in rhombohedral graphene has been observed across many layer numbers, with mounting evidence pointing toward spin-triplet pairing, yet complementary probes of the superconducting spin structure remain needed. Here we use one-sided WS$_2$ proximity in rhombohedral pentalayer graphene (R5G) as a surface-selective spin-orbit probe. The induced Ising spin-orbit coupling is strongest for carriers localized near the WS$_2$ interface, allowing the displacement field to tune the overlap between superconducting carriers and the spin-orbit perturbation. We observe a strongly asymmetric superconducting landscape: two robust pockets, SC1 and SC2, survive only on mutually opposite signs of displacement field, while a third pocket, SC3, is substantially weaker. Gate-tracking features, quantum oscillations, and self-consistent band-structure calculations identify the layer polarization and Fermi-surface character of the relevant carriers. The robust superconducting states are absent or strongly weakened when the active high-DOS carriers are polarized toward the WS$_2$ interface. Since Ising spin-orbit coupling is compatible with time-reversed spin-singlet pairing but competes with same-spin intervalley triplet pairing by canting or pinning the parent spin texture, this surface-selective suppression provides additional evidence for spin-triplet superconductivity involving both hole-like and electron-like carriers. Our results establish one-sided TMD proximity as a displacement-field-tunable probe of superconducting spin structure in rhombohedral graphene.

</details>
